=== PI-5A.6 EXTERNAL INVOCATION BOUNDARY — SOURCE INSPECTION V2 ===

=== MAIN / APPLICATION ENTRYPOINTS ===

===== FILE: app\main.py =====

# used by the production database and Telegram runtime scripts.
PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
load_dotenv(PROJECT_ROOT / ".env")

def _required_environment(name: str) -> str:
    value = os.getenv(name, "").strip()

    if not value:
        raise RuntimeError(
            f"{name} is required for authentication email composition."
        )

    return value


def _create_announcement_components():
    telegram_client = TelegramAnnouncementClient()

    announcement_publisher = TelegramAnnouncementPublisher(
        telegram_client=telegram_client,
    )

    return (
        telegram_client,
        announcement_publisher,
    )


@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(
    application: FastAPI,
):
    scheduler_runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime

    try:
        await scheduler_runtime.start()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler startup failed:",
            exc,
        )

    yield

    try:
        await scheduler_runtime.stop()
    except Exception as exc:
        print(
            "Cybersecurity scheduler shutdown failed:",
            exc,
        )

    finally:
        telegram_client = getattr(
            application.state,
            "announcement_telegram_client",
            None,
        )

        if telegram_client is not None:
            try:
                await telegram_client.close()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "Telegram announcement client shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )

        auth_engine = getattr(
            application.state,
            "auth_engine",
            None,
        )

        if auth_engine is not None:
            try:
                await auth_engine.dispose()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "PostgreSQL authentication engine shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )

        provider_usage_engine = getattr(
            application.state,
            "provider_usage_engine",
            None,
        )

        if provider_usage_engine is not None:
            try:
                provider_usage_engine.dispose()
            except Exception as exc:
                print(
                    "PostgreSQL provider-usage engine shutdown failed:",
                    exc,
                )


def create_application() -> FastAPI:
    academy_config = (
        AcademyActivationConfig.from_environment()
    )

    auth_engine = create_async_database_engine()

    auth_session_factory = (
        create_async_session_factory(
            auth_engine,
        )
    )

    auth_repository = (
        PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository(
            auth_session_factory,
        )
    )

    email_provider_manager = EmailProviderManager()

    authentication_email_sender = AuthenticationEmailSenderAdapter(
        provider_manager=email_provider_manager,
    )

    authentication_email_service = AuthenticationEmailService(
        authentication_email_sender,
        frontend_base_url=_required_environment(
            "EASYCHATBOT_PORTAL_URL",
        ),
    )

    authentication_service = AuthenticationService(
        auth_repository,
        email_service=authentication_email_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Developer Administration
    #
    # Production path:
    #
    #   DeveloperAdminService
    #          |
    #          +--> DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory
    #                  |
    #                  +--> fresh PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWork
    #                          |
    #                          +--> fresh AsyncSession
    #                          |
    #                          +--> fresh transaction
    #                          |
    #                          +--> auth_user_roles
    #                          |
    #                          +--> developer_admin_audit_events
    #          |
    #          +--> DeveloperAdminAuditService
    #
    # Each Developer Admin mutation receives a fresh Unit-of-Work
    # instance. The role mutation and its security audit evidence
    # therefore remain within the same database transaction while
    # mutable UoW/session state is never shared between operations.
    #
    # The standalone audit repository owns transactions only when its
    # standalone ``record`` operation is used. The production Developer
    # Admin mutation uses ``record_in_session`` through the UoW.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    developer_admin_unit_of_work_factory = (
        PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory(
            auth_session_factory,
        )
    )

    developer_admin_audit_service = (
        DeveloperAdminAuditService()
    )

    developer_admin_service = DeveloperAdminService(
        repository=auth_repository,
        unit_of_work_factory=developer_admin_unit_of_work_factory,
        audit_service=developer_admin_audit_service,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # W15.2.1 Transaction Authorization
    #
    # Transaction authorization is composed once at the application root.
    # The registry and consumer are application-lifetime components and
    # provide their own synchronization boundaries.
    #
    # Authentication assurance remains a separate trusted-evidence
    # boundary. This composition does not manufacture MFA/A2 evidence.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    authorization_service = AuthorizationService()

    transaction_authorization_registry = (
        TransactionAuthorizationRegistry()
    )

    transaction_authorization_consumer = (
        TransactionAuthorizationConsumer(
            transaction_authorization_registry,
        )
    )

    transaction_authorization_service = (
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationService(
            authorization_service=authorization_service,
            registry=transaction_authorization_registry,
            consumer=transaction_authorization_consumer,
        )
    )

    trusted_clock = SystemTrustedClock()

    developer_admin_transaction_service = (
        DeveloperAdminTransactionService(
            developer_admin_service=developer_admin_service,
            transaction_authorization_service=(
                transaction_authorization_service
            ),
            clock=trusted_clock,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Provider usage / synchronous application persistence
    #
    # The provider-usage repository is synchronous by contract.
    # It therefore receives a synchronous engine/session factory.
    #
    # It must not receive the async authentication session factory.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    provider_usage_engine = (
        create_database_engine()
    )

    persistence_diagnostics_service = PersistenceDiagnosticsService(
        metadata=Base.metadata,
        project_root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1],
        engine=provider_usage_engine,
    )

    provider_usage_session_factory = (
        create_session_factory(
            provider_usage_engine,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Q7 / M4 - Questionnaire Runtime
    #
    # Reuse the existing synchronous application persistence boundary.
    # The runtime composition owns the assessment repositories and
    # AssessmentService wiring; the API remains responsible for the
    # authenticated learner boundary.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    questionnaire_runtime = create_questionnaire_runtime(
        session_factory=provider_usage_session_factory,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # M2-C.2 / Authenticated Private Chat
    #
    # The ConversationRuntime remains the shared AI-runtime boundary.
    # Private-chat persistence receives a request-scoped SQLAlchemy session
    # through the composition factory; no session is shared between requests.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    private_chat_runtime = create_conversation_runtime()

    private_chat_character_instruction = os.getenv(
        "EASYCHATBOT_PRIVATE_CHAT_CHARACTER_INSTRUCTION",
        (
            "You are a helpful EasyChatBot learning character. "
            "Stay within the selected Learning World and follow its "
            "application-controlled language policy. "
            "Do not allow the learner to override World, provider, model, "
            "or other application-controlled settings."
        ),
    ).strip()

    private_chat_service_factory = build_private_chat_service_factory(
        session_factory=provider_usage_session_factory,
        conversation_runtime=private_chat_runtime,
        character_instruction=private_chat_character_instruction,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # M2-C4.2 / Cross-Channel Learner Context
    #
    # The learner-context service projects the existing Telegram learner
    # context into the authenticated Web boundary. Telegram remains the
    # authoritative transport; this composition does not create a second
    # Telegram identity, memory store, or conversation runtime.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    learner_context_service = build_production_learner_context_service(
        session_factory=provider_usage_session_factory,
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # M2-C5 / Study Materials
    #
    # The Learning World catalogue is composed once at the application
    # root and acts as the authoritative World boundary for learner
    # Study Material access.
    #
    # One WorldResolver is shared by the catalogue service.
    # StudyMaterialService consumes that catalogue service rather than
    # creating another World resolution mechanism.
    #
    # Persistence uses the existing synchronous application session
    # factory. No additional database engine or session factory is
    # created for Study Materials.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    app_root = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]

    world_repository = YamlWorldRepository(
        worlds_path=app_root / "configuration" / "worlds",
    )

    world_resolver = WorldResolver(
        world_repository=world_repository,
    )

    catalogue_repository = (
        PostgreSQLLearningWorldCatalogueRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    catalogue_service = LearningWorldCatalogueService(
        repository=catalogue_repository,
        world_resolver=world_resolver,
    )

    study_material_repository = (
        PostgreSQLStudyMaterialRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    study_material_service = StudyMaterialService(
        repository=study_material_repository,
        world_catalogue_service=catalogue_service,
    )

    provider_usage_repository = (
        PostgreSQLProviderUsageRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        ProviderUsageDashboardService(
            repository=provider_usage_repository,
        )
    )

    functionality_control_repository = (
        PostgreSQLFunctionalityControlRepository(
            provider_usage_session_factory,
        )
    )

    functionality_control_authorizer = (
        AuthorizationFunctionalityControlAuthorizer()
    )

    functionality_control_service = (
        FunctionalityOperationalControlService(
            authorizer=functionality_control_authorizer,
            repository=functionality_control_repository,
        )
    )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Project Control / operational health composition
    #
    # Project Control is read-only. It consumes application-owned health
    # and operational services and owns only its own PCD registry and
    # snapshot repository.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    production_health_configuration = (
        ProductionHealthConfiguration(
            database_engine=provider_usage_engine,
            telegram_bot_factory=None,
            llm_provider=None,
            llm_request_get=None,
        )
    )

    production_health_service = (
        ProductionHealthService(
            configuration=production_health_configuration,
        )
    )

    production_readiness_service = (
        production_health_service._readiness_service
    )

    operational_health_snapshot_service = (
        OperationalHealthSnapshotService(
            readiness_service=production_readiness_service,
        )
    )

    operational_event_service = (
        OperationalEventService()
    )

    operational_dashboard_service = (
        OperationalDashboardService(
            health_snapshot_service=operational_health_snapshot_service,
            event_service=operational_event_service,
        )
    )

    privacy_governance_composition = (
        get_privacy_governance_composition()
    )

    project_registry = PostgreSQLProjectRegistry(
        provider_usage_session_factory,
    )

    project_registry_service = ProjectRegistryService(
        project_registry,
    )

    project_discovery_repository = PostgreSQLProjectDiscoveryRepository(
        provider_usage_session_factory,
    )

    project_discovery_service = ProjectDiscoveryService(
        discovery_repository=project_discovery_repository,
        project_registry=project_registry_service,
    )

    project_snapshot_repository = (
        InMemorySnapshotRepository()
    )

    project_control_service = (
        create_project_control_service(
            registry=project_registry,
            snapshots=project_snapshot_repository,
            health_service=production_health_service,
            dashboard_service=operational_dashboard_service,
            case_registry=(
                privacy_governance_composition.case_registry
            ),
        )
    )

    academy_one_week_workflow = None
    announcement_telegram_client = None

    if academy_config.activation_allowed:
        academy_session_factory = (
            auth_session_factory
        )

        (
            announcement_telegram_client,
            _announcement_publisher,
        ) = _create_announcement_components()

        academy_one_week_workflow = (
            create_academy_one_week_workflow(
                session_factory=academy_session_factory,
                telegram_bot=announcement_telegram_client,
            )
        )

    scheduler_runtime = (
        create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_one_week_workflow,
        )
    )

    application = FastAPI(
        title="EasyChatBot API",
        description="AI character learning platform API",
        version="0.1.0",
        lifespan=lifespan,
    )

    application.state.scheduler_runtime = (
        scheduler_runtime
    )

    application.state.auth_repository = (
        auth_repository
    )

    application.state.authentication_service = (
        authentication_service
    )

    application.state.developer_admin_service = (
        developer_admin_service
    )

    application.state.developer_admin_transaction_service = (
        developer_admin_transaction_service
    )

    application.state.auth_engine = (
        auth_engine
    )

    application.state.provider_usage_engine = (
        provider_usage_engine
    )

    application.state.provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        provider_usage_dashboard_service
    )

    application.state.functionality_control_service = (
        functionality_control_service
    )

    application.state.private_chat_service_factory = (
        private_chat_service_factory
    )

    application.state.learner_context_service = (
        learner_context_service
    )

    application.state.study_material_service = (
        study_material_service
    )

    application.state.private_chat_character_instruction = (
        private_chat_character_instruction
    )

    application.state.project_control_service = (
        project_control_service
    )

    application.state.project_discovery_service = (
        project_discovery_service
    )

    application.state.production_health_service = (
        production_health_service
    )

    application.state.operational_dashboard_service = (
        operational_dashboard_service
    )

    application.state.announcement_telegram_client = (
        announcement_telegram_client
    )

    application.add_middleware(
        CORSMiddleware,
        allow_origins=[
            "http://localhost:3000",
            "http://127.0.0.1:3000",
            "http://localhost:5173",
            "http://127.0.0.1:5173",
        ],
        allow_credentials=True,
        allow_methods=["*"],
        allow_headers=["*"],
    )

    application.include_router(
        authentication_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        private_chat_router,
        prefix="/api",
    )

    application.include_router(
        learner_context_router,
        prefix="/api",
    )

    application.include_router(
        study_material_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        developer_admin_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_calls_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_reconnect_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_call_trigger_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        voice_notification_acceptance_router,
    )

    operations_center_composition = (
        create_operations_center_composition(
            provider_usage_dashboard_service=(
                provider_usage_dashboard_service
            ),
            functionality_control_service=(
                functionality_control_service
            ),
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_operations_center_router(
            composition=operations_center_composition,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_persistence_operations_router(
            persistence_diagnostics_service,
            operator_dependency=require_permission(
                Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
            ),
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_project_control_router(
            project_control=project_control_service,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_project_registry_router(
            project_registry=project_registry_service,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_project_discovery_router(
            project_discovery=project_discovery_service,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_email_workflows_router(
            session_factory=provider_usage_session_factory,
            operator_dependency=require_permission(
                Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
            ),
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        privacy_governance_router,
    )

    processing_registry = (
        create_beta1_processing_registry()
    )

    screening_service = ScreeningService()

    screening_adapter = (
        ProcessingActivityScreeningAdapter(
            screening_service,
        )
    )

    application.include_router(
        create_privacy_screening_router(
            processing_registry=processing_registry,
            screening_adapter=screening_adapter,
        ),
    )

    application.include_router(
        provider_management_controls_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_audit_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_operations_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_evidence_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        governance_change_lifecycle_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_intelligence_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_decision_support_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_reports_router,
    )

    application.include_router(
        cybersecurity_live_reports_router,
    )

    # Q7 / M4 - Questionnaire Runtime
    #
    # Authenticated learner runtime endpoint:
    # POST /api/academy/questionnaires/answers
    #
    # Learner identity is derived server-side from the authenticated
    # principal. The runtime reuses the existing AssessmentService and
    # synchronous PostgreSQL persistence boundary.
    application.include_router(
        create_questionnaire_runtime_router(
            runtime=questionnaire_runtime,
        ),
    )

    # Academy World - Static Questionnaires
    #
    # This exposes:
    # GET /api/academy/worlds/{world_id}/questionnaires
    #
    # Questionnaire content remains controlled/static.
    # Recommendation metadata is handled separately.

    application.include_router(
        academy_questionnaires_router,
    )

    return application


app = create_application()


__all__ = [
    "app",
    "create_application",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\api\announcement_operations_approval.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.application.announcements.approval.async_approval_service import (
    AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


announcement_publish_permission = require_permission(
    Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH
)


class PublicationApprovalRequest(BaseModel):
    pass


class PublicationApprovalResponse(BaseModel):
    publication_id: str
    status: str
    approved_at: str | None


def create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
    *,
    approval_service: AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
) -> APIRouter:
    if approval_service is None:
        raise ValueError("approval_service cannot be None.")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/announcement-operations",
        tags=["announcement-operations"],
    )

    @router.post(
        "/publications/{publication_id}/approve",
        response_model=PublicationApprovalResponse,
        status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    )
    async def approve_publication(
        publication_id: str,
        _request: PublicationApprovalRequest,
        principal: Principal = Depends(announcement_publish_permission),
    ) -> PublicationApprovalResponse:
        try:
            result = await approval_service.approve(
                publication_id=publication_id,
                actor=principal.user_id,
            )
        except LookupError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

        publication = result.publication

        return PublicationApprovalResponse(
            publication_id=publication.publication_id,
            status=publication.status.value,
            approved_at=(
                publication.approved_at.isoformat()
                if publication.approved_at is not None
                else None
            ),
        )

    return router


__all__ = [
    "PublicationApprovalRequest",
    "PublicationApprovalResponse",
    "announcement_publish_permission",
    "create_announcement_operations_approval_router",
]

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status

    OperationsCenterComposition,
    create_operations_center_composition,
)

)

    OperationsEnvironment,

    FunctionalityOperationalControlService,

class FunctionalityStopRequest(BaseModel):
    environment: OperationsEnvironment
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class FunctionalityResumeRequest(BaseModel):
    environment: OperationsEnvironment


def _control_unavailable() -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
        detail="Functionality operational control is not configured.",
    )


def _result_as_dict(result: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "functionality_id": result.functionality_id,
        "environment": result.environment,
        "availability": result.availability,
        "action": result.action,
        "reason": result.reason,
        "changed_at": result.changed_at.isoformat(),
        "changed_by": result.changed_by,
    }


def create_operations_center_router(
    *,
    composition: OperationsCenterComposition | None = None,
) -> APIRouter:
    resolved_composition = composition or create_operations_center_composition()

    dashboard_api = resolved_composition.dashboard_api
    chat_operations_api = resolved_composition.chat_operations_api
    functionality_health_service = resolved_composition.functionality_health_service
    functionality_control_service = (
        resolved_composition.functionality_control_service
    )
    event_service = resolved_composition.event_service
    provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        resolved_composition.provider_usage_dashboard_service
    )
    platform_intelligence_review_api = (
        resolved_composition.platform_intelligence_review_api
    )

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/operations-center",
        tags=["operations-center"],
    )

    @router.get("/dashboard")
    async def get_dashboard() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await dashboard_api.get_dashboard()

    @router.get("/chat-operations")
    async def get_chat_operations_dashboard() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_dashboard()

    @router.get("/chat-operations/private-classroom")
    async def get_private_classroom_operations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_private_classroom_details()

    @router.get("/chat-operations/announcement-group")
    async def get_announcement_group_operations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_announcement_group_details()

    @router.get("/functionality-health")
    async def get_functionality_health(
        environment: OperationsEnvironment | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await functionality_health_service.get_health(
            environment=environment
        )

    @router.post(
        "/functionality/{functionality_id}/stop",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL))],
    )
    def stop_functionality(
        functionality_id: str,
        request: FunctionalityStopRequest,
        principal: Principal = Depends(
            require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL)
        ),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if functionality_control_service is None:
            raise _control_unavailable()

        try:
            result = functionality_control_service.stop(
                actor=principal,
                actor_name=principal.user_id,
                environment=request.environment.value,
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
                reason=request.reason,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except PermissionError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

        return _result_as_dict(result)

    @router.post(
        "/functionality/{functionality_id}/resume",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL))],
    )
    def resume_functionality(
        functionality_id: str,
        request: FunctionalityResumeRequest,
        principal: Principal = Depends(
            require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL)
        ),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if functionality_control_service is None:

            result = functionality_control_service.resume(
                actor=principal,
                actor_name=principal.user_id,
                environment=request.environment.value,
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except PermissionError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

        return _result_as_dict(result)



    @router.get(
        "/platform-intelligence/reviews",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))],
    )
    def get_platform_intelligence_reviews() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return platform_intelligence_review_api.list_reviews()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,

        try:
            result = platform_intelligence_review_api.get_review(review_item_id)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        if result is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail="Platform Intelligence review item not found.",
            )
        return result

    @router.get(

    )

        try:

        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    @router.get("/operational-events")
    def get_operational_events() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        incidents = event_service.list_incidents()
        return [
            event.as_dict()
            for incident in incidents
            for event in incident.events
        ]

    @router.get("/provider-usage")
    def get_provider_usage() -> dict[str, Any]:
        if provider_usage_dashboard_service is None:
            return {
                "status": "not_configured",
                "reason": "provider_usage_dashboard_service_not_configured",
            }
        return provider_usage_dashboard_service.get_snapshot()

    return router

from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request

from app.application.operations_center.email_workflow_status_service import (
    EmailWorkflowStatusService,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_email_workflow_registry import (
    PostgreSQLEmailWorkflowRegistry,
)
from cybersecurity.email.email_provider_manager import EmailProviderManager


def create_email_workflows_router(
    *,
    session_factory,
    operator_dependency: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    if session_factory is None:
        raise ValueError("session_factory is required.")
    if operator_dependency is None:
        raise ValueError("operator_dependency is required.")

    registry = PostgreSQLEmailWorkflowRegistry(session_factory)

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/operations-center/email-workflows",
        tags=["Operations Center", "Email Workflows"],
    )

    @router.get("")
    async def get_email_workflows(
        request: Request,
        _operator: Any = Depends(operator_dependency),
    ):
        service = EmailWorkflowStatusService(
            scheduler_runtime=request.app.state.scheduler_runtime,
            provider_manager=EmailProviderManager(),
            registry=registry,
        )
        return service.snapshot()

    @router.get("/{workflow_id}")
    async def get_email_workflow(
        workflow_id: str,
        request: Request,
        _operator: Any = Depends(operator_dependency),
    ):
        service = EmailWorkflowStatusService(
            scheduler_runtime=request.app.state.scheduler_runtime,
            provider_manager=EmailProviderManager(),
            registry=registry,
        )
        workflow = service.get(workflow_id)

        if workflow is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=404,
                detail="Email workflow not found.",
            )

        return workflow

    return router

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.api.routes.developer_admin_transaction_contracts import (
    DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse,
    DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse,
    DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_service import (
    DeveloperAdminLastAdminError,
    DeveloperAdminService,
    DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError,
    DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_transaction_service import (
    DeveloperAdminTransactionService,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.trusted_approval_provider import (
    TrustedApprovalRequest,
    TrustedApprovalUnavailableError,
    UnavailableTrustedApprovalProvider,
)

    TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
)

from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


def _get_service(request: Request) -> DeveloperAdminService:
    service = getattr(request.app.state, "developer_admin_service", None)
    if service is None:
        raise RuntimeError("developer_admin_service is not configured.")
    return service


def _get_transaction_service(
    request: Request,
) -> DeveloperAdminTransactionService:
    service = getattr(
        request.app.state,
        "developer_admin_transaction_service",
        None,
    )
    if service is None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "developer_admin_transaction_service is not configured."
        )
    return service


def _get_approval_provider(request: Request):
    return getattr(
        request.app.state,
        "developer_admin_trusted_approval_provider",
        UnavailableTrustedApprovalProvider(),
    )


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/operations-center/developer-administrators",
    tags=["operations-center-developer-administrators"],
)


@router.get("")
async def list_developer_administrators(
    request: Request,
    _principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
):
    administrators = await _get_service(request).list_developer_administrators()

    return {
        "administrators": [
            {
                "user_id": item.user_id,
                "email": item.email,
                "username": item.username,
                "status": item.status,
                "email_verified": item.email_verified,
            }
            for item in administrators
        ]
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Legacy collection mutation retirement boundary
#
# Direct Developer Administrator mutations are no longer supported.
#
# The only supported mutation path is:
#
#   create transaction
#       ->
#   trusted approval
#       ->
#   execute transaction
#
# This explicit POST tombstone exists only for the former collection
# mutation endpoint. It performs no mutation.
#
# The former DELETE /{user_id} endpoint intentionally does NOT exist.
# Keeping that route out of the router is part of the API contract.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@router.post("")
async def legacy_direct_create_developer_administrator() -> None:
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
        detail=(
            "Developer Administrator direct mutation endpoint "
            "is no longer available."
        ),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Transaction-authorized Developer Admin mutations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@router.post(
    "/transactions/grant",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
)
async def create_grant_transaction(
    payload: DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse:
    try:
        authorization = _get_transaction_service(request).create_grant_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=payload.target_user_id,
        )
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    transaction = authorization.transaction
    risk_context = dict(transaction.risk_context)

    return DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=transaction.transaction_id,
        operation=transaction.operation,
        audience=risk_context["audience"],
        target_user_id=UUID(risk_context["target_user_id"]),
        state=authorization.state.value,
        expires_at=authorization.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/revoke",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
)
async def create_revoke_transaction(
    payload: DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse:
    try:
        authorization = _get_transaction_service(request).create_revoke_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=payload.target_user_id,
        )
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    transaction = authorization.transaction
    risk_context = dict(transaction.risk_context)

    return DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=transaction.transaction_id,
        operation=transaction.operation,
        audience=risk_context["audience"],
        target_user_id=UUID(risk_context["target_user_id"]),
        state=authorization.state.value,
        expires_at=authorization.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/{authorization_id}/approve",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse,
)
async def approve_transaction(
    authorization_id: str,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse:
    transaction_service = _get_transaction_service(request)

    authorization = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    if authorization is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Transaction authorization was not found.",
        )

    provider = _get_approval_provider(request)

    try:
        approval = await provider.approve(
            request=TrustedApprovalRequest(
                authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
                principal_id=principal.user_id,
                audience=transaction_service.AUDIENCE,
                transaction_id=authorization.transaction.transaction_id,
                requested_at=authorization.created_at,
            )
        )

        approved = transaction_service.attach_trusted_approval(
            principal=principal,
            approval=approval,
        )
    except TrustedApprovalUnavailableError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    return DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse(
        authorization_id=approved.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=approved.transaction.transaction_id,
        state=approved.state.value,
        expires_at=approved.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/{authorization_id}/execute",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse,
)
async def execute_transaction(
    authorization_id: str,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse:
    transaction_service = _get_transaction_service(request)

    authorization = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    if authorization is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Transaction authorization was not found.",
        )

    try:
        transaction = authorization.transaction

        if transaction.operation == transaction_service.GRANT_OPERATION:
            await transaction_service.execute_grant(
                principal=principal,
                authorization_id=authorization_id,
                transaction=transaction,
            )
        elif transaction.operation == transaction_service.REVOKE_OPERATION:
            await transaction_service.execute_revoke(
                principal=principal,
                authorization_id=authorization_id,
                transaction=transaction,
            )
        else:
            raise PermissionError(
                "Developer Administrator transaction operation is invalid."
            )

    except DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError,
        DeveloperAdminLastAdminError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    consumed = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    state = (
        consumed.state.value
        if consumed is not None
        else "CONSUMED"
    )

    return DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization_id,
        state=state,
    )


__all__ = ["router"]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\api\routes\developer_admin_transaction_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    target_user_id: UUID


class DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request contract for executing one already-authorized transaction.

    The client supplies only the authorization reference.
    The canonical transaction, including its target, is resolved server-side
    from the stored transaction authorization.
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    authorization_id: str


class DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    authorization_id: str
    transaction_id: str
    operation: str
    audience: str
    target_user_id: UUID
    state: str
    expires_at: datetime


class DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    authorization_id: str
    transaction_id: str
    state: str
    expires_at: datetime


class DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    authorization_id: str
    state: str


__all__ = [
    "DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse",
    "DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest",
    "DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionRequest",
    "DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse",
    "DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcement_operations\publication_execution_ports.py =====
"""WS-22.3.1 provider/channel-independent ports."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Protocol

from app.domain.announcement_publication.execution import (
    PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PublicationForExecution:
    """Minimal execution projection required by the use case.

    The adapter/application layer can map the existing WS-22.1
    publication model into this projection without coupling the execution
    engine to a specific channel provider.
    """

    publication_id: str
    status: str
    destination: str
    rendered_content: str


class PublicationExecutionRepository(Protocol):
    """Port for loading and recording publication execution state."""

    async def get_for_execution(
        self,
        publication_id: str,
    ) -> PublicationForExecution | None:
        ...

    async def record_execution(
        self,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        ...


class PublicationPublisher(Protocol):
    """Provider/channel-neutral publishing port."""

    async def publish(
        self,
        *,
        publication: PublicationForExecution,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> str:
        """Publish and return an opaque provider reference.

        Implementations must not expose credentials through the result.
        """
        ...


class PublicationAuditSink(Protocol):
    """Port for durable/observable publication audit events."""

    async def record(self, event: object) -> None:
        ...


class PublicationIdempotencyStore(Protocol):
    """Port preventing duplicate external publication attempts."""

    async def get_result(
        self,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult | None:
        ...

    async def store_result(
        self,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        ...


__all__ = [
    "PublicationAuditSink",
    "PublicationExecutionRepository",
    "PublicationExecutionResult",
    "PublicationForExecution",
    "PublicationIdempotencyStore",
    "PublicationPublisher",
]


===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcement_operations\publication_execution_service.py =====
"""WS-22.3.1 publication execution application contract."""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.announcement_operations.publication_execution_ports import (
    PublicationAuditSink,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
    PublicationIdempotencyStore,
    PublicationPublisher,
)
from app.domain.announcement_publication.execution import (
    PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    PublicationAuditEvent,
    PublicationExecutionErrorCode,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
)


class PublicationExecutionService:
    """Execute an approved publication through an injected publishing port.

    This service contains orchestration only. It does not know Telegram,
    HTTP, frontend concerns, credentials, or provider-specific APIs.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        repository: PublicationExecutionRepository,
        publisher: PublicationPublisher,
        idempotency_store: PublicationIdempotencyStore,
        audit_sink: PublicationAuditSink,
    ) -> None:
        if any(
            dependency is None
            for dependency in (
                repository,
                publisher,
                idempotency_store,
                audit_sink,
            )
        ):
            raise ValueError("All publication execution dependencies are required.")

        self._repository = repository
        self._publisher = publisher
        self._idempotency_store = idempotency_store
        self._audit_sink = audit_sink

    async def execute(
        self,
        command: PublishAnnouncementCommand,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        """Execute one explicit publication request."""

        existing = await self._idempotency_store.get_result(
            command.idempotency_key
        )
        if existing is not None:
            return existing

        publication = await self._repository.get_for_execution(
            command.publication_id
        )

        if publication is None:
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.INVALID_STATE,
                error_message="Publication is not available for execution.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        if publication.status.lower() not in {"approved", "ready_to_publish"}:
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.INVALID_STATE,
                error_message="Publication is not in an executable state.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        try:
            provider_reference = await self._publisher.publish(
                publication=publication,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
            )
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            result = PublicationExecutionResult.failed(
                publication_id=command.publication_id,
                idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
                error_code=PublicationExecutionErrorCode.PROVIDER_ERROR,
                error_message=str(exc) or "Publication provider failed.",
            )
            await self._record_result(command, result)
            return result

        result = PublicationExecutionResult.succeeded(
            publication_id=command.publication_id,
            idempotency_key=command.idempotency_key,
            provider_reference=provider_reference,
        )
        await self._record_result(command, result)
        return result

    async def _record_result(
        self,
        command: PublishAnnouncementCommand,
        result: PublicationExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        await self._idempotency_store.store_result(result)
        await self._repository.record_execution(result)

        await self._audit_sink.record(
            PublicationAuditEvent(
                event_name="announcement_publication_execution",
                publication_id=result.publication_id,
                requested_by=command.requested_by,
                status=result.status,
                occurred_at=result.executed_at,
                idempotency_key=result.idempotency_key,
                provider_reference=result.provider_reference,
                error_code=result.error_code,
                safe_metadata={"execution_status": result.status.value},
            )
        )


__all__ = ["PublicationExecutionService"]


===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcements\announcement_operations_service.py =====
"""Application service for controlled Announcement Operations lifecycle."""
from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from app.domain.announcements.announcement import Announcement
from app.domain.announcements.publication_event import PublicationEvent, PublicationEventType
from .announcement_repository import AnnouncementRepository
from .publication_event_repository import PublicationEventRepository


class AnnouncementOperationsService:
    """Coordinates lifecycle changes and immutable audit events.

    This foundation intentionally does not deliver to Telegram. Delivery is a
    later adapter behind an explicit port so the domain remains provider-independent.
    """

    def __init__(self, *, repository: AnnouncementRepository, event_repository: PublicationEventRepository) -> None:
        self._repository = repository
        self._events = event_repository

    async def create(self, *, title: str, body: str, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        if not title.strip() or not body.strip() or not actor.strip():
            raise ValueError("title, body and actor are required.")
        announcement = Announcement(title=title.strip(), body=body.strip(), created_by=actor.strip())
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.CREATED, actor, correlation_id)
        return announcement

    async def mark_ready_for_review(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.mark_ready_for_review()
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.READY_FOR_REVIEW, actor, correlation_id)
        return announcement

    async def approve(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.approve(actor)
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.APPROVED, actor, correlation_id)
        return announcement

    async def request_publication(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.request_publication()
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.PUBLISH_REQUESTED, actor, correlation_id)
        return announcement

    async def mark_published(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None = None, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.mark_published()
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.PUBLISHED, actor, correlation_id, provider_message_id=provider_message_id)
        return announcement

    async def mark_failed(self, announcement_id: UUID, *, actor: str, error_code: str, correlation_id: str | None = None) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._require(announcement_id)
        announcement.mark_failed()
        await self._repository.save(announcement)
        await self._audit(announcement, PublicationEventType.FAILED, actor, correlation_id, error_code=error_code)
        return announcement

    async def get(self, announcement_id: UUID) -> Announcement:
        return await self._require(announcement_id)

    async def list(self) -> tuple[Announcement, ...]:
        return await self._repository.list()

    async def audit_log(self, announcement_id: UUID) -> tuple[PublicationEvent, ...]:
        await self._require(announcement_id)
        return await self._events.list_for_publication(announcement_id)

    async def _require(self, announcement_id: UUID) -> Announcement:
        announcement = await self._repository.get(announcement_id)
        if announcement is None:
            raise LookupError(f"Announcement {announcement_id} was not found.")
        return announcement

    async def _audit(self, announcement: Announcement, event_type: PublicationEventType, actor: str, correlation_id: str | None, *, error_code: str | None = None, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> None:
        if not actor.strip():
            raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")
        await self._events.append(PublicationEvent(
            publication_id=announcement.id,
            event_type=event_type,
            actor=actor,
            correlation_id=correlation_id or str(uuid4()),
            error_code=error_code,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        ))


===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcements\execution\approved_publication_execution.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from uuid import UUID

from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    PublicationExecutionRequest,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.domain.announcements.publication import Publication


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ApprovedPublicationExecutionService:
    """Application boundary from WS-23 approved state into WS-22."""

    durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if self.durable_execution is None:
            raise ValueError("durable_execution cannot be None.")

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        publication: Publication,
        actor: str,
        idempotency_key: str,
        correlation_id: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        """Delegate an approved publication to the existing WS-22 service.

        WS-22 remains responsible for:
        - durable execution;
        - idempotency;
        - execution persistence;
        - execution audit;
        - retry semantics;
        - controlled provider invocation.
        """

        if publication is None:
            raise ValueError("publication cannot be None.")

        if not actor.strip():
            raise ValueError("actor cannot be empty.")

        if not idempotency_key.strip():
            raise ValueError("idempotency_key cannot be empty.")

        if not correlation_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("correlation_id cannot be empty.")

        try:
            publication_uuid = UUID(publication.publication_id)
        except (ValueError, AttributeError) as exc:
            raise ValueError(
                "Approved publication ID must be a UUID for WS-22 durable execution."
            ) from exc

        request = PublicationExecutionRequest(
            publication_id=publication_uuid,
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key.strip(),
            correlation_id=correlation_id.strip(),
            actor=actor.strip(),
        )

        return await self.durable_execution.execute(
            publication=_PublicationExecutionAdapter(publication),
            request=request,
        )


class _PublicationExecutionAdapter:
    """Adapt the WS-23 publication to the existing WS-22 execution contract."""

    def __init__(self, publication: Publication) -> None:
        self._publication = publication

    @property
    def id(self) -> UUID:
        return UUID(self._publication.publication_id)

    @property
    def title(self) -> str:
        return self._publication.title

    @property
    def content(self) -> str:
        return self._publication.content

    @property
    def status(self):
        return self._publication.status

    def begin_publish(self) -> None:
        self._publication.begin_publish()

    def mark_failure(self, reason: str) -> None:
        self._publication.mark_failure(reason)

    def publish_success(self, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> None:
        del provider_message_id
        self._publication.publish_success()


__all__ = ["ApprovedPublicationExecutionService"]

from __future__ import annotations

    DurablePublicationExecutionService,

    durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService

    *,
    durable_execution: DurablePublicationExecutionService,

        raise ValueError("durable_execution cannot be None.")

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcements\execution\durable_execution_composition.py =====
"""WS-22.3.5.3 canonical durable publication execution composition.

This module is the composition boundary for the WS-22 durable execution
vertical slice.

It deliberately does not create a new persistence architecture. The
canonical PostgreSQL engine and async session factory are supplied by the
platform persistence boundary in:

    app.infrastructure.persistence.database

The resulting object graph is:

    PostgreSQL
        |
        v
    canonical async session factory
        |
        +--> execution repository
        |
        +--> audit repository
                  |
                  v
        DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
                  |
                  v
        DurablePublicationExecutionService

Provider selection remains outside this module.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncEngine, AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.announcements.execution.audit_adapter import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionAudit,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_execution_service import (
    DurablePublicationExecutionService,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.publication_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.announcements.sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Fully composed WS-22 durable execution boundary."""

    engine: AsyncEngine
    session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession]
    execution_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository
    audit_repository: SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository
    audit: DurablePublicationExecutionAudit
    service: DurablePublicationExecutionService

    async def dispose(self) -> None:
        """Release the shared PostgreSQL async engine."""

        await self.engine.dispose()


def create_durable_publication_execution_composition(
    *,
    publisher,
) -> DurablePublicationExecutionComposition:
    """Create the canonical WS-22 durable execution graph.

    The publisher is deliberately injected because provider selection belongs
    outside the durable execution composition.

    This function creates exactly one PostgreSQL engine and exactly one async
    session factory. Both durable repositories receive that same factory.
    """

    if publisher is None:
        raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    session_factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)

    execution_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    audit_repository = SqlAlchemyPublicationAuditRepository(
        session_factory
    )

    audit = DurablePublicationExecutionAudit(
        repository=audit_repository,
    )

    service = DurablePublicationExecutionService(
        publisher=publisher,
        execution_repository=execution_repository,
        audit=audit,
    )

    return DurablePublicationExecutionComposition(
        engine=engine,
        session_factory=session_factory,
        execution_repository=execution_repository,
        audit_repository=audit_repository,
        audit=audit,
        service=service,
    )


__all__ = [
    "DurablePublicationExecutionComposition",
    "create_durable_publication_execution_composition",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcements\execution\durable_execution_service.py =====
"""WS-22.3.2 durable execution orchestration."""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    AnnouncementPublisher,
    PublicationExecutionRequest,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
    PublicationExecutionStatus,
)
from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    DurableExecutionStatus,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
)


class DurablePublicationExecutionService:
    """Retry-safe execution service backed by durable idempotency state.

    A completed idempotency key is never sent again.

    A STARTED record is treated as an interrupted attempt and is retryable.

    A FAILED record is also retryable. Before a retry is executed, the
    repository is responsible for incrementing the durable attempt counter
    and transitioning the record back to STARTED.

    The repository implementation must make creation atomic against a
    unique idempotency key.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publisher: AnnouncementPublisher,
        execution_repository: PublicationExecutionRepository,
        audit,
    ) -> None:
        if publisher is None:
            raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")

        if execution_repository is None:
            raise ValueError("execution_repository cannot be None.")

        if audit is None:
            raise ValueError("audit cannot be None.")

        self._publisher = publisher
        self._repository = execution_repository
        self._audit = audit

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        publication,
        request: PublicationExecutionRequest,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        existing = await self._repository.get_by_idempotency_key(
            request.idempotency_key
        )

        # A successful execution is terminal for the idempotency key.
        # Never call the external provider again.
        if (
            existing is not None
            and existing.status is DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED
        ):
            return PublicationExecutionResult(
                publication_id=existing.publication_id,
                status=PublicationExecutionStatus.IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY,
                idempotency_key=existing.idempotency_key,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                provider_message_id=existing.provider_message_id,
            )

        self._assert_approved(publication)

        # The repository owns the atomic unique-key operation. If another
        # worker already created the same key, re-read and honour its state.
        if existing is None:
            try:
                record = await self._repository.create_started(
                    publication_id=publication.id,
                    idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
                    correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                )
            except Exception:
                record = await self._repository.get_by_idempotency_key(
                    request.idempotency_key
                )

                if record is None:
                    raise

        elif existing.status is DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED:
            # A FAILED execution is retryable. Reuse the same durable
            # idempotency record and increment its attempt number rather
            # than creating a second row.
            record = await self._repository.start_retry(
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            )

        else:
            # STARTED represents an interrupted/incomplete attempt.
            # Preserve the existing durable attempt number and retry it.
            record = existing

        publication.begin_publish()

        try:
            provider_message_id = await self._publisher.publish(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                title=publication.title,
                content=publication.content,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            error_code = self._error_code(exc)

            publication.mark_failure(error_code)

            await self._repository.mark_failed(
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
                error_code=error_code,
            )

            await self._audit.record_failure(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                actor=request.actor,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                error_code=error_code,
            )

            return PublicationExecutionResult(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                status=PublicationExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                error_code=error_code,
            )

        publication.publish_success(provider_message_id)

        await self._repository.mark_succeeded(
            idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

        await self._audit.record_success(
            publication_id=publication.id,
            actor=request.actor,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

        return PublicationExecutionResult(
            publication_id=publication.id,
            status=PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED,
            idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _assert_approved(publication) -> None:
        value = getattr(publication.status, "value", publication.status)

        if value != "approved":
            raise ValueError(
                "Only approved publications may be executed."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _error_code(exc: Exception) -> str:
        explicit = getattr(exc, "error_code", None)

        return (
            explicit.strip()[:100]
            if isinstance(explicit, str) and explicit.strip()
            else type(exc).__name__.upper()[:100]
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\announcements\execution\publication_execution_service.py =====
"""WS-22.3.1 provider-independent publication execution service."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Protocol
from uuid import UUID

from app.application.announcements.execution.contracts import (
    AnnouncementPublisher,
    PublicationExecutionRequest,
    PublicationExecutionResult,
    PublicationExecutionStatus,
)


class ExecutablePublication(Protocol):
    """Minimum aggregate surface required by the execution service."""

    id: UUID
    title: str
    content: str
    status: object

    def begin_publish(self) -> None: ...

    def publish_success(self, provider_message_id: str | None = None) -> None: ...

    def mark_failure(self, reason: str) -> None: ...


class PublicationExecutionAudit(Protocol):
    """Minimal audit boundary used by execution.

    The concrete publication-event repository remains an existing
    application concern; this protocol prevents execution from coupling to
    a particular persistence implementation.
    """

    async def record_success(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: UUID,
        actor: str,
        correlation_id: str,
        provider_message_id: str | None,
    ) -> None: ...

    async def record_failure(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id: UUID,
        actor: str,
        correlation_id: str,
        error_code: str,
    ) -> None: ...


class PublicationExecutionService:
    """Execute approved publications through a provider-independent port.

    The service owns orchestration and idempotency. It does not know which
    channel/provider performs the external publication.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        publisher: AnnouncementPublisher,
        audit: PublicationExecutionAudit,
    ) -> None:
        if publisher is None:
            raise ValueError("publisher cannot be None.")
        if audit is None:
            raise ValueError("audit cannot be None.")
        self._publisher = publisher
        self._audit = audit
        self._completed: dict[str, PublicationExecutionResult] = {}

    async def execute(
        self,
        *,
        publication: ExecutablePublication,
        request: PublicationExecutionRequest,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionResult:
        if publication is None:
            raise ValueError("publication cannot be None.")

        existing = self._completed.get(request.idempotency_key)
        if existing is not None:
            return PublicationExecutionResult(
                publication_id=existing.publication_id,
                status=PublicationExecutionStatus.IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY,
                idempotency_key=existing.idempotency_key,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                provider_message_id=existing.provider_message_id,
                error_code=existing.error_code,
                error_message=existing.error_message,
            )

        self._assert_approved(publication)
        publication.begin_publish()

        try:
            provider_message_id = await self._publisher.publish(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                title=publication.title,
                content=publication.content,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            error_code = self._error_code(exc)
            publication.mark_failure(error_code)
            await self._audit.record_failure(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                actor=request.actor,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                error_code=error_code,
            )
            result = PublicationExecutionResult(
                publication_id=publication.id,
                status=PublicationExecutionStatus.FAILED,
                idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
                correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
                error_code=error_code,
            )
            return result

        publication.publish_success(provider_message_id)
        await self._audit.record_success(
            publication_id=publication.id,
            actor=request.actor,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )

        result = PublicationExecutionResult(
            publication_id=publication.id,
            status=PublicationExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED,
            idempotency_key=request.idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
        )
        self._completed[request.idempotency_key] = result
        return result

    @staticmethod
    def _assert_approved(publication: ExecutablePublication) -> None:
        status_value = getattr(publication.status, "value", publication.status)
        if status_value != "approved":
            raise ValueError(
                "Only approved publications may be executed."
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _error_code(exc: Exception) -> str:
        """Return a stable, non-sensitive operational error code."""
        explicit = getattr(exc, "error_code", None)
        if isinstance(explicit, str) and explicit.strip():
            return explicit.strip()[:100]
        return type(exc).__name__.upper()[:100]
