===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\developer_admin\developer_admin_audit.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Protocol
from uuid import UUID, uuid4


class DeveloperAdminAuditError(Exception):
    """Base error for Developer Administrator audit operations."""


class DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(DeveloperAdminAuditError):
    """Raised when an audit event contains unsafe or invalid data."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DeveloperAdminAuditEvent:
    event_id: UUID
    event_type: str
    actor_user_id: UUID | None
    target_user_id: UUID
    actor_email: str | None
    target_email: str
    occurred_at: datetime
    correlation_id: str | None
    outcome: str
    metadata: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()
    actor_type: str = "user"


class DeveloperAdminAuditPort(Protocol):
    async def record(self, event: DeveloperAdminAuditEvent) -> DeveloperAdminAuditEvent: ...


class DeveloperAdminAuditService:
    """Creates validated security-audit events for Developer Admin changes."""

    GRANTED = "developer_admin.granted"
    REVOKED = "developer_admin.revoked"
    BOOTSTRAPPED = "developer_admin.bootstrap"

    SUCCESS = "success"
    FAILURE = "failure"

    ACTOR_USER = "user"
    ACTOR_BOOTSTRAP = "bootstrap"

    _FORBIDDEN_KEYS = frozenset(
        {
            "password",
            "password_hash",
            "token",
            "access_token",
            "refresh_token",
            "session",
            "cookie",
            "api_key",
            "secret",
            "database_url",
            "prompt",
            "model_response",
        }
    )

    def __init__(self, repository: DeveloperAdminAuditPort | None = None) -> None:
        self._repository = repository

    async def build_event(
        self,
        *,
        event_type: str,
        actor_user_id: UUID,
        target_user_id: UUID,
        actor_email: str,
        target_email: str,
        correlation_id: str | None = None,
        outcome: str = SUCCESS,
        metadata: dict[str, str] | None = None,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminAuditEvent:
        if event_type not in {self.GRANTED, self.REVOKED}:
            raise DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(
                "Unsupported Developer Admin audit event type."
            )

        if outcome not in {self.SUCCESS, self.FAILURE}:
            raise DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(
                "Unsupported Developer Admin audit outcome."
            )

        if not actor_email or not target_email:
            raise DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(
                "Actor and target email are required for audit evidence."
            )

        safe_metadata = self._validate_metadata(metadata or {})

        return DeveloperAdminAuditEvent(
            event_id=uuid4(),
            event_type=event_type,
            actor_user_id=actor_user_id,
            target_user_id=target_user_id,
            actor_email=actor_email,
            target_email=target_email,
            occurred_at=datetime.now().astimezone(),
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            outcome=outcome,
            metadata=tuple(sorted(safe_metadata.items())),
            actor_type=self.ACTOR_USER,
        )

    async def build_bootstrap_event(
        self,
        *,
        target_user_id: UUID,
        target_email: str,
        correlation_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminAuditEvent:
        if not target_email:
            raise DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(
                "Target email is required for bootstrap audit evidence."
            )

        safe_metadata = self._validate_metadata(
            {
                "operation": "bootstrap",
                "mechanism": "controlled_backend_bootstrap",
            }
        )

        return DeveloperAdminAuditEvent(
            event_id=uuid4(),
            event_type=self.BOOTSTRAPPED,
            actor_user_id=None,
            target_user_id=target_user_id,
            actor_email=None,
            target_email=target_email,
            occurred_at=datetime.now().astimezone(),
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            outcome=self.SUCCESS,
            metadata=tuple(sorted(safe_metadata.items())),
            actor_type=self.ACTOR_BOOTSTRAP,
        )

    async def record_change(self, **kwargs) -> DeveloperAdminAuditEvent:
        if self._repository is None:
            raise DeveloperAdminAuditError("Audit repository is required for persistence.")

        event = await self.build_event(**kwargs)
        return await self._repository.record(event)

    @classmethod
    def _validate_metadata(cls, metadata: dict[str, str]) -> dict[str, str]:
        if not isinstance(metadata, dict):
            raise DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(
                "Audit metadata must be a dictionary."
            )

        safe: dict[str, str] = {}

        for key, value in metadata.items():
            normalized_key = str(key).strip().lower()

            if normalized_key in cls._FORBIDDEN_KEYS:
                raise DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(
                    f"Unsafe audit metadata key: {normalized_key}."
                )

            if not normalized_key or len(normalized_key) > 100:
                raise DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(
                    "Audit metadata key is invalid."
                )

            normalized_value = str(value)

            if len(normalized_value) > 1000:
                raise DeveloperAdminAuditValidationError(
                    "Audit metadata value is too long."
                )

            safe[normalized_key] = normalized_value

        return safe

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\developer_admin\developer_admin_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from uuid import UUID

from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_audit import (
    DeveloperAdminAuditService,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_uow import (
    DeveloperAdminUnitOfWork,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_uow_factory import (
    DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Role
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal
from app.infrastructure.auth.postgresql_auth_repository import (
    AuthUserRecord,
    PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository,
)


class DeveloperAdminError(Exception):
    """Base error for Developer Administration operations."""


class DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(DeveloperAdminError):
    """Requested target user does not exist."""


class DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError(DeveloperAdminError):
    """Requested target user cannot receive Developer Admin privileges."""


class DeveloperAdminLastAdminError(DeveloperAdminError):
    """The final Developer Administrator cannot be removed."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DeveloperAdminRecord:
    user_id: UUID
    email: str
    username: str
    status: str
    email_verified: bool


class DeveloperAdminService:
    """Application boundary for controlled Developer Administrator management.

    Production composition injects a DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory.
    Each Developer Administrator mutation obtains a fresh Unit-of-Work
    instance from that factory so role mutations and their security audit
    evidence share one database transaction without sharing mutable UoW
    state between operations.

    The repository-only constructor path is retained for compatibility with
    existing isolated service tests. That compatibility path is deliberately
    not used by the production application.
    """

    ROLE = Role.DEVELOPER_ADMIN.value

    def __init__(
        self,
        repository: PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository,
        *,
        unit_of_work_factory: DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory | None = None,
        audit_service: DeveloperAdminAuditService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if repository is None:
            raise ValueError("repository is required.")

        if (unit_of_work_factory is None) != (audit_service is None):
            raise ValueError(
                "unit_of_work_factory and audit_service must be supplied together."
            )

        self._repository = repository
        self._unit_of_work_factory = unit_of_work_factory
        self._audit_service = audit_service

    async def list_developer_administrators(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[DeveloperAdminRecord, ...]:
        users = await self._repository.get_users_with_role(
            role=self.ROLE,
        )

        return tuple(
            self._to_record(user)
            for user in users
        )

    async def grant_developer_admin(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
        actor: Principal | None = None,
        correlation_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        # Production path: role mutation + audit share one transaction.
        if self._unit_of_work_factory is not None:
            if actor is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "actor is required when using the atomic Developer Admin path."
                )

            return await self._grant_atomically(
                actor=actor,
                user_id=user_id,
                correlation_id=correlation_id,
            )

        # Compatibility path for existing isolated application tests.
        # Production composition never uses this path.
        return await self._grant_legacy(
            user_id=user_id,
        )

    async def revoke_developer_admin(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
        actor: Principal | None = None,
        correlation_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        # Production path: role mutation + audit share one transaction.
        if self._unit_of_work_factory is not None:
            if actor is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "actor is required when using the atomic Developer Admin path."
                )

            await self._revoke_atomically(
                actor=actor,
                user_id=user_id,
                correlation_id=correlation_id,
            )
            return

        # Compatibility path for existing isolated application tests.
        # Production composition never uses this path.
        await self._revoke_legacy(
            user_id=user_id,
        )

    async def _grant_atomically(
        self,
        *,
        actor: Principal,
        user_id: UUID,
        correlation_id: str | None,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        assert self._unit_of_work_factory is not None
        assert self._audit_service is not None

        # A fresh UoW is created for every Developer Admin operation.
        async with self._unit_of_work_factory.create() as uow:
            target = await uow.get_user(user_id)

            if target is None:
                raise DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(
                    "Target user does not exist."
                )

            if target.status != "active" or not target.email_verified:
                raise DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError(
                    "Developer Administrator privileges require an active, "
                    "verified user."
                )

            created = await uow.add_user_role(
                user_id=user_id,
                role=self.ROLE,
            )

            if created:
                event = await self._audit_service.build_event(
                    event_type=DeveloperAdminAuditService.GRANTED,
                    actor_user_id=UUID(actor.user_id),
                    target_user_id=target.id,
                    actor_email=actor.email,
                    target_email=target.email,
                    correlation_id=correlation_id,
                    metadata={"operation": "grant"},
                )

                await uow.record_audit(event)

            return self._to_record(target)

    async def _revoke_atomically(
        self,
        *,
        actor: Principal,
        user_id: UUID,
        correlation_id: str | None,
    ) -> None:
        assert self._unit_of_work_factory is not None
        assert self._audit_service is not None

        # A fresh UoW is created for every Developer Admin operation.
        async with self._unit_of_work_factory.create() as uow:
            target = await uow.get_user(user_id)

            if target is None:
                raise DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(
                    "Target user does not exist."
                )

            roles = await uow.get_user_roles(user_id)

            if self.ROLE not in roles:
                return

            current_count = await uow.count_users_with_role(
                role=self.ROLE,
            )

            if current_count <= 1:
                raise DeveloperAdminLastAdminError(
                    "The last Developer Administrator cannot be removed."
                )

            removed = await uow.remove_user_role(
                user_id=user_id,
                role=self.ROLE,
            )

            if removed:
                event = await self._audit_service.build_event(
                    event_type=DeveloperAdminAuditService.REVOKED,
                    actor_user_id=UUID(actor.user_id),
                    target_user_id=target.id,
                    actor_email=actor.email,
                    target_email=target.email,
                    correlation_id=correlation_id,
                    metadata={"operation": "revoke"},
                )

                await uow.record_audit(event)

    async def _grant_legacy(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        user = await self._repository.get_user_for_identity(user_id)

        if user is None:
            raise DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(
                "Target user does not exist."
            )

        if user.status != "active" or not user.email_verified:
            raise DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError(
                "Developer Administrator privileges require an active, "
                "verified user."
            )

        await self._repository.add_user_role(
            user_id=user_id,
            role=self.ROLE,
        )

        return self._to_record(user)

    async def _revoke_legacy(
        self,
        *,
        user_id: UUID,
    ) -> None:
        user = await self._repository.get_user_for_identity(user_id)

        if user is None:
            raise DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError(
                "Target user does not exist."
            )

        current_count = await self._repository.count_users_with_role(
            role=self.ROLE,
        )

        roles = await self._repository.get_user_roles(user_id)

        if self.ROLE not in roles:
            return

        if current_count <= 1:
            raise DeveloperAdminLastAdminError(
                "The last Developer Administrator cannot be removed."
            )

        await self._repository.remove_user_role(
            user_id=user_id,
            role=self.ROLE,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _to_record(
        user: AuthUserRecord,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        return DeveloperAdminRecord(
            user_id=user.id,
            email=user.email,
            username=user.username,
            status=user.status,
            email_verified=user.email_verified,
        )


__all__ = [
    "DeveloperAdminError",
    "DeveloperAdminLastAdminError",
    "DeveloperAdminRecord",
    "DeveloperAdminService",
    "DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError",
    "DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\developer_admin\developer_admin_transaction_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Protocol
from uuid import UUID, uuid4

from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_service import (
    DeveloperAdminRecord,
    DeveloperAdminService,
)
from app.application.identity.transaction_authorization import (
    TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
    TransactionAuthorizationApplicationService,
)
from app.domain.identity.authentication_assurance import (
    AuthenticationAssuranceLevel,
)
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal
from app.domain.identity.transaction_authorization import (
    CanonicalTransaction,
    TransactionAuthorization,
    TransactionAuthorizationDecision,
    TrustedApprovalEvidence,
)


class TrustedClock(Protocol):
    """Trusted application/server clock."""

    def now(self) -> datetime:
        ...


class DeveloperAdminTransactionService:
    """Application boundary for authorized Developer Admin mutations.

    Security flow:

        authenticated principal
            -> exact Developer Admin transaction
            -> pending user approval
            -> trusted assurance / approval
            -> exact transaction binding
            -> single-use consumption
            -> Developer Admin mutation

    The target is always extracted from the authorized canonical transaction.
    No execution request is allowed to replace the authorized target.
    """

    AUDIENCE = "easychatbot.developer_admin"

    GRANT_OPERATION = "developer_admin.grant"
    REVOKE_OPERATION = "developer_admin.revoke"

    REQUIRED_ASSURANCE_LEVEL = AuthenticationAssuranceLevel.A2
    DEFAULT_TRANSACTION_TTL = timedelta(minutes=5)

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        developer_admin_service: DeveloperAdminService,
        transaction_authorization_service: (
            TransactionAuthorizationApplicationService
        ),
        clock: TrustedClock,
    ) -> None:
        if developer_admin_service is None:
            raise ValueError("developer_admin_service is required.")

        if transaction_authorization_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "transaction_authorization_service is required."
            )

        if clock is None:
            raise ValueError("clock is required.")

        self._developer_admin_service = developer_admin_service
        self._transaction_authorization_service = (
            transaction_authorization_service
        )
        self._clock = clock

    def create_grant_transaction(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        target_user_id: UUID,
        created_at: datetime | None = None,
        expires_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        return self._create_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=target_user_id,
            operation=self.GRANT_OPERATION,
            risk_operation="grant",
            created_at=created_at,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

    def create_revoke_transaction(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        target_user_id: UUID,
        created_at: datetime | None = None,
        expires_at: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        return self._create_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=target_user_id,
            operation=self.REVOKE_OPERATION,
            risk_operation="revoke",
            created_at=created_at,
            expires_at=expires_at,
        )

    def _create_transaction(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        target_user_id: UUID,
        operation: str,
        risk_operation: str,
        created_at: datetime | None,
        expires_at: datetime | None,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        self._require_valid_principal(principal)

        if not isinstance(target_user_id, UUID):
            raise ValueError("target_user_id must be a UUID.")

        effective_created_at = (
            created_at
            if created_at is not None
            else self._clock.now()
        )

        effective_expires_at = (
            expires_at
            if expires_at is not None
            else effective_created_at + self.DEFAULT_TRANSACTION_TTL
        )

        self._validate_transaction_times(
            created_at=effective_created_at,
            expires_at=effective_expires_at,
        )

        transaction = CanonicalTransaction.from_mapping(
            transaction_id=str(uuid4()),
            principal_id=principal.user_id,
            amount="0.00",
            currency="N/A",
            merchant_or_service="easychatbot",
            operation=operation,
            timestamp=effective_created_at,
            nonce=str(uuid4()),
            risk_context={
                "target_user_id": str(target_user_id),
                "operation": risk_operation,
                "audience": self.AUDIENCE,
            },
        )

        return (
            self._transaction_authorization_service.create_pending_authorization(
                principal=principal,
                transaction=transaction,
                authorization_id=str(uuid4()),
                created_at=effective_created_at,
                expires_at=effective_expires_at,
            )
        )

    def get_authorization(
        self,
        *,
        authorization_id: str,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization | None:
        if not authorization_id:
            return None

        return self._transaction_authorization_service.registry.get(
            authorization_id
        )

    def attach_trusted_approval(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        approval: TrustedApprovalEvidence,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        authorization = self.get_authorization(
            authorization_id=approval.authorization_id,
        )

        if authorization is None:
            raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                "Transaction authorization was not found."
            )

        return self._transaction_authorization_service.attach_user_approval(
            principal=principal,
            authorization=authorization,
            approval=approval,
            clock=self._clock,
        )

    async def execute_grant(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        authorization_id: str,
        transaction: CanonicalTransaction | None = None,
        clock: TrustedClock | None = None,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminRecord:
        authorization = self._resolve_execution_authorization(
            authorization_id=authorization_id,
            transaction=transaction,
            expected_operation=self.GRANT_OPERATION,
        )

        result = self._consume(
            principal=principal,
            authorization=authorization,
            clock=clock,
        )

        if result.decision is not TransactionAuthorizationDecision.ALLOW:
            raise PermissionError("transaction authorization denied")

        target_user_id = self._target_user_id_from_transaction(
            authorization.transaction,
            expected_operation=self.GRANT_OPERATION,
            expected_risk_operation="grant",
        )

        return await self._developer_admin_service.grant_developer_admin(
            actor=principal,
            user_id=target_user_id,
        )

    async def execute_revoke(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        authorization_id: str,
        transaction: CanonicalTransaction | None = None,
        clock: TrustedClock | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        authorization = self._resolve_execution_authorization(
            authorization_id=authorization_id,
            transaction=transaction,
            expected_operation=self.REVOKE_OPERATION,
        )

        result = self._consume(
            principal=principal,
            authorization=authorization,
            clock=clock,
        )

        if result.decision is not TransactionAuthorizationDecision.ALLOW:
            raise PermissionError("transaction authorization denied")

        target_user_id = self._target_user_id_from_transaction(
            authorization.transaction,
            expected_operation=self.REVOKE_OPERATION,
            expected_risk_operation="revoke",
        )

        await self._developer_admin_service.revoke_developer_admin(
            actor=principal,
            user_id=target_user_id,
        )

    def _consume(
        self,
        *,
        principal: Principal,
        authorization: TransactionAuthorization,
        clock: TrustedClock | None,
    ):
        self._require_valid_principal(principal)

        return self._transaction_authorization_service.consume_authorization(
            principal=principal,
            authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
            expected_transaction=authorization.transaction,
            clock=clock if clock is not None else self._clock,
            required_assurance_level=self.REQUIRED_ASSURANCE_LEVEL,
        )

    def _resolve_execution_authorization(
        self,
        *,
        authorization_id: str,
        transaction: CanonicalTransaction | None,
        expected_operation: str,
    ) -> TransactionAuthorization:
        if not authorization_id:
            raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                "Transaction authorization ID is required."
            )

        authorization = self.get_authorization(
            authorization_id=authorization_id,
        )

        if authorization is None:
            raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                "Transaction authorization was not found."
            )

        if transaction is not None:
            if not isinstance(transaction, CanonicalTransaction):
                raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                    "Canonical transaction is required."
                )

            if (
                transaction.canonical_representation()
                != authorization.transaction.canonical_representation()
            ):
                raise TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError(
                    "Canonical transaction binding mismatch."
                )

        self._target_user_id_from_transaction(
            authorization.transaction,
            expected_operation=expected_operation,
            expected_risk_operation=(
                "grant"
                if expected_operation == self.GRANT_OPERATION
                else "revoke"
            ),
        )

        return authorization

    def _target_user_id_from_transaction(
        self,
        transaction: CanonicalTransaction,
        *,
        expected_operation: str | None = None,
        expected_risk_operation: str | None = None,
    ) -> UUID:
        """Extract and validate the target exclusively from the transaction.

        When no expected operation is supplied, this helper defaults to the
        grant operation because the target-extraction contract is defined
        against the standard Developer Admin grant transaction.

        Execution paths always supply the explicit expected operation and
        risk operation, so grant and revoke cannot be substituted for one
        another.
        """
        if not isinstance(transaction, CanonicalTransaction):
            raise PermissionError("transaction is invalid")

        operation = (
            expected_operation
            if expected_operation is not None
            else self.GRANT_OPERATION
        )

        risk_operation = (
            expected_risk_operation
            if expected_risk_operation is not None
            else "grant"
        )

        if transaction.operation != operation:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction operation is invalid"
            )

        risk_context = dict(transaction.risk_context)

        if risk_context.get("operation") != risk_operation:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction risk operation is invalid"
            )

        if risk_context.get("audience") != self.AUDIENCE:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction audience is invalid"
            )

        target_value = risk_context.get("target_user_id")

        if not target_value:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction target is invalid"
            )

        try:
            return UUID(str(target_value))
        except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError) as exc:
            raise PermissionError(
                "transaction target is invalid"
            ) from exc

    @staticmethod
    def _require_valid_principal(
        principal: Principal,
    ) -> None:
        if principal is None:
            raise PermissionError(
                "authenticated principal is required"
            )

        if not principal.user_id:
            raise PermissionError(
                "authenticated principal is invalid"
            )

        if not principal.email_verified:
            raise PermissionError(
                "authenticated principal email is not verified"
            )

        account_status = getattr(
            principal,
            "account_status",
            None,
        )

        status_value = getattr(
            account_status,
            "value",
            account_status,
        )

        if status_value != "active":
            raise PermissionError(
                "authenticated principal is not active"
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_transaction_times(
        *,
        created_at: datetime,
        expires_at: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        if created_at.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "created_at must be timezone-aware"
            )

        if expires_at.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "expires_at must be timezone-aware"
            )

        if expires_at <= created_at:
            raise ValueError(
                "expires_at must be later than created_at"
            )


__all__ = ["DeveloperAdminTransactionService"]

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any


class HealthStatus(str, Enum):
    HEALTHY = "healthy"
    UNHEALTHY = "unhealthy"
    NOT_CONFIGURED = "not_configured"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HealthCheckResult:
    """Sanitized result of one dependency health check."""

    name: str
    status: HealthStatus
    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    @property
    def is_healthy(self) -> bool:
        return self.status is HealthStatus.HEALTHY


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProductionReadinessResult:
    """Aggregated, dashboard-safe production readiness result."""

    status: HealthStatus
    checks: tuple[HealthCheckResult, ...]

    @property
    def ready(self) -> bool:
        return self.status is HealthStatus.HEALTHY

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "status": self.status.value,
            "ready": self.ready,
            "checks": {
                check.name: {
                    "status": check.status.value,
                    "details": dict(check.details),
                }
                for check in self.checks
            },
        }

    total: int = 0
    open: int = 0
    critical: int = 0
    high: int = 0
    medium: int = 0
    low: int = 0
    info: int = 0

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        return {
            "total": self.total,
            "open": self.open,
            "critical": self.critical,
            "high": self.high,
            "medium": self.medium,
            "low": self.low,
            "info": self.info,

    status: HealthStatus

        DashboardDependencyStatus,

        DashboardIncidentSummary()

        DashboardOperationalMetrics()

            "status": self.status.value,
            "dependencies": [

                for dependency in self.dependencies

            "metrics": self.metrics.as_dict(),

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DashboardIncident:
    """
    Sanitized incident representation for the dashboard.

    Operational references are retained for authorized investigation.
    Learner content and secrets are never part of this model.
    """

    incident_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    surface: OperationalSurface
    severity: str
    status: str
    first_seen: datetime
    last_seen: datetime
    occurrence_count: int
    user_reference: str | None = None
    world_id: str | None = None

    @classmethod
    def from_incident(
        cls,
        incident: OperationalIncident,
    ) -> "DashboardIncident":
        return cls(
            incident_id=incident.incident_id,
            correlation_id=incident.correlation_id,
            surface=incident.surface,
            severity=incident.severity.value,
            status=incident.status.value,
            first_seen=incident.first_seen,
            last_seen=incident.last_seen,
            occurrence_count=incident.occurrence_count,
            user_reference=incident.user_reference,
            world_id=incident.world_id,
        )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "incident_id": self.incident_id,
            "correlation_id": self.correlation_id,
            "surface": self.surface.value,
            "severity": self.severity,
            "status": self.status,
            "first_seen": self.first_seen.isoformat(),
            "last_seen": self.last_seen.isoformat(),
            "occurrence_count": self.occurrence_count,
            "user_reference": self.user_reference,
            "world_id": self.world_id,
        }

class OperationalDashboardSnapshot:
    """
    Stable application contract consumed by the future
    Operations Center UI/API adapter.
    """

    generated_at: datetime
    overall_status: HealthStatus
    health_score: int

    private_classroom: DashboardSurfaceStatus
    announcement_group: DashboardSurfaceStatus

    incidents: tuple[
        DashboardIncident,
        ...
    ] = ()

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "generated_at": (
                self.generated_at.isoformat()
            ),
            "overall": {
                "status": self.overall_status.value,
                "health_score": self.health_score,
            },
            "private_classroom": (
                self.private_classroom.as_dict()
            ),
            "announcement_group": (
                self.announcement_group.as_dict()
            ),
            "incidents": [
                incident.as_dict()
                for incident in self.incidents
            ],
        }

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\health\operational_dashboard_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from .health_result import HealthStatus
from .operational_dashboard_models import (
    DashboardDependencyStatus,
    DashboardIncident,
    DashboardIncidentSummary,
    DashboardOperationalMetrics,
    DashboardSurfaceStatus,
    OperationalDashboardSnapshot,
)
from .operational_event import OperationalEventType, OperationalSurface
from .operational_event_service import OperationalEventService
from .operational_health_snapshot import OperationalHealthSnapshotService


class OperationalDashboardService:
    """
    Application service producing the read-only operational dashboard.

    Dependency health is probed fresh for every snapshot.

    Incident/event state comes from the application-owned
    OperationalEventService.

    The event service is exposed through a read-only property so dependent
    Operations Center application APIs can explicitly reuse the same
    authoritative operational state.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        health_snapshot_service: OperationalHealthSnapshotService,
        event_service: OperationalEventService,
    ) -> None:
        if health_snapshot_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "health_snapshot_service cannot be None."
            )

        if event_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "event_service cannot be None."
            )

        self._health_snapshot_service = (
            health_snapshot_service
        )

        self._event_service = event_service

    @property
    def event_service(self) -> OperationalEventService:
        """
        Return the authoritative application-owned event service.

        This does not create a new service or event store.

        It allows dependent read-only Operations Center APIs to use the
        exact same operational state as this dashboard service.
        """
        return self._event_service

    async def create_dashboard_snapshot(
        self,
    ) -> OperationalDashboardSnapshot:
        """
        Create a fresh operational dashboard snapshot.

        Dependency health and current incident state are evaluated for
        every request.
        """

        health = (
            await self._health_snapshot_service.create_snapshot()
        )

        incidents = self._event_service.list_incidents()

        operational_events = tuple(
            event
            for incident in incidents
            for event in incident.events
        )

        dashboard_incidents = tuple(
            DashboardIncident.from_incident(
                incident
            )
            for incident in incidents
        )

        private_incidents = tuple(
            incident
            for incident in incidents
            if incident.surface
            is OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
        )

        announcement_incidents = tuple(
            incident
            for incident in incidents
            if incident.surface
            is OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP
        )

        private_events = tuple(
            event
            for event in operational_events
            if event.surface
            is OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
        )

        announcement_events = tuple(
            event
            for event in operational_events
            if event.surface
            is OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP
        )

        return OperationalDashboardSnapshot(
            generated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            overall_status=health.overall_status,
            health_score=self._calculate_health_score(
                health.overall_status,
                incidents,
            ),
            private_classroom=self._surface_status(
                health=health,
                surface=OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM,
                incidents=private_incidents,
                events=private_events,
            ),
            announcement_group=self._surface_status(
                health=health,
                surface=OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP,
                incidents=announcement_incidents,
                events=announcement_events,
            ),
            incidents=dashboard_incidents,
        )

    def list_incidents(
        self,
        *,
        surface: OperationalSurface | None = None,
    ):
        """
        Read current incidents through the existing operational-event
        abstraction.

        No second incident store is created.
        """

        return self._event_service.list_incidents(
            surface=surface
        )

    def _surface_status(
        self,
        *,
        health,
        surface: OperationalSurface,
        incidents,
        events,
    ) -> DashboardSurfaceStatus:
        return DashboardSurfaceStatus(
            surface=surface,
            status=self._surface_health_status(
                health=health,
                surface=surface,
                incidents=incidents,
            ),
            dependencies=self._dependency_statuses(
                health
            ),
            incidents=self._incident_summary(
                incidents
            ),
            metrics=self._operational_metrics(
                events
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _dependency_statuses(health):
        result = []

        for name, value in health.dependency_health.items():
            try:
                status = HealthStatus(
                    value.get(
                        "status",
                        HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY.value,
                    )
                )
            except ValueError:
                status = HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY

            result.append(
                DashboardDependencyStatus(
                    name=name,
                    status=status,
                    details=dict(
                        value.get(
                            "details",
                            {}
                        )
                    ),
                )
            )

        return tuple(result)

    @staticmethod
    def _incident_summary(incidents):
        return DashboardIncidentSummary(
            total=len(incidents),
            open=sum(
                incident.is_open
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            critical=sum(
                incident.severity.value == "critical"
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            high=sum(
                incident.severity.value == "high"
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            medium=sum(
                incident.severity.value == "medium"
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            low=sum(
                incident.severity.value == "low"
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            info=sum(
                incident.severity.value == "info"
                for incident in incidents
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _operational_metrics(events):
        return DashboardOperationalMetrics(
            message_failures=sum(
                event.event_type
                is OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE
                for event in events
            ),
            rate_limit_events=sum(
                event.event_type
                is OperationalEventType.RATE_LIMIT
                for event in events
            ),
            abuse_events=sum(
                event.event_type
                is OperationalEventType.ABUSE
                for event in events
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _surface_health_status(
        *,
        health,
        surface,
        incidents,
    ):
        if any(
            incident.is_open
            and incident.severity.value
            in {"critical", "high"}
            for incident in incidents
        ):
            return HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY

        if surface is OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM:
            statuses = (
                health.telegram_status,
                health.postgresql_status,
                health.llm_status,
            )
        else:
            statuses = (
                health.telegram_status,
            )

        if all(
            status is HealthStatus.HEALTHY
            for status in statuses
        ):
            return HealthStatus.HEALTHY

        return HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY

    @staticmethod
    def _calculate_health_score(
        overall_status,
        incidents,
    ) -> int:
        score = (
            100
            if overall_status is HealthStatus.HEALTHY
            else 60
        )

        critical_open = sum(
            incident.is_open
            and incident.severity.value == "critical"
            for incident in incidents
        )

        high_open = sum(
            incident.is_open
            and incident.severity.value == "high"
            for incident in incidents
        )

        score -= critical_open * 20
        score -= high_open * 10

        return max(
            0,
            min(100, score),
        )


__all__ = [
    "OperationalDashboardService",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\health\operational_event.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any


class OperationalSurface(str, Enum):
    """
    Operational surface being monitored.

    Private classroom and announcement-group operations
    remain explicitly separated.
    """

    PRIVATE_CLASSROOM = "private_classroom"
    ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP = "announcement_group"


class OperationalEventType(str, Enum):
    """Supported operational event categories."""

    MESSAGE_FAILURE = "message_failure"
    RATE_LIMIT = "rate_limit"
    ABUSE = "abuse"
    PROVIDER_FAILURE = "provider_failure"
    DATABASE_FAILURE = "database_failure"
    TELEGRAM_FAILURE = "telegram_failure"
    NOTIFICATION_FAILURE = "notification_failure"


class OperationalSeverity(str, Enum):
    """Operational incident severity."""

    CRITICAL = "critical"
    HIGH = "high"
    MEDIUM = "medium"
    LOW = "low"
    INFO = "info"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OperationalEvent:
    """
    Sanitized operational event.

    The event intentionally contains operational metadata only.

    It must never contain:
        - learner message content
        - prompts
        - model responses
        - passwords
        - API keys
        - database URLs
        - Telegram bot tokens
    """

    event_type: OperationalEventType
    surface: OperationalSurface
    correlation_id: str

    severity: OperationalSeverity = (
        OperationalSeverity.MEDIUM
    )

    occurred_at: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    user_reference: str | None = None
    world_id: str | None = None

    metadata: dict[str, Any] = field(
        default_factory=dict
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        correlation_id = (
            self.correlation_id
            .replace("\r", "")
            .replace("\n", "")
            .strip()
        )

        if not correlation_id:
            raise ValueError(
                "correlation_id cannot be empty."
            )

        object.__setattr__(
            self,
            "correlation_id",
            correlation_id,
        )

        if self.user_reference is not None:
            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "user_reference",
                self._clean_identifier(
                    self.user_reference
                ),
            )

        if self.world_id is not None:
            object.__setattr__(
                self,
                "world_id",
                self._clean_identifier(
                    self.world_id
                ),
            )

    @staticmethod
    def _clean_identifier(
        value: str,
    ) -> str:
        return (
            str(value)
            .replace("\r", "")
            .replace("\n", "")
            .strip()
        )

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Return an operationally safe representation.

        Learner content is not part of the event contract.
        """

        return {
            "event_type": self.event_type.value,
            "surface": self.surface.value,
            "correlation_id": self.correlation_id,
            "severity": self.severity.value,
            "occurred_at": (
                self.occurred_at.isoformat()
            ),
            "user_reference": self.user_reference,
            "world_id": self.world_id,
            "metadata": dict(self.metadata),
        }


__all__ = [
    "OperationalEvent",
    "OperationalEventType",
    "OperationalSeverity",
    "OperationalSurface",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\health\operational_event_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterable
from uuid import uuid4

from .operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
)
from .operational_incident import (
    IncidentStatus,
    OperationalIncident,
)


class OperationalEventService:
    """
    Application service for operational event correlation
    and incident lifecycle management.

    This service intentionally uses in-memory state for WS-21.9.

    Persistence will be introduced separately once the event
    semantics are proven.

    Responsibilities:
        - accept sanitized operational events
        - correlate repeated events
        - create incidents
        - update occurrence counts
        - maintain incident lifecycle
        - expose dashboard-safe snapshots

    Does NOT:
        - perform remediation
        - send Telegram messages
        - modify production systems
        - execute AI
        - access learner conversation content
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        initial_incidents: Iterable[
            OperationalIncident
        ] = (),
    ) -> None:
        self._incidents: dict[
            str,
            OperationalIncident,
        ] = {
            incident.incident_id: incident
            for incident in initial_incidents
        }

        self._correlation_index: dict[
            str,
            str,
        ] = {}

        for incident in self._incidents.values():
            self._correlation_index[
                incident.correlation_id
            ] = incident.incident_id

    def record_event(
        self,
        event: OperationalEvent,
    ) -> OperationalIncident:
        """
        Record an event.

        If an open incident already exists for the
        correlation ID, the event is aggregated into it.

        Otherwise a new incident is created.
        """

        existing_id = (
            self._correlation_index.get(
                event.correlation_id
            )
        )

        if existing_id is not None:
            incident = self._incidents[
                existing_id
            ]

            if not incident.is_open:
                incident = self._create_incident(
                    event
                )
            else:
                incident.add_event(event)

            return incident

        return self._create_incident(event)

    def get_incident(
        self,
        incident_id: str,
    ) -> OperationalIncident | None:
        return self._incidents.get(
            incident_id
        )

    def get_by_correlation_id(
        self,
        correlation_id: str,
    ) -> OperationalIncident | None:
        incident_id = (
            self._correlation_index.get(
                correlation_id
            )
        )

        if incident_id is None:
            return None

        return self._incidents.get(
            incident_id
        )

    def list_incidents(
        self,
        *,
        surface=None,
        status=None,
    ) -> tuple[
        OperationalIncident,
        ...
    ]:
        """
        Return incidents with optional operational filters.
        """

        incidents = tuple(
            self._incidents.values()
        )

        if surface is not None:
            incidents = tuple(
                incident
                for incident in incidents
                if incident.surface is surface
            )

        if status is not None:
            incidents = tuple(
                incident
                for incident in incidents
                if incident.status is status
            )

        return incidents

    def transition_incident(
        self,
        *,
        incident_id: str,
        status: IncidentStatus,
    ) -> OperationalIncident:
        """
        Explicitly transition an incident.
        """

        incident = self._incidents.get(
            incident_id
        )

        if incident is None:
            raise KeyError(
                f"Unknown incident: {incident_id}"
            )

        incident.transition(status)

        return incident

    def count_open_incidents(self) -> int:
        return sum(
            incident.is_open
            for incident
            in self._incidents.values()
        )

    def dashboard_snapshot(self) -> dict:
        """
        Return a dashboard-safe operational snapshot.
        """

        incidents = tuple(
            self._incidents.values()
        )

        return {
            "total_incidents": len(
                incidents
            ),
            "open_incidents": sum(
                incident.is_open
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            "critical_incidents": sum(
                incident.severity.value
                == "critical"
                and incident.is_open
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            "private_classroom_incidents": sum(
                incident.surface.value
                == "private_classroom"
                and incident.is_open
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            "announcement_group_incidents": sum(
                incident.surface.value
                == "announcement_group"
                and incident.is_open
                for incident in incidents
            ),
            "incidents": [
                incident.as_dict()
                for incident in incidents
            ],
        }

    def _create_incident(
        self,
        event: OperationalEvent,
    ) -> OperationalIncident:
        incident = OperationalIncident(
            incident_id=(
                f"incident-{uuid4().hex}"
            ),
            correlation_id=(
                event.correlation_id
            ),
            event_type=event.event_type,
            surface=event.surface,
            severity=event.severity,
            user_reference=(
                event.user_reference
            ),
            world_id=event.world_id,
            events=[event],
        )

        self._incidents[
            incident.incident_id
        ] = incident

        self._correlation_index[
            incident.correlation_id
        ] = incident.incident_id

        return incident


__all__ = [
    "OperationalEventService",

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum
import re
from typing import Any, Protocol

from .health_result import (
    HealthStatus,
    ProductionReadinessResult,
)
from .operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventType,
    OperationalSurface,
    OperationalSeverity,
)


class TelegramIdentityResolver(Protocol):
    """
    Controlled application boundary for Telegram identity resolution.
    """

    async def resolve(
        self,
        user_reference: str,
    ) -> str | None:
        ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OperationalHealthSnapshot:
    """
    Complete dashboard-safe operational snapshot.
    """

    generated_at: datetime
    overall_status: HealthStatus
    ready: bool

    dependency_health: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]

    events: tuple[OperationalEvent, ...]

    private_classroom_events: tuple[
        OperationalEvent,
        ...
    ]

    announcement_group_events: tuple[
        OperationalEvent,
        ...
    ]

    @property
    def postgresql_status(self) -> HealthStatus:
        return self._dependency_status("postgresql")

    @property
    def llm_status(self) -> HealthStatus:
        return self._dependency_status("llm_provider")

    @property
    def telegram_status(self) -> HealthStatus:
        return self._dependency_status("telegram")

    def _dependency_status(
        self,
        name: str,
    ) -> HealthStatus:
        value = self.dependency_health.get(name)

        if value is None:
            return HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY

        raw_status = value.get(
            "status",
            HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY.value,
        )

        try:
            return HealthStatus(raw_status)
        except ValueError:
            return HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "generated_at": self.generated_at.isoformat(),
            "overall_status": self.overall_status.value,
            "ready": self.ready,
            "dependency_health": {
                name: dict(details)
                for name, details
                in self.dependency_health.items()
            },
            "events": [
                _event_to_dict(event)
                for event in self.events
            ],
            "private_classroom_events": [
                _event_to_dict(event)
                for event in self.private_classroom_events
            ],
            "announcement_group_events": [
                _event_to_dict(event)
                for event in self.announcement_group_events
            ],
        }


class OperationalHealthSnapshotService:
    """
    Produces the complete operational health snapshot.
    """

    _SECRET_PATTERN = re.compile(
        r"(?i)"
        r"(password|passwd|secret|api[_-]?key|"
        r"access[_-]?token|refresh[_-]?token|"
        r"authorization|database[_-]?url|"
        r"telegram[_-]?bot[_-]?token)"
        r"\s*[:=]\s*"
        r"[^,\s;]+"
    )

    _SENSITIVE_KEY_PATTERN = re.compile(
        r"(?i)"
        r"password|passwd|secret|api[_-]?key|"
        r"access[_-]?token|refresh[_-]?token|"
        r"authorization|database[_-]?url|"
        r"bot[_-]?token|message|prompt|response"
    )

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        readiness_service,
        identity_resolver: (
            TelegramIdentityResolver | None
        ) = None,
    ) -> None:
        if readiness_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "readiness_service cannot be None."
            )

        self._readiness_service = readiness_service
        self._identity_resolver = identity_resolver

    async def create_snapshot(
        self,
        *,
        events: tuple[OperationalEvent, ...] = (),
    ) -> OperationalHealthSnapshot:

        readiness = await self._readiness_service.check()

        sanitized_events: list[OperationalEvent] = []

        for event in events:
            sanitized_event = await self._sanitize_event(event)
            sanitized_events.append(sanitized_event)

        private_events = tuple(
            event
            for event in sanitized_events
            if event.surface
            is OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM
        )

        announcement_events = tuple(
            event
            for event in sanitized_events
            if event.surface
            is OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP
        )

        dependency_health = (
            self._sanitize_dependency_health(readiness)
        )

        return OperationalHealthSnapshot(
            generated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            overall_status=readiness.status,
            ready=readiness.ready,
            dependency_health=dependency_health,
            events=tuple(sanitized_events),
            private_classroom_events=private_events,
            announcement_group_events=announcement_events,
        )

    async def _sanitize_event(
        self,
        event: OperationalEvent,
    ) -> OperationalEvent:

        user_reference = event.user_reference

        if user_reference and self._identity_resolver:
            try:
                resolved = await self._identity_resolver.resolve(
                    user_reference
                )
            except Exception:
                resolved = None

            if resolved:
                user_reference = resolved

        sanitized_metadata = self._sanitize_metadata(
            event.metadata
        )

        return OperationalEvent(
            event_type=event.event_type,
            surface=event.surface,
            correlation_id=self._sanitize_identifier(
                event.correlation_id
            ),
            severity=event.severity,
            occurred_at=event.occurred_at,
            user_reference=(
                self._sanitize_identifier(user_reference)
                if user_reference
                else None
            ),
            world_id=(
                self._sanitize_identifier(event.world_id)
                if event.world_id
                else None
            ),
            metadata=sanitized_metadata,
        )

    def _sanitize_dependency_health(
        self,
        readiness: ProductionReadinessResult,
    ) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:

        result: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}

        for check in readiness.checks:
            details = self._sanitize_metadata(
                check.details
            )

            result[
                self._sanitize_identifier(check.name)
            ] = {
                "status": check.status.value,
                "details": details,
            }

        return result

    def _sanitize_metadata(
        self,
        metadata: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:

        sanitized: dict[str, Any] = {}

        for key, value in metadata.items():

            if self._SENSITIVE_KEY_PATTERN.search(
                str(key)
            ):
                continue

            sanitized[
                self._sanitize_identifier(str(key))
            ] = self._sanitize_value(value)

        return sanitized

    def _sanitize_value(
        self,
        value: Any,
    ) -> Any:

        if isinstance(value, dict):
            return self._sanitize_metadata(value)

        if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
            return [
                self._sanitize_value(item)
                for item in value
            ]

        if isinstance(value, str):
            sanitized = self._SECRET_PATTERN.sub(
                "[REDACTED]",
                value,
            )

            if len(sanitized) > 500:
                return sanitized[:500] + "..."

            return sanitized

        if isinstance(value, (int, float, bool)):
            return value

        return str(value)

    @staticmethod
    def _sanitize_identifier(value: str) -> str:
        return (
            str(value)
            .replace("\r", "")
            .replace("\n", "")
            .strip()
        )


def _event_to_dict(
    event: OperationalEvent,
) -> dict[str, Any]:

    return {
        "event_type": event.event_type.value,
        "surface": event.surface.value,
        "correlation_id": event.correlation_id,
        "severity": event.severity.value,
        "occurred_at": event.occurred_at.isoformat(),
        "user_reference": event.user_reference,
        "world_id": event.world_id,
        "metadata": dict(event.metadata),
    }

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\health\operational_incident.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from enum import Enum

from .operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventType,
    OperationalSeverity,
    OperationalSurface,
)


class IncidentStatus(str, Enum):
    """Operational incident lifecycle."""

    DETECTED = "detected"
    OPEN = "open"
    ACKNOWLEDGED = "acknowledged"
    INVESTIGATING = "investigating"
    RESOLVED = "resolved"
    CLOSED = "closed"


@dataclass
class OperationalIncident:
    """
    Correlated operational incident.

    An incident aggregates one or more operational events
    describing the same operational problem.

    This is an application model only. Persistence is deliberately
    handled outside this service.
    """

    incident_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    event_type: OperationalEventType
    surface: OperationalSurface
    severity: OperationalSeverity

    status: IncidentStatus = (
        IncidentStatus.DETECTED
    )

    first_seen: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    last_seen: datetime = field(
        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )
    )

    occurrence_count: int = 1

    user_reference: str | None = None
    world_id: str | None = None

    events: list[OperationalEvent] = field(
        default_factory=list
    )

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if not self.incident_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "incident_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if not self.correlation_id.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "correlation_id cannot be empty."
            )

        if self.occurrence_count < 1:
            raise ValueError(
                "occurrence_count must be at least 1."
            )

    @property
    def is_open(self) -> bool:
        return self.status in {
            IncidentStatus.DETECTED,
            IncidentStatus.OPEN,
            IncidentStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED,
            IncidentStatus.INVESTIGATING,
        }

    def add_event(
        self,
        event: OperationalEvent,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Associate another event with this incident.
        """

        if event.event_type is not self.event_type:
            raise ValueError(
                "Event type does not match incident."
            )

        if event.surface is not self.surface:
            raise ValueError(
                "Event surface does not match incident."
            )

        if (
            event.correlation_id
            != self.correlation_id
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Event correlation ID does not "
                "match incident."
            )

        self.events.append(event)

        self.occurrence_count += 1

        if event.occurred_at > self.last_seen:
            self.last_seen = event.occurred_at

        if (
            self.user_reference is None
            and event.user_reference is not None
        ):
            self.user_reference = (
                event.user_reference
            )

        if (
            self.world_id is None
            and event.world_id is not None
        ):
            self.world_id = event.world_id

    def transition(
        self,
        new_status: IncidentStatus,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Apply an explicit lifecycle transition.
        """

        if not isinstance(
            new_status,
            IncidentStatus,
        ):
            raise TypeError(
                "new_status must be IncidentStatus."
            )

        allowed = {
            IncidentStatus.DETECTED: {
                IncidentStatus.OPEN,
                IncidentStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED,
                IncidentStatus.RESOLVED,
            },
            IncidentStatus.OPEN: {
                IncidentStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED,
                IncidentStatus.INVESTIGATING,
                IncidentStatus.RESOLVED,
            },
            IncidentStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED: {
                IncidentStatus.INVESTIGATING,
                IncidentStatus.RESOLVED,
            },
            IncidentStatus.INVESTIGATING: {
                IncidentStatus.RESOLVED,
            },
            IncidentStatus.RESOLVED: {
                IncidentStatus.CLOSED,
            },
            IncidentStatus.CLOSED: set(),
        }

        if new_status not in allowed[self.status]:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Invalid incident transition: "
                f"{self.status.value} -> "
                f"{new_status.value}"
            )

        self.status = new_status

    def as_dict(self) -> dict:
        """
        Return a dashboard-safe incident representation.
        """

        return {
            "incident_id": self.incident_id,
            "correlation_id": self.correlation_id,
            "event_type": self.event_type.value,
            "surface": self.surface.value,
            "severity": self.severity.value,
            "status": self.status.value,
            "first_seen": (
                self.first_seen.isoformat()
            ),
            "last_seen": (
                self.last_seen.isoformat()
            ),
            "occurrence_count": (
                self.occurrence_count
            ),
            "user_reference": (
                self.user_reference
            ),
            "world_id": self.world_id,
            "events": [
                event.as_dict()
                for event in self.events
            ],
        }


__all__ = [
    "IncidentStatus",
    "OperationalIncident",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from app.infrastructure.health.llm_provider_health_check import (
    LLMProviderHealthCheck,
)
from app.infrastructure.health.postgresql_health_check import (
    PostgreSQLHealthCheck,
)
from app.infrastructure.health.telegram_health_check import (
    TelegramHealthCheck,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProductionHealthConfiguration:
    """
    Composition configuration for production health monitoring.

    Keeps dependency creation outside the readiness service.
    """

    database_engine: Any | None = None

    telegram_bot_factory: Any | None = None

    llm_provider: str | None = None

    llm_request_get: Any | None = None

    def build_checks(self) -> tuple:
        """
        Build production dependency health checks.
        """

        return (
            PostgreSQLHealthCheck(
                engine=self.database_engine,
            ),
            LLMProviderHealthCheck(
                provider=self.llm_provider,
                request_get=self.llm_request_get,
            ),
            TelegramHealthCheck(
                bot_factory=self.telegram_bot_factory,
            ),
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.health.production_readiness_service import (
    ProductionReadinessService,
)
from app.application.health.production_health_configuration import (
    ProductionHealthConfiguration,
)
from app.application.health.health_result import (
    ProductionReadinessResult,
)


class ProductionHealthService:
    """
    Application service exposing production health status.

    Responsibilities:

    - Compose approved production health checks.
    - Execute readiness validation.
    - Return sanitized structured results.

    Does NOT:

    - Render dashboards.
    - Store monitoring data.
    - Send notifications.
    - Access Telegram conversations.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        configuration: ProductionHealthConfiguration,
    ) -> None:

        if configuration is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "configuration cannot be None."
            )

        self._readiness_service = (
            ProductionReadinessService(
                checks=(
                    configuration.build_checks()
                )
            )
        )

    async def check_production_health(
        self,
    ) -> ProductionReadinessResult:
        """
        Execute production dependency checks.
        """

        return await (
            self._readiness_service
            .check()
        )

    "ProductionHealthService",

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\announcement_group_operations_api.py =====
"""
WS-21.18 Announcement Group Operations application API.

Read-only, fresh operational detail for the Announcement Group surface.

The API deliberately reuses the existing OperationalDashboardService
and the authoritative OperationalEventService supplied by the Operations
Center composition root.

No second incident/event store is created.

Security boundary:
    Raw operational event metadata is never exposed through this API.
    Only explicitly approved operational metadata keys are returned.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.application.health.operational_dashboard_service import (
    OperationalDashboardService,
)
from app.application.health.operational_event import (
    OperationalSurface,
)
from app.application.health.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)
from app.application.health.operational_incident import (
    OperationalIncident,
)


# Operational metadata explicitly approved for the Operations Center UI.
#
# Sensitive learner content, prompts, AI responses, credentials, tokens,
# and arbitrary provider payloads are deliberately excluded.
SAFE_EVENT_METADATA_KEYS = frozenset(
    {
        "safe_status",
    }
)


class AnnouncementGroupOperationsAPI:
    """Read-only application boundary for WS-21.18."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        dashboard_service: OperationalDashboardService,
        event_service: OperationalEventService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if dashboard_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "dashboard_service cannot be None."
            )

        self._dashboard_service = dashboard_service

        self._event_service = (
            event_service
            if event_service is not None
            else dashboard_service.event_service
        )

    async def get_details(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Build a fresh Announcement Group snapshot.

        Every request performs fresh dependency health evaluation and reads
        the current operational incident/event state.
        """

        snapshot = (
            await self._dashboard_service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
        )

        incidents = self._event_service.list_incidents(
            surface=OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP,
        )

        events = tuple(
            event
            for incident in incidents
            for event in incident.events
        )

        return {
            "generated_at": snapshot.generated_at.isoformat(),
            "surface": "announcement_group",
            "status": (
                snapshot.announcement_group.status.value
            ),
            "health_score": snapshot.health_score,
            "dependencies": [
                dependency.as_dict()
                for dependency
                in snapshot.announcement_group.dependencies
            ],
            "metrics": (
                snapshot.announcement_group.metrics.as_dict()
            ),
            "incidents": {
                "summary": (
                    snapshot.announcement_group
                    .incidents
                    .as_dict()
                ),
                "items": [
                    _incident_to_dict(incident)
                    for incident in incidents
                ],
            },
            "events": [
                _event_to_dict(event)
                for event in events
            ],
        }


def _incident_to_dict(
    incident: OperationalIncident,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Return an existing incident in dashboard-safe form.

    Raw event metadata is deliberately not serialized.
    """

    return {
        "incident_id": incident.incident_id,
        "correlation_id": incident.correlation_id,
        "event_type": incident.event_type.value,
        "surface": incident.surface.value,
        "severity": incident.severity.value,
        "status": incident.status.value,
        "first_seen": incident.first_seen.isoformat(),
        "last_seen": incident.last_seen.isoformat(),
        "occurrence_count": incident.occurrence_count,
        "user_reference": incident.user_reference,
        "world_id": incident.world_id,
        "events": [
            _event_to_dict(event)
            for event in incident.events
        ],
    }


def _event_to_dict(event) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Serialize an operational event through an explicit security boundary.

    Do not call event.as_dict() here because that currently includes the
    complete raw metadata dictionary.
    """

    metadata = getattr(event, "metadata", {}) or {}

    safe_metadata = {
        key: metadata[key]
        for key in SAFE_EVENT_METADATA_KEYS
        if key in metadata
    }

    return {
        "event_type": event.event_type.value,
        "surface": event.surface.value,
        "correlation_id": event.correlation_id,
        "severity": event.severity.value,
        "occurred_at": event.occurred_at.isoformat(),
        "user_reference": event.user_reference,
        "world_id": event.world_id,
        "metadata": safe_metadata,
    }


__all__ = [
    "AnnouncementGroupOperationsAPI",
]





===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\operations_center\chat_operations_dashboard_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.application.health.operational_dashboard_service import (
    OperationalDashboardService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.announcement_group_operations_api import (
    AnnouncementGroupOperationsAPI,
)
from app.application.operations_center.private_classroom_operations_api import (
    PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI,
)


class ChatOperationsDashboardAPI:
    """Read-only application boundary for Chat Operations."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        dashboard_service: OperationalDashboardService,
        private_classroom_api: PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI | None = None,
        announcement_group_api: AnnouncementGroupOperationsAPI | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if dashboard_service is None:
            raise ValueError("dashboard_service cannot be None.")

        self._dashboard_service = dashboard_service
        self._private_classroom_api = (
            private_classroom_api
            if private_classroom_api is not None
            else PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI(
                dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
            )
        )
        self._announcement_group_api = (
            announcement_group_api
            if announcement_group_api is not None
            else AnnouncementGroupOperationsAPI(
                dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
            )
        )

    async def get_dashboard(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        snapshot = await self._dashboard_service.create_dashboard_snapshot()
        return snapshot.as_dict()

    async def get_private_classroom_details(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._private_classroom_api.get_details()

    async def get_announcement_group_details(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await self._announcement_group_api.get_details()
