from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Protocol

from .privacy_governance_reminder_policy import (
    GovernanceReminderPolicy,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_scheduler import (
    GovernanceReminderRunResult,
    GovernanceReminderStatePort,
    GovernanceReminderEmailPort,
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler,
)


class GovernanceCaseRegistryPort(Protocol):
    """Read-only registry contract required by the operational follow-up job."""

    def all(self) -> list[object]:
        ...


class GovernanceOperationalFollowUpJob(Protocol):
    """Contract suitable for registration with the existing scheduler runtime."""

    def run_once(self, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> GovernanceReminderRunResult:
        ...


@dataclass(slots=True)
class PrivacyGovernanceOperationalFollowUp:
    """Controlled operational entry point for governance follow-up.

    This is deliberately an application job, not a second scheduler framework.
    The existing platform scheduler should invoke ``run_once`` at its approved
    cadence. The governance policy decides whether an individual case is due.

    The job:
    - reads governance cases;
    - evaluates the existing reminder policy;
    - sends controlled decision-support reminders;
    - records reminder evidence/idempotency state.

    It MUST NOT approve, reject, request changes, or mutate governance state.
    """

    scheduler: PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler

    def run_once(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> GovernanceReminderRunResult:
        return self.scheduler.run_once(now=now)


def create_privacy_governance_operational_follow_up(
    *,
    case_registry: GovernanceCaseRegistryPort,
    reminder_state: GovernanceReminderStatePort,
    email_sender: GovernanceReminderEmailPort,
    policy: GovernanceReminderPolicy | None = None,
) -> PrivacyGovernanceOperationalFollowUp:
    """Build the governance follow-up job using existing scheduler infrastructure.

    The caller owns the lifecycle of the state and email adapters. In
    production, ``reminder_state`` should be persistent and idempotent.
    """

    if case_registry is None:
        raise ValueError("case_registry cannot be None.")
    if reminder_state is None:
        raise ValueError("reminder_state cannot be None.")
    if email_sender is None:
        raise ValueError("email_sender cannot be None.")

    scheduler = PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler(
        case_registry=case_registry,
        policy=policy or GovernanceReminderPolicy(),
        reminder_state=reminder_state,
        email_sender=email_sender,
    )
    return PrivacyGovernanceOperationalFollowUp(scheduler=scheduler)


__all__ = [
    "GovernanceCaseRegistryPort",
    "GovernanceOperationalFollowUpJob",
    "PrivacyGovernanceOperationalFollowUp",
    "create_privacy_governance_operational_follow_up",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\privacy\privacy_governance_reminder_scheduler.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Protocol

from .privacy_governance_reminder_history import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_policy import (
    GovernanceReminderPolicy,
    build_decision_proposal,
)
from .privacy_governance_reminder_result import (
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult,
)


class GovernanceReminderStatePort(Protocol):
    def get_last_sent(self, case_id: str) -> Any:
        ...

    def record(
        self,
        *,
        case_id: str,
        sent_at: datetime,
        recipient_role: str,
        email_reference: str | None = None,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


class GovernanceReminderEmailPort(Protocol):
    def send_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        proposal: Any,
    ) -> None:
        ...


class PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort(Protocol):
    async def send_governance_reminder(
        self,
        *,
        case: Any,
        recipient_role: str,
        reason: str,
    ) -> Any:
        ...


class InMemoryGovernanceReminderState(
    PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory
):
    """Process-local Beta reminder state."""

    pass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class GovernanceReminderRunResult:
    """Aggregate result for the operational follow-up invocation."""

    scanned: int
    reminded: int
    case_ids_reminded: tuple[str, ...]


class PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistryPort(Protocol):
    def all(self) -> tuple[Any, ...]:
        ...

    def get(self, case_id: str) -> Any:
        ...


class PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler:
    """Single governance reminder workflow with compatibility contracts.

    Supported application contract:

        run_once()
        reminder_state
        email_sender

    Supported cybersecurity contract:

        run()
        run_case()
        history
        email_service

    This class is not an APScheduler implementation. It is an application
    workflow invoked by the approved scheduler runtime.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        case_registry: Any,
        policy: GovernanceReminderPolicy,
        reminder_state: GovernanceReminderStatePort | None = None,
        email_sender: GovernanceReminderEmailPort | None = None,
        history: PrivacyGovernanceReminderHistory | None = None,
        email_service: PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if case_registry is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "case_registry cannot be None."
            )

        if policy is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "policy cannot be None."
            )

        self.case_registry = case_registry
        self.policy = policy

        self.reminder_state = (
            reminder_state
            or history
            or InMemoryGovernanceReminderState()
        )

        self.email_sender = email_sender
        self.email_service = email_service

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Phase 6J operational follow-up API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def run_once(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> GovernanceReminderRunResult:
        """Run the synchronous operational follow-up contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        cases = tuple(
            self.case_registry.all()
        )

        reminded: list[str] = []

        for case in cases:
            if self._run_sync_case(
                case,
                now=current,
            ):
                reminded.append(
                    case.case_id
                )

        return GovernanceReminderRunResult(
            scanned=len(cases),
            reminded=len(reminded),
            case_ids_reminded=tuple(reminded),
        )

    def _run_sync_case(
        self,
        case: Any,
        *,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> bool:
        if not self.policy.is_due(
            case,
            history=self.reminder_state,
            now=now,
        ):
            return False

        if self.email_sender is None:
            return False

        proposal = build_decision_proposal(case)

        self.email_sender.send_reminder(
            case=case,
            proposal=proposal,
        )

        self.reminder_state.record(
            case_id=case.case_id,
            sent_at=now,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
        )

        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Existing cybersecurity async API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    async def run(
        self,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult, ...]:
        """Evaluate all cases through the async email-service contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        results: list[
            PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult
        ] = []

        for case in self.case_registry.all():
            results.append(
                await self._evaluate_case(
                    case,
                    now=current,
                )
            )

        return tuple(results)

    async def run_case(
        self,
        case_id: str,
        *,
        now: datetime | None = None,
    ) -> PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult:
        """Evaluate one case through the async contract."""

        current = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        self._validate_now(current)

        case = self.case_registry.get(
            case_id
        )

        return await self._evaluate_case(
            case,
            now=current,
        )

    async def _evaluate_case(
        self,
        case: Any,
        *,
        now: datetime,
    ) -> PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult:
        if not self.policy.is_eligible(case):
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="NOT_ELIGIBLE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=False,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason="case_not_pending_dpo_grc",
                generated_at=now,
            )

        if not self.policy.is_due(
            case,
            history=self.reminder_state,
            now=now,
        ):
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="NOT_DUE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=False,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason="reminder_interval_or_cooldown_not_reached",
                generated_at=now,
            )

        reason = (
            "pending_dpo_grc_reminder_due"
        )

        if self.email_service is None:
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="DUE",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=True,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason=reason,
                generated_at=now,
                email_status="not_configured",
            )

        try:
            response = (
                await self.email_service.send_governance_reminder(
                    case=case,
                    recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                    reason=reason,
                )
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
                status="EMAIL_FAILED",
                case_id=case.case_id,
                governance_status=case.governance_status,
                due=True,
                recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
                reason=reason,
                generated_at=now,
                email_status="failed",
                error=str(exc),
            )

        email_status = "sent"
        reference: str | None = None
        case_url: str | None = None

        if isinstance(response, dict):
            email_status = str(
                response.get(
                    "status",
                    "sent",
                )
            )

            value = (
                response.get("reference")
                or response.get("message_id")
            )

            if value is not None:
                reference = str(value)

            url = response.get("case_url")

            if url is not None:
                case_url = str(url)

        elif response is not None:
            reference = str(response)

        self.reminder_state.record(
            case_id=case.case_id,
            sent_at=now,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
            email_reference=reference,
        )

        return PrivacyGovernanceReminderResult(
            status="REMINDER_SENT",
            case_id=case.case_id,
            governance_status=case.governance_status,
            due=True,
            recipient_role=self.policy.recipient_role,
            reason=reason,
            generated_at=now,
            email_status=email_status,
            email_reference=reference,
            case_url=case_url,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_now(
        now: datetime,
    ) -> None:
        if now.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "now must be timezone-aware."
            )


__all__ = [
    "GovernanceReminderEmailPort",
    "GovernanceReminderRunResult",
    "GovernanceReminderStatePort",
    "InMemoryGovernanceReminderState",
    "PrivacyGovernanceCaseRegistryPort",
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderEmailPort",
    "PrivacyGovernanceReminderScheduler",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\privacy\privacy_operational_register.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from .processing_activity_record import ProcessingActivityRecord
from .data_processing_map import DataProcessingMap
from .retention_classification import RetentionClassification
from .privacy_evidence_record import PrivacyEvidenceRecord


@dataclass(slots=True)
class PrivacyOperationalRegister:
    """Governance register linking processing, flow, retention and evidence."""

    processing_activities: dict[str, ProcessingActivityRecord] = field(default_factory=dict)
    processing_maps: dict[str, DataProcessingMap] = field(default_factory=dict)
    retention: dict[str, RetentionClassification] = field(default_factory=dict)
    evidence: dict[str, PrivacyEvidenceRecord] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def add_processing_activity(self, record: ProcessingActivityRecord) -> None:
        if record.activity_id in self.processing_activities:
            raise ValueError(f"Duplicate processing activity: {record.activity_id}")
        self.processing_activities[record.activity_id] = record

    def add_processing_map(self, record: DataProcessingMap) -> None:
        if record.map_id in self.processing_maps:
            raise ValueError(f"Duplicate processing map: {record.map_id}")
        if record.processing_activity_id not in self.processing_activities:
            raise ValueError("Processing activity must exist before data map.")
        self.processing_maps[record.map_id] = record

    def add_retention(self, record: RetentionClassification) -> None:
        if record.classification_id in self.retention:
            raise ValueError(f"Duplicate retention classification: {record.classification_id}")
        self.retention[record.classification_id] = record

    def add_evidence(self, record: PrivacyEvidenceRecord) -> None:
        if record.evidence_id in self.evidence:
            raise ValueError(f"Duplicate evidence record: {record.evidence_id}")
        if record.activity_id not in self.processing_activities:
            raise ValueError("Processing activity must exist before evidence.")
        self.evidence[record.evidence_id] = record

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\functionality_health_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

from app.application.health.health_result import (
    HealthCheckResult,
    HealthStatus,
)
from app.application.health.production_readiness_service import (
    ProductionReadinessService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.environment import (
    OperationsEnvironment,
    resolve_environment,
)
from app.application.provider_management.functionality_operational_control_service import (
    FunctionalityOperationalControlService,
)


class FunctionalityHealthService:
    _FUNCTIONALITIES = (
        ("conversation", "AI Conversation"),
        ("telegram", "Telegram"),
        ("voice_tts", "Voice / TTS"),
        ("lessons", "Lessons"),
        ("review", "Review / Spaced Repetition"),
        ("pdf_presentation", "PDF / Presentation"),
        ("email_workflows", "Email Workflows"),
        ("payments", "Payments"),
        ("authentication", "Authentication"),
        ("scheduler", "Scheduler"),
        ("provider_runtime", "Provider / LLM Runtime"),
        ("database", "Database"),
    )

    _CHECK_TO_FUNCTIONALITIES = {
        "llm_provider": {"conversation", "provider_runtime"},
        "telegram": {"telegram"},
        "postgresql": {"database"},
    }

    _CATEGORY_BY_FUNCTIONALITY = {
        "conversation": "AI",
        "telegram": "Communication",
        "voice_tts": "AI",
        "lessons": "Learning",
        "review": "Learning",
        "pdf_presentation": "Content",
        "email_workflows": "Communication",
        "payments": "Platform",
        "authentication": "Platform",
        "scheduler": "Platform",
        "provider_runtime": "Infrastructure",
        "database": "Infrastructure",
    }

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        readiness_service: ProductionReadinessService,
        functionality_control_service: FunctionalityOperationalControlService | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if readiness_service is None:
            raise ValueError("readiness_service cannot be None.")
        self._readiness_service = readiness_service
        self._functionality_control_service = functionality_control_service

    async def get_health(
        self,
        *,
        environment: OperationsEnvironment | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        resolved_environment = resolve_environment(environment)
        readiness = await self._readiness_service.check()
        checks_by_name = {check.name: check for check in readiness.checks}
        generated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()

        functionalities = [
            self._build_functionality(
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
                name=name,
                check=self._resolve_check(
                    functionality_id=functionality_id,
                    checks_by_name=checks_by_name,
                ),
                environment=resolved_environment,
                checked_at=generated_at,
                availability=self._get_availability(
                    functionality_id=functionality_id,
                    environment=resolved_environment,
                ),
            )
            for functionality_id, name in self._FUNCTIONALITIES
        ]

        attention = [
            item for item in functionalities
            if item["status"] in {"degraded", "unavailable"}
        ]

        counts = {
            "total": len(functionalities),
            "healthy": sum(item["status"] == "healthy" for item in functionalities),
            "degraded": sum(item["status"] == "degraded" for item in functionalities),
            "incident": sum(item["status"] == "unavailable" for item in functionalities),
            "maintenance": 0,
            "disabled": 0,
            "unknown": sum(item["status"] == "unknown" for item in functionalities),
        }

        if counts["incident"] > 0:
            platform_health, score, impact = "incident", 0.0, "high"
        elif counts["degraded"] > 0:
            platform_health, score, impact = "degraded", 50.0, "medium"
        elif counts["healthy"] > 0:
            platform_health, score, impact = "healthy", 100.0, "low"
        else:
            platform_health, score, impact = "unknown", None, "unknown"

        return {
            "generated_at": generated_at,
            "environment": resolved_environment.value,
            "platform_health": platform_health,
            "platform_health_score": score,
            "user_impact": impact,
            "counts": counts,
            "functionalities": functionalities,
            "attention": attention,
        }

    @classmethod
    def _resolve_check(
        cls,
        *,
        functionality_id: str,
        checks_by_name: dict[str, HealthCheckResult],
    ) -> HealthCheckResult | None:
        for check_name, functionality_ids in cls._CHECK_TO_FUNCTIONALITIES.items():
            if functionality_id in functionality_ids:
                return checks_by_name.get(check_name)
        return None

    @classmethod
    def _build_functionality(
        cls,
        *,
        functionality_id: str,
        name: str,
        check: HealthCheckResult | None,
        environment: OperationsEnvironment,
        checked_at: str,
        availability: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if check is None:
            status = "unknown"
            health_score = None
            risk_level = "unknown"
            provider = model = latency_ms = None
            reason = "No dedicated health check is currently available."
        else:
            status = cls._map_status(check.status)
            health_score = (
                100.0 if status == "healthy"
                else 50.0 if status == "degraded"
                else 0.0 if status == "unavailable"
                else None
            )
            risk_level = {
                "healthy": "low",
                "degraded": "medium",
                "unavailable": "high",
                "unknown": "unknown",
            }[status]
            provider = check.details.get("provider")
            model = check.details.get("model")
            latency_ms = check.details.get("latency_ms")
            reason = check.details.get("reason", "Health check completed.")

        return {
            "functionality_id": functionality_id,
            "name": name,
            "category": cls._CATEGORY_BY_FUNCTIONALITY[functionality_id],
            "description": f"Operational health for {name}.",
            "status": status,
            "health_score": health_score,
            "latency_ms": latency_ms,
            "provider": provider,
            "model": model,
            "capability": functionality_id,
            "checked_at": checked_at,
            "controllable": False,
            "risk_level": risk_level,
            "reason": reason,
            "environment": environment.value,
            "availability": availability,
            "policy_status": "not_evaluated",
        }

    def _get_availability(
        self,
        *,
        functionality_id: str,
        environment: OperationsEnvironment,
    ) -> str:
        if self._functionality_control_service is None:
            return "enabled"

        return self._functionality_control_service.get_availability(
            environment=environment.value,
            functionality_id=functionality_id,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _map_status(status: HealthStatus) -> str:
        if status is HealthStatus.HEALTHY:
            return "healthy"
        if status is HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY:
            return "unavailable"
        return "unknown"


__all__ = ["FunctionalityHealthService"]










===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\functionality_operational_control_service.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from threading import RLock
from typing import Protocol

from app.application.provider_management.functionality_registry import (
    is_known_functionality,
)


class FunctionalityControlAuthorizer(Protocol):
    def can_control_functionality(
        self, *, actor: object, environment: str, functionality_id: str
    ) -> bool: ...


class FunctionalityControlAuditor(Protocol):
    def record_functionality_control(
        self,
        *,
        actor: object,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
        action: str,
        reason: str | None,
        changed_at: datetime,
    ) -> None: ...


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OperationalControlResult:
    functionality_id: str
    environment: str
    availability: str
    action: str
    reason: str | None
    changed_at: datetime
    changed_by: str


class FunctionalityControlRepository(Protocol):
    def get_availability(
        self, *, environment: str, functionality_id: str
    ) -> str: ...

    def get_last_action(
        self, *, environment: str, functionality_id: str
    ) -> OperationalControlResult | None: ...

    def record_transition(
        self,
        *,
        functionality_id: str,
        environment: str,
        availability: str,
        action: str,
        reason: str | None,
        changed_at: datetime,
        changed_by: str,
    ) -> OperationalControlResult: ...


class FunctionalityOperationalControlService:
    VALID_ENVIRONMENTS = {"kids", "adults"}

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        authorizer: FunctionalityControlAuthorizer,
        auditor: FunctionalityControlAuditor | None = None,
        repository: FunctionalityControlRepository | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if auditor is None and repository is None:
            raise ValueError("auditor or repository is required.")

        self._authorizer = authorizer
        self._auditor = auditor
        self._repository = repository
        self._lock = RLock()

        # Compatibility fallback for existing unit-test/in-memory usage.
        self._availability: dict[tuple[str, str], str] = {}
        self._last_action: dict[
            tuple[str, str], OperationalControlResult
        ] = {}

    def stop(
        self,
        *,
        actor: object,
        actor_name: str,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
        reason: str,
    ) -> OperationalControlResult:
        self._validate(environment, functionality_id)

        clean_reason = reason.strip()
        if not clean_reason:
            raise ValueError("A reason is required to stop a functionality.")

        self._authorize(actor, environment, functionality_id)

        return self._transition(
            actor=actor,
            actor_name=actor_name,
            environment=environment,
            functionality_id=functionality_id,
            action="stop",
            availability="disabled",
            reason=clean_reason,
        )

    def resume(
        self,
        *,
        actor: object,
        actor_name: str,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
    ) -> OperationalControlResult:
        self._validate(environment, functionality_id)
        self._authorize(actor, environment, functionality_id)

        return self._transition(
            actor=actor,
            actor_name=actor_name,
            environment=environment,
            functionality_id=functionality_id,
            action="resume",
            availability="enabled",
            reason=None,
        )

    def get_availability(
        self,
        *,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
    ) -> str:
        self._validate(environment, functionality_id)

        if self._repository is not None:
            return self._repository.get_availability(
                environment=environment,
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
            )

        with self._lock:
            return self._availability.get(
                (environment, functionality_id),
                "enabled",
            )

    def get_last_action(
        self,
        *,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
    ) -> OperationalControlResult | None:
        self._validate(environment, functionality_id)

        if self._repository is not None:
            return self._repository.get_last_action(
                environment=environment,
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
            )

        with self._lock:
            return self._last_action.get(
                (environment, functionality_id)
            )

    def _authorize(
        self,
        actor: object,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
    ) -> None:
        if not self._authorizer.can_control_functionality(
            actor=actor,
            environment=environment,
            functionality_id=functionality_id,
        ):
            raise PermissionError(
                "Not authorised to control this functionality."
            )

    def _transition(
        self,
        *,
        actor: object,
        actor_name: str,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
        action: str,
        availability: str,
        reason: str | None,
    ) -> OperationalControlResult:
        changed_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        if self._repository is not None:
            return self._repository.record_transition(
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
                environment=environment,
                availability=availability,
                action=action,
                reason=reason,
                changed_at=changed_at,
                changed_by=actor_name,
            )

        result = OperationalControlResult(
            functionality_id=functionality_id,
            environment=environment,
            availability=availability,
            action=action,
            reason=reason,
            changed_at=changed_at,
            changed_by=actor_name,
        )

        with self._lock:
            self._availability[
                (environment, functionality_id)
            ] = availability
            self._last_action[
                (environment, functionality_id)
            ] = result

        if self._auditor is None:
            raise RuntimeError(
                "Control auditor is required for in-memory operation."
            )

        self._auditor.record_functionality_control(
            actor=actor,
            environment=environment,
            functionality_id=functionality_id,
            action=action,
            reason=reason,
            changed_at=changed_at,
        )

        return result

    @classmethod
    def _validate(
        cls,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
    ) -> None:
        if environment not in cls.VALID_ENVIRONMENTS:
            raise ValueError("environment must be 'kids' or 'adults'")

        if not functionality_id.strip():
            raise ValueError("functionality_id is required")

        if not is_known_functionality(functionality_id):
            raise ValueError(
                f"Unknown functionality_id: {functionality_id}"
            )



===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\health_dashboard_service.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center health dashboard service.

Provides the application-level data contract for the
AI Operations Center health dashboard.

Architecture:

Provider Analytics
        |
        +----------------------+
        |                      |
        v                      v

Provider Health         Escalation Repository
        |                      |
        +----------+-----------+
                   |
                   v
        HealthDashboardService
                   |
                   v
        AI Operations Center


Responsibilities:

- aggregate provider operational health;
- expose active provider information;
- expose active escalation information;
- provide a dashboard-ready health summary.


Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- change provider routing;
- create escalations;
- send emails;
- render UI;
- make autonomous operational changes.


Important:

"System confidence" represents operational confidence
derived from provider execution success.

It is not an AI quality score.

Quality intelligence can be incorporated later when
the approved quality evaluation framework is connected.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_repository import (
    EscalationRepository,
)

from app.application.analytics.ai_platform.provider_analytics_service import (
    ProviderAnalyticsService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationStatus,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AIHealthDashboard:
    """
    Dashboard-ready AI Operations Center health data.
    """

    system_confidence: float

    active_providers: tuple[str, ...]

    monitoring_active: bool

    active_escalations: int

    high_priority_escalations: int


class AIHealthDashboardService:
    """
    Generates the AI Operations Center health summary.

    This service is deliberately read-only.

    It aggregates existing analytics and escalation
    information instead of maintaining another source
    of operational truth.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        analytics_service: ProviderAnalyticsService,
        escalation_repository: EscalationRepository,
    ) -> None:

        if analytics_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "ProviderAnalyticsService cannot be None."
            )

        if escalation_repository is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "EscalationRepository cannot be None."
            )

        self._analytics_service = (
            analytics_service
        )

        self._escalation_repository = (
            escalation_repository
        )

    def generate_dashboard(
        self,
    ) -> AIHealthDashboard:
        """
        Generate the current AI Operations health view.
        """

        analytics = (
            self._analytics_service
            .generate_summary()
        )

        escalations = (
            self._escalation_repository
            .get_all()
        )

        active_escalations = [
            escalation
            for escalation in escalations
            if escalation.status
            in (
                EscalationStatus.OPEN,
                EscalationStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED,
            )
        ]

        high_priority_escalations = [
            escalation
            for escalation in active_escalations
            if escalation.severity
            in (
                EscalationSeverity.HIGH,
                EscalationSeverity.CRITICAL,
            )
        ]

        return AIHealthDashboard(
            system_confidence=(
                analytics.success_rate
            ),
            active_providers=(
                analytics.providers
            ),
            monitoring_active=True,
            active_escalations=(
                len(active_escalations)
            ),
            high_priority_escalations=(
                len(high_priority_escalations)
            ),
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\operational_event.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center operational event contract.

Defines the immutable operational facts that can be emitted by
the Runtime, provider-management services, and future agent
framework integrations.

Architecture:

Agent / Runtime / Provider Service
                |
                v
        OperationalEvent
                |
                v
     Operational Event Service
                |
                v
       Operations Center


Responsibilities:

- define a stable operational event contract;
- define supported event categories;
- define operational severity;
- carry correlation and structured metadata;
- remain independent from HTTP, persistence, providers,
  agents, and Runtime implementation details.

Does NOT:

- execute agents;
- execute providers;
- change Runtime routing;
- create autonomous actions;
- persist events;
- send notifications;
- approve escalations.

Operational events represent FACTS, not COMMANDS.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum
from types import MappingProxyType
from typing import Any, Mapping
from uuid import UUID, uuid4


class OperationalEventType(str, Enum):
    """
    Supported categories of operational facts.
    """

    PROVIDER_HEALTH_CHANGED = (
        "provider_health_changed"
    )

    PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED = (
        "provider_incident_created"
    )

    PROVIDER_RECOVERED = (
        "provider_recovered"
    )

    ESCALATION_CREATED = (
        "escalation_created"
    )

    ESCALATION_APPROVAL_REQUIRED = (
        "escalation_approval_required"
    )

    AGENT_EXECUTION_FAILED = (
        "agent_execution_failed"
    )

    AGENT_EXECUTION_COMPLETED = (
        "agent_execution_completed"
    )


class OperationalEventSeverity(str, Enum):
    """
    Operational severity associated with an event.
    """

    INFO = "info"

    WARNING = "warning"

    ERROR = "error"

    CRITICAL = "critical"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OperationalEvent:
    """
    Immutable operational fact.

    Events are deliberately generic so that both the future
    internal AI Operations/Quality framework and the external
    AI Agents framework can emit the same operational contract
    without being coupled to the Operations Center implementation.

    Metadata is structured context only. It must not contain
    executable commands or instructions.
    """

    event_type: OperationalEventType

    severity: OperationalEventSeverity

    source: str

    component: str

    occurred_at: datetime

    message: str

    event_id: UUID

    correlation_id: str | None

    metadata: Mapping[str, Any]

    @classmethod
    def create(
        cls,
        *,
        event_type: OperationalEventType,
        severity: OperationalEventSeverity,
        source: str,
        component: str,
        occurred_at: datetime,
        message: str,
        correlation_id: str | None = None,
        metadata: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> "OperationalEvent":
        """
        Create a validated operational event.

        A new UUID is generated for every event.

        The event timestamp must be timezone-aware so that
        operational events remain unambiguous across environments.
        """

        if not source.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event source "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not component.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event component "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if not message.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event message "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        if occurred_at.tzinfo is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event timestamp "
                "must be timezone-aware."
            )

        if (
            correlation_id is not None
            and not correlation_id.strip()
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event correlation ID "
                "cannot be empty."
            )

        event_metadata = (
            {}
            if metadata is None
            else dict(metadata)
        )

        return cls(
            event_type=event_type,
            severity=severity,
            source=source.strip(),
            component=component.strip(),
            occurred_at=occurred_at,
            message=message.strip(),
            event_id=uuid4(),
            correlation_id=(
                correlation_id.strip()
                if correlation_id is not None
                else None
            ),
            metadata=MappingProxyType(
                event_metadata
            ),
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\operational_event_repository.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center operational event repository.

Provides the persistence boundary for immutable operational
events.

Architecture:

OperationalEvent
        |
        v
OperationalEventRepository
        |
        v
In-Memory / Future Persistent Store


Responsibilities:

- define the repository contract;
- store operational events;
- retrieve events deterministically;
- keep persistence concerns outside the event model.

Does NOT:

- interpret event meaning;
- execute agents;
- execute providers;
- change Runtime routing;
- create autonomous actions;
- send notifications.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from abc import ABC, abstractmethod

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
)


class OperationalEventRepository(ABC):
    """
    Persistence boundary for operational events.
    """

    @abstractmethod
    def save(
        self,
        event: OperationalEvent,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Persist one operational event.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    def get_by_id(
        self,
        event_id,
    ) -> OperationalEvent | None:
        """
        Return an event by its unique identifier.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    def get_all(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[
        OperationalEvent,
        ...
    ]:
        """
        Return all stored events in deterministic order.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError

    @abstractmethod
    def clear(self) -> None:
        """
        Clear repository state.

        Intended primarily for isolated tests and demonstrations.
        """

        raise NotImplementedError


class InMemoryOperationalEventRepository(
    OperationalEventRepository
):
    """
    In-memory operational event repository.

    This implementation is intentionally small and deterministic.
    It can later be replaced by a persistent implementation
    without changing the application service contract.
    """

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._events: dict[
            object,
            OperationalEvent,
        ] = {}

    def save(
        self,
        event: OperationalEvent,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Store an operational event.
        """

        if event is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event cannot be None."
            )

        self._events[
            event.event_id
        ] = event

    def get_by_id(
        self,
        event_id,
    ) -> OperationalEvent | None:
        """
        Retrieve an event by ID.
        """

        if event_id is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event ID cannot be None."
            )

        return self._events.get(
            event_id
        )

    def get_all(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[
        OperationalEvent,
        ...
    ]:
        """
        Return events ordered by occurrence time and event ID.
        """

        return tuple(
            sorted(
                self._events.values(),
                key=lambda event: (
                    event.occurred_at,
                    str(event.event_id),
                ),
            )
        )

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """
        Remove all stored events.
        """

        self._events.clear()

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\operational_event_service.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center operational event service.

Provides the application boundary for recording operational
facts emitted by Runtime components, provider services, and
future agent frameworks.

Architecture:

Agent / Runtime / Provider Service
                |
                v
       OperationalEventService
                |
                v
       OperationalEventRepository
                |
                v
        Operations Center


Responsibilities:

- accept validated operational events;
- persist events through the repository abstraction;
- retrieve operational events;
- provide a stable application boundary for future
  internal and external agent integrations.

Does NOT:

- execute agents;
- execute providers;
- change Runtime routing;
- approve escalations;
- perform autonomous remediation;
- send notifications;
- access persistence directly.

Operational events remain immutable facts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_repository import (
    OperationalEventRepository,
)


class OperationalEventService:
    """
    Application service for operational event management.

    The service deliberately delegates persistence to the
    repository abstraction and contains no infrastructure logic.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        repository: OperationalEventRepository,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the operational event service.
        """

        if repository is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event repository "
                "cannot be None."
            )

        self._repository = repository

    def record(
        self,
        event: OperationalEvent,
    ) -> OperationalEvent:
        """
        Record one operational event.

        The event itself is immutable. The service therefore
        returns the same event after successful persistence.
        """

        if event is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event cannot be None."
            )

        self._repository.save(event)

        return event

    def get_event(
        self,
        event_id: UUID,
    ) -> OperationalEvent | None:
        """
        Retrieve one operational event by ID.
        """

        if event_id is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event ID cannot be None."
            )

        return self._repository.get_by_id(
            event_id
        )

    def list_events(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[
        OperationalEvent,
        ...
    ]:
        """
        Return all operational events in repository order.
        """

        return self._repository.get_all()

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """
        Clear operational event state.

        Intended for isolated tests and demonstrations.
        """

        self._repository.clear()

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\operations_center_dashboard_api.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center dashboard API.

Provides the application-facing read-only boundary for
the Operations Center dashboard.

Architecture:

Operations Center Dashboard Service
                |
                v
      OperationsCenterDashboardFacade
                |
                v
        OperationsCenterDashboardAPI
                |
                v
          HTTP Router

Responsibilities:

- expose the dashboard application contract;
- delegate dashboard generation to the facade;
- keep HTTP concerns outside the application layer;
- preserve compatibility with explicit provider input;
- provide a stable boundary for future Operations Center
  integrations.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- execute agents;
- modify Runtime routing;
- access repositories directly;
- approve escalations;
- send notifications;
- perform autonomous remediation.

The dashboard is read-only.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_facade import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardFacade,
)

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_service import (
    ActiveProvider,
)


_UNSET = object()


class OperationsCenterDashboardAPI:
    """
    Application-facing API for the Operations Center dashboard.

    The API deliberately contains no business logic.
    It delegates dashboard generation to the facade.

    For compatibility and deterministic testing, callers may
    explicitly supply active providers.

    Calling get_dashboard() without provider input preserves
    the facade's normal provider-resolution behaviour.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        facade: OperationsCenterDashboardFacade,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the dashboard API.
        """

        if facade is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operations Center dashboard facade "
                "cannot be None."
            )

        self._facade = facade

    def get_dashboard(
        self,
        *,
        active_providers: (
            tuple[
                ActiveProvider,
                ...
            ]
            | None
            | object
        ) = _UNSET,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Return the current Operations Center dashboard.

        Resolution rules:

        - omitted:
            delegate to the facade without explicit provider input;

        - explicit tuple:
            delegate using the supplied providers;

        - explicit None:
            reject invalid provider input.

        The API performs no dashboard business logic.
        """

        if active_providers is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Active providers cannot be None."
            )

        if active_providers is _UNSET:
            return self._facade.get_dashboard()

        return self._facade.get_dashboard(
            active_providers=active_providers,
        )

"""EasyChatBot AI Operations Center composition root.

The same ProviderHealthHistoryRepository instance is shared by:
- provider-health observation ingestion;
- provider intelligence analytics.

This keeps dashboard intelligence aligned with the observed
provider-health lifecycle.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_history_service import (
    EscalationHistoryService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.escalation_workflow_repository import (

)
from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_facade import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardFacade,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_service import (
    OperationsCenterDashboardService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_operational_events_api import (
    OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_provider_intelligence_service import (
    OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceService,
)

)
from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_repository import (
    InMemoryOperationalEventRepository,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)

    ProviderHealthApplicationService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_history_repository import (
    ProviderHealthHistoryRepository,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_monitor_service import (
    ProviderHealthMonitorService,
)

    ProviderHealthProbeRunner,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_refresh_service import (
    ProviderHealthRefreshService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_incident_service import (
    InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_operational_event_service import (
    ProviderOperationalEventService,
)
from providers.health.ollama_cloud_health_probe import (
    OllamaCloudHealthProbe,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class OperationsCenterComposition:
    """Composed Operations Center application graph."""

    dashboard_api: OperationsCenterDashboardAPI
    health_application_service: ProviderHealthApplicationService
    operational_event_service: OperationalEventService
    operational_events_api: OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI
    health_refresh_service: ProviderHealthRefreshService | None


def create_operations_center_composition() -> OperationsCenterComposition:
    """Construct the complete Operations Center application graph."""

    health_monitor = ProviderHealthMonitorService()

    # One repository is intentionally shared across health ingestion
    # and provider intelligence.
    provider_health_history_repository = ProviderHealthHistoryRepository()

    provider_intelligence_service = (
        OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceService(
            repository=provider_health_history_repository,
        )
    )

    operational_event_repository = InMemoryOperationalEventRepository()

    operational_event_service = OperationalEventService(
        repository=operational_event_repository,
    )

    provider_operational_event_service = ProviderOperationalEventService(
        operational_event_service=operational_event_service,
    )

    operational_events_api = OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI(
        operational_event_service=operational_event_service,
    )

    incident_repository = InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository()

    incident_service = ProviderIncidentService(
        repository=incident_repository,
        operational_event_service=provider_operational_event_service,
    )

    # IMPORTANT: use the SAME history repository instance here.
    health_application_service = ProviderHealthApplicationService(
        health_monitor=health_monitor,
        incident_service=incident_service,
        history_repository=provider_health_history_repository,
    )

    health_refresh_service = None

    if os.getenv("OLLAMA_API_KEY"):
        ollama_probe = OllamaCloudHealthProbe()

        probe_runner = ProviderHealthProbeRunner(
            health_application_service=health_application_service,
            probes=[ollama_probe],
        )

        health_refresh_service = ProviderHealthRefreshService(
            probe_runner=probe_runner,
        )

    workflow_repository = InMemoryEscalationWorkflowRepository()

    history_service = EscalationHistoryService(
        repository=workflow_repository,
    )

    provider_service = OperationsCenterProviderService(
        health_monitor=health_monitor,
    )

    dashboard_service = OperationsCenterDashboardService(
        history_service=history_service,
        provider_service=provider_service,
        provider_intelligence_service=provider_intelligence_service,
    )

    facade = OperationsCenterDashboardFacade(
        dashboard_service=dashboard_service,
    )

    dashboard_api = OperationsCenterDashboardAPI(
        facade=facade,
    )

    return OperationsCenterComposition(
        dashboard_api=dashboard_api,
        health_application_service=health_application_service,
        operational_event_service=operational_event_service,
        operational_events_api=operational_events_api,
        health_refresh_service=health_refresh_service,
    )


def create_operations_center_dashboard_api() -> OperationsCenterDashboardAPI:
    """Backward-compatible dashboard API composition entry point."""
    return create_operations_center_composition().dashboard_api


__all__ = [
    "OperationsCenterComposition",
    "create_operations_center_composition",
    "create_operations_center_dashboard_api",
]

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center dashboard facade.

Provides a stable application-facing contract for the
Operations Center presentation/API layer.

Architecture:

Provider Health
      |
Escalations
      |
Workflow
      |
History Service
      |
Dashboard Service
      |
      v
OperationsCenterDashboardFacade
      |
      +----------------------+
      |                      |
      v                      v
Provider Mapper       Dashboard Contract
      |
      v
API / Frontend


Responsibilities:

- expose dashboard information to presentation layers;
- convert dashboard read models into stable API output;
- keep frontend/API independent from application internals;
- preserve compatibility with existing consumers.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- calculate intelligence;
- query repositories;
- change Runtime routing;
- approve escalations;
- reject escalations;
- send notifications;
- perform autonomous operational actions.

This is a read-only presentation boundary.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


from typing import Any


from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_service import (
    ActiveProvider,
    OperationsCenterDashboard,
    OperationsCenterDashboardService,
)


from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_provider_intelligence_dashboard_mapper import (
    OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceDashboardMapper,
)



_UNSET = object()



class OperationsCenterDashboardFacade:
    """
    Presentation-facing facade for the AI Operations Center.
    """


    def __init__(
        self,
        dashboard_service: OperationsCenterDashboardService,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the dashboard facade.
        """

        if dashboard_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operations Center dashboard service "
                "cannot be None."
            )


        self._dashboard_service = (
            dashboard_service
        )



    def get_dashboard(
        self,
        *,
        active_providers=(
            _UNSET
        ),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Return a frontend-safe dashboard representation.
        """

        if active_providers is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Active providers cannot be None."
            )


        if active_providers is _UNSET:

            dashboard = (
                self._dashboard_service
                .build_dashboard()
            )

        else:

            dashboard = (
                self._dashboard_service
                .build_dashboard(
                    active_providers=(
                        active_providers
                    )
                )
            )


        return self._serialize_dashboard(
            dashboard
        )



    @staticmethod
    def _serialize_dashboard(
        dashboard: OperationsCenterDashboard,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Convert dashboard read model into
        stable API contract.
        """

        return {

            "system_confidence": (
                dashboard.system_confidence
            ),


            "monitoring_status": (
                dashboard.monitoring_status.value
            ),


            "active_providers": [

                OperationsCenterDashboardFacade
                ._serialize_provider(
                    provider
                )

                for provider

                in dashboard.active_providers
            ],


            "provider_intelligence": (

                OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceDashboardMapper
                .to_dashboard_list(
                    dashboard.provider_intelligence
                )

            ),


            "active_escalations": (
                dashboard.active_escalations
            ),


            "pending_approvals": (
                dashboard.pending_approvals
            ),


            "approved_escalations": (
                dashboard.approved_escalations
            ),


            "rejected_escalations": (
                dashboard.rejected_escalations
            ),


            "pending_notifications": (
                dashboard.pending_notifications
            ),


            "total_escalations": (
                dashboard.total_escalations
            ),
        }



    @staticmethod
    def _serialize_provider(
        provider: ActiveProvider,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Convert provider into existing frontend contract.

        Intentionally minimal to preserve
        backward compatibility.
        """

        return {

            "provider": (
                provider.provider
            ),

            "model": (
                provider.model
            ),

            "capabilities": list(
                provider.capabilities
            ),

            "active": (
                provider.active
            ),
        }



__all__ = [
    "OperationsCenterDashboardFacade",
]

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center dashboard service.

Builds the read-only Operations Center dashboard.

Architecture:

Provider Health
        |
Provider Service
        |
Provider Intelligence Service
        |
OperationsCenterDashboardService

OperationsCenterDashboard

Does NOT:

    MonitoringSummary,
    OperationsCenterDashboard,
    OperationsMetrics,
)


_UNSET = object()



class OperationsCenterDashboardService:
    """
    Application service responsible for dashboard composition.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        history_service: EscalationHistoryService,
        provider_service=None,
        provider_intelligence_service=None,
    ) -> None:

        if history_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Escalation history service cannot be None."
            )


        self._history_service = (
            history_service
        )


        self._provider_service = (
            provider_service
        )


        self._provider_intelligence_service = (
            provider_intelligence_service
        )



    def build_dashboard(
        self,
        *,
        active_providers=_UNSET,
    ) -> OperationsCenterDashboard:
        """
        Build Operations Center dashboard.
        """


        #
        # Provider state
        #

        if active_providers is _UNSET:

            if self._provider_service is None:

                providers = ()

            else:

                providers = (
                    self._provider_service
                    .get_active_providers()
                )

        else:

            if active_providers is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Active providers cannot be None."
                )

            providers = active_providers



        #
        # Provider intelligence
        #

        provider_intelligence = ()


        if self._provider_intelligence_service is not None:

            provider_intelligence = (
                self
                ._provider_intelligence_service
                .get_all_provider_intelligence()
            )



        #
        # Escalation aggregation
        #

        pending_notifications = len(
            self._history_service
            .list_pending_notification()
        )


        pending_approvals = len(
            self._history_service
            .list_pending_approval()
        )


        approved = len(
            self._history_service
            .list_approved()
        )


        rejected = len(
            self._history_service
            .list_rejected()
        )


        total = (
            pending_notifications
            +
            pending_approvals
            +
            approved
            +
            rejected
        )


        active_escalations = (
            pending_notifications
            +
            pending_approvals
        )


        escalation_summary = EscalationSummary(
            active=active_escalations,

            pending=pending_approvals,

            pending_notifications=(
                pending_notifications
            ),

            approved=approved,

            rejected=rejected,

            total=total,
        )



        #
        # Monitoring state
        #

        if active_escalations > 0:

            monitoring = MonitoringSummary(
                status=MonitoringStatus.INCIDENT,
                message=(
                    "Operational attention required"
                ),
            )

        else:

            monitoring = MonitoringSummary(
                status=MonitoringStatus.HEALTHY,
                message=(
                    "Monitoring is healthy"
                ),
            )



        #
        # Confidence calculation
        #

        confidence = (
            100.0
            -
            (
                active_escalations
                *
                15.0
            )
        )



        return OperationsCenterDashboard(

            system_confidence=max(
                0.0,
                confidence,
            ),

            metrics=OperationsMetrics(),

            providers=tuple(
                providers
            ),

            escalations=escalation_summary,

            monitoring=monitoring,

            provider_intelligence=tuple(
                provider_intelligence
            ),
        )



#
# Backward compatibility exports
#

__all__ = [
    "ActiveProvider",
    "OperationsCenterDashboard",
    "OperationsCenterDashboardService",
]

"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center operational events API.

Provides the application-facing read-only boundary for
operational event history.

Architecture:

OperationalEventRepository
        |
        v
OperationalEventService
        |
        v
OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI
        |
        v
HTTP Router / Operations Center UI

Responsibilities:

- expose operational event history;
- delegate retrieval to OperationalEventService;
- convert OperationalEvent objects into stable dictionaries;
- keep presentation/API consumers independent from
  internal event dataclasses and enums.

Does NOT:

- create events;
- modify events;
- execute providers;
- execute agents;
- change Runtime routing;
- approve escalations;
- perform autonomous actions.

Operational events remain immutable facts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)


class OperationsCenterOperationalEventsAPI:
    """
    Read-only application API for operational event history.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        operational_event_service: OperationalEventService,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the operational events API.
        """

        if operational_event_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event service "
                "cannot be None."
            )

        self._operational_event_service = (
            operational_event_service
        )

    def list_events(
        self,
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Return all operational events in a
        presentation-safe representation.
        """

        events = (
            self._operational_event_service
            .list_events()
        )

        return [
            self._serialize_event(event)
            for event in events
        ]

    @staticmethod
    def _serialize_event(
        event: Any,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Convert an OperationalEvent into a stable
        presentation contract.
        """

        return {
            "event_id": str(
                event.event_id
            ),
            "event_type": (
                event.event_type.value
            ),
            "severity": (
                event.severity.value
            ),
            "source": event.source,
            "component": event.component,
            "occurred_at": (
                event.occurred_at.isoformat()
            ),
            "message": event.message,
            "correlation_id": (
                event.correlation_id
            ),
            "metadata": dict(
                event.metadata
            ),
        }

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\operations_center_provider_intelligence_dashboard_mapper.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider intelligence dashboard mapper.

Transforms provider intelligence read models into
dashboard/API-safe representations.

Architecture:

ProviderHealthHistoryRepository
        |
        v
Provider Intelligence Service
        |
        v
ProviderIntelligence
        |
        v
OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceDashboardMapper
        |
        v
Dashboard API


Responsibilities:

- convert ProviderIntelligence read models;
- provide stable dashboard contract;
- isolate presentation formatting from services;
- support future dashboard evolution.

Does NOT:

- calculate intelligence;
- query repositories;
- execute providers;
- modify provider health state;
- perform routing;
- create incidents.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_provider_intelligence_service import (
    ProviderIntelligence,
)



class OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceDashboardMapper:
    """
    Maps provider intelligence read models into
    dashboard-safe dictionaries.
    """


    @staticmethod
    def to_dashboard_model(
        intelligence: ProviderIntelligence,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Convert one ProviderIntelligence object into
        dashboard representation.
        """

        if intelligence is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider intelligence cannot be None."
            )


        return {
            "provider": (
                intelligence.provider
            ),

            "model": (
                intelligence.model
            ),

            "capability": (
                intelligence.capability
            ),

            "availability_percentage": (
                intelligence.availability_percentage
            ),

            "average_latency_ms": (
                intelligence.average_latency_ms
            ),

            "total_checks": (
                intelligence.total_checks
            ),

            "successful_checks": (
                intelligence.successful_checks
            ),

            "failed_checks": (
                intelligence.failed_checks
            ),

            "health_trend": (
                intelligence.health_trend
            ),
        }



    @classmethod
    def to_dashboard_list(
        cls,
        intelligence_items: (
            tuple[
                ProviderIntelligence,
                ...
            ]
        ),
    ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        Convert multiple provider intelligence models.
        """

        if intelligence_items is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider intelligence collection "
                "cannot be None."
            )


        return [
            cls.to_dashboard_model(
                item
            )

            for item

            in intelligence_items
        ]

    "OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceDashboardMapper",
