===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\operations_center_provider_intelligence_service.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider intelligence service.

Transforms provider health history into dashboard-safe
operational intelligence.

Architecture:

ProviderHealthHistoryRepository
        |
        v
OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceService
        |
        v
Provider Intelligence Read Model
        |
        v
Operations Center Dashboard


Responsibilities:

- calculate provider availability;
- calculate latency statistics;
- identify provider health trends;
- expose read-only intelligence.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- call external APIs;
- change Runtime routing;
- perform failover;
- approve operational actions.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_history_repository import (
    ProviderHealthHistoryRepository,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthStatus,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_intelligence_models import (
    ProviderIntelligence,
)


class OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceService:
    """
    Calculates provider intelligence from health history.
    """


    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        repository: ProviderHealthHistoryRepository,
    ) -> None:

        if repository is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health history repository cannot be None."
            )

        self._repository = repository


    def get_provider_intelligence(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> ProviderIntelligence:
        """
        Calculate intelligence for one provider.
        """

        if not provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )


        observations = (
            self._repository
            .get_by_provider(provider)
        )


        if not observations:
            raise ValueError(
                f"No health observations found for {provider}."
            )


        total_checks = len(
            observations
        )


        successful_checks = len(
            [
                item
                for item in observations
                if self._is_healthy(
                    item.status
                )
            ]
        )


        failed_checks = (
            total_checks
            -
            successful_checks
        )


        availability = (
            successful_checks
            /
            total_checks
            *
            100
        )


        latencies = [
            item.latency_ms
            for item in observations
            if item.latency_ms is not None
        ]


        average_latency = None

        if latencies:

            average_latency = (
                sum(latencies)
                /
                len(latencies)
            )


        latest = observations[-1]


        return ProviderIntelligence(
            provider=latest.provider,

            model=latest.model,

            capability=latest.capability,

            availability_percentage=round(
                availability,
                2,
            ),

            average_latency_ms=(
                round(
                    average_latency,
                    2,
                )
                if average_latency is not None
                else None
            ),

            total_checks=total_checks,

            successful_checks=successful_checks,

            failed_checks=failed_checks,

            health_trend=(
                self._calculate_trend(
                    latest.status
                )
            ),
        )


    def get_all_provider_intelligence(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[ProviderIntelligence, ...]:
        """
        Return intelligence for all known providers.
        """

        providers = {
            item.provider
            for item
            in self._repository.get_all()
        }


        return tuple(
            self.get_provider_intelligence(
                provider
            )
            for provider
            in providers
        )


    @staticmethod
    def _is_healthy(
        status,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Support enum and string health states.
        """

        value = (
            status.value
            if hasattr(
                status,
                "value",
            )
            else str(status)
        )

        return (
            value
            ==
            ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY.value
        )


    @staticmethod
    def _calculate_trend(
        status,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Convert health status into dashboard trend.
        """

        value = (
            status.value
            if hasattr(
                status,
                "value",
            )
            else str(status)
        )


        if value == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY.value:
            return "stable"


        if value == ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED.value:
            return "degrading"


        if value == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE.value:
            return "incident"


        return "unknown"


__all__ = [
    "OperationsCenterProviderIntelligenceService",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\operations_center_provider_service.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider service.

Bridges provider health state into the Operations
Center dashboard provider read model.

Architecture:

Provider Health Monitor
        |
        v
ProviderHealthReadModel
        |
        v
OperationsCenterProviderService
        |
        v
ActiveProvider
        |
        v
Operations Center Dashboard


Responsibilities:

- expose dashboard-safe provider information;
- translate health state into UI fields;
- preserve provider health separation.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- perform health probes;
- change Runtime routing;
- perform remediation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


from app.application.provider_management.operations_center_dashboard_models import (
    ActiveProvider,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_monitor_service import (
    ProviderHealthMonitorService,
)


class OperationsCenterProviderService:
    """
    Provides provider information for Operations Center.
    """


    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        health_monitor: ProviderHealthMonitorService,
    ) -> None:

        if health_monitor is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health monitor cannot be None."
            )

        self._health_monitor = health_monitor


    def get_active_providers(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[ActiveProvider, ...]:
        """
        Return dashboard provider information.

        Consumes ProviderHealthReadModel only.
        """

        health_records = (
            self._health_monitor
            .get_all_provider_health()
        )


        providers: list[ActiveProvider] = []


        for record in health_records:

            if record.model is None:
                continue


            capabilities = ()

            if record.capability:
                capabilities = (
                    record.capability,
                )


            status_value = (
                record.status.value
                if hasattr(
                    record.status,
                    "value",
                )
                else str(
                    record.status
                )
            )


            providers.append(
                ActiveProvider(
                    provider=record.provider,

                    model=record.model,

                    capabilities=capabilities,

                    active=(
                        status_value
                        != "unavailable"
                    ),

                    health_status=status_value,

                    # ProviderHealthReadModel does not
                    # expose latency or counters.
                    # Keep dashboard contract safe.
                    latency_ms=None,

                    consecutive_failures=0,

                    consecutive_successes=0,

                    current_incident=(
                        status_value
                        == "unavailable"
                    ),
                )
            )


        return tuple(providers)


__all__ = [
    "OperationsCenterProviderService",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_application_service.py =====
"""EasyChatBot provider health application service."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_monitor_service import (
    ProviderHealthMonitorService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_incident_service import (
    ProviderIncident,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_history_repository import (
    ProviderHealthHistoryRepository,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthApplicationResult:
    """Stable result returned after processing one observation."""

    provider: str
    model: str
    capability: str
    status: ProviderHealthStatus
    transition: ProviderHealthTransition
    incident: ProviderIncident | None


class ProviderHealthApplicationService:
    """Controlled application boundary for provider-health ingestion."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        health_monitor: ProviderHealthMonitorService,
        incident_service: ProviderIncidentService,
        history_repository: ProviderHealthHistoryRepository | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if health_monitor is None:
            raise ValueError("Provider health monitor cannot be None.")
        if incident_service is None:
            raise ValueError("Provider incident service cannot be None.")

        self._health_monitor = health_monitor
        self._incident_service = incident_service
        self._history_repository = (
            history_repository
            if history_repository is not None
            else ProviderHealthHistoryRepository()
        )

    def observe(
        self,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
    ) -> ProviderHealthApplicationResult:
        """Persist, calculate, and process one provider-health observation."""

        if observation is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health observation cannot be None."
            )

        self._history_repository.save(observation)

        transition = self._health_monitor.observe(observation)

        incident = self._incident_service.process_transition(
            transition
        )

        return ProviderHealthApplicationResult(
            provider=observation.provider,
            model=observation.model,
            capability=observation.capability,
            status=observation.status,
            transition=transition.transition,
            incident=incident,
        )


__all__ = [
    "ProviderHealthApplicationResult",
    "ProviderHealthApplicationService",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_composition.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot provider health composition.

Builds the provider-health application graph used by the
Operations Center.

Composition only:

    OllamaCloudHealthProbe
            |
            v
    ProviderHealthProbeRunner
            |
            v
    ProviderHealthApplicationService
            |
            +-----------------------------+
            |                             |
            v                             v
    ProviderHealthMonitorService   ProviderIncidentService
            |                             |
            v                             v
    ProviderHealthReadModel        Incident lifecycle

This module does NOT:

- implement provider-specific health logic;
- call provider APIs directly;
- calculate provider health;
- render dashboard data;
- modify Runtime routing;
- perform autonomous failover;
- perform autonomous remediation.

Provider-specific probes are injected into the generic
ProviderHealthProbeRunner.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_application_service import (
    ProviderHealthApplicationService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_monitor_service import (
    ProviderHealthMonitorService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_probe_runner import (
    ProviderHealthProbeRunner,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_incident_service import (
    InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository,
    ProviderIncidentService,
)
from providers.health.ollama_cloud_health_probe import (
    OllamaCloudHealthProbe,
)


DEFAULT_OLLAMA_CLOUD_MODEL = "gpt-oss:120b"


def create_provider_health_probe_runner(
    *,
    ollama_api_key: str | None = None,
    ollama_model: str | None = None,
    ollama_timeout_seconds: float = 10.0,
    health_monitor: ProviderHealthMonitorService | None = None,
    incident_service: ProviderIncidentService | None = None,
) -> ProviderHealthProbeRunner:
    """
    Build the provider-health runner for the Operations Center.

    Configuration is supplied explicitly where possible.
    Environment variables are used only at this composition
    boundary for the provider credential and model selection.

    The provider API key never leaves this backend composition
    boundary and is never exposed to the frontend.
    """

    api_key = (
        ollama_api_key
        if ollama_api_key is not None
        else os.getenv("OLLAMA_API_KEY", "")
    )

    model = (
        ollama_model
        if ollama_model is not None
        else os.getenv(
            "OLLAMA_CLOUD_MODEL",
            DEFAULT_OLLAMA_CLOUD_MODEL,
        )
    )

    if not api_key.strip():
        raise ValueError(
            "OLLAMA_API_KEY is required to configure "
            "the Ollama Cloud provider health probe."
        )

    if not model.strip():
        raise ValueError(
            "Ollama Cloud model cannot be empty."
        )

    if ollama_timeout_seconds <= 0:
        raise ValueError(
            "Ollama Cloud timeout must be positive."
        )

    monitor = (
        health_monitor
        if health_monitor is not None
        else ProviderHealthMonitorService()
    )

    incidents = (
        incident_service
        if incident_service is not None
        else ProviderIncidentService(
            repository=InMemoryProviderIncidentRepository(),
        )
    )

    application_service = ProviderHealthApplicationService(
        health_monitor=monitor,
        incident_service=incidents,
    )

    ollama_probe = OllamaCloudHealthProbe(
        api_key=api_key,
        model=model,
        timeout_seconds=ollama_timeout_seconds,
    )

    return ProviderHealthProbeRunner(
        health_application_service=application_service,
        probes=(ollama_probe,),
    )


__all__ = [
    "DEFAULT_OLLAMA_CLOUD_MODEL",
    "create_provider_health_probe_runner",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_escalation_service.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health escalation service.

Bridges provider availability incidents into the existing
EscalationService.

Architecture:

Provider Health Service
        |
        v
Provider Incident Service
        |
        v
ProviderHealthEscalationService
        |
        v
EscalationService
        |
        v
EscalationRepository
        |
        v
AI Operations Center


Responsibilities:

- identify provider outage incidents that require escalation;
- translate provider incidents into operational escalations;
- determine escalation severity;
- preserve provider/capability information;
- create escalations through the existing EscalationService.

Does NOT:

- send emails;
- approve escalations;
- change Runtime routing;
- execute providers;
- perform autonomous failover;
- directly persist escalation records.

Human approval remains mandatory.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.provider_management.escalation_service import (
    EscalationService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.models import (
    EscalationSeverity,
    EscalationType,
    ProviderEscalation,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderIncident,
)


class ProviderHealthEscalationService:
    """
    Converts sustained provider outages into operational
    escalation records.

    The service deliberately sits between incident
    detection and notification.

    Example:

        OpenAI unavailable
                |
                v
        ProviderIncident
                |
                v
        ProviderHealthEscalationService
                |
                v
        HIGH / CRITICAL escalation
                |
                v
        Human review
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        escalation_service: EscalationService,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the health escalation service.
        """

        if escalation_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Escalation service cannot be None."
            )

        self._escalation_service = (
            escalation_service
        )

    def escalate_incident(
        self,
        *,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> ProviderEscalation | None:
        """
        Create an escalation for a provider outage.

        Only unavailable provider incidents are escalated.

        Already recovered incidents are not escalated as
        active outages.

        The escalation remains advisory and requires human
        review before any operational change.
        """

        if incident is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider incident cannot be None."
            )

        if not incident.provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )

        if (
            incident.status
            != ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
        ):
            return None

        severity = self._determine_severity(
            incident
        )

        capability = (
            self._format_capability_scope(
                incident
            )
        )

        message = (
            self._build_message(
                incident
            )
        )

        recommendation = (
            self._build_recommendation(
                incident
            )
        )

        return (
            self._escalation_service
            .create_escalation(
                escalation_type=(
                    EscalationType.PROVIDER_FAILURE
                ),
                severity=severity,
                provider=incident.provider,
                capability=capability,
                message=message,
                recommendation=(
                    recommendation
                ),
            )
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _determine_severity(
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> EscalationSeverity:
        """
        Determine outage escalation severity.

        Hackathon policy:

        - 3-4 consecutive failures:
          HIGH

        - 5+ consecutive failures:
          CRITICAL

        The policy is intentionally conservative:
        the system recommends escalation but never
        performs the operational action itself.
        """

        if incident.consecutive_failures >= 5:
            return EscalationSeverity.CRITICAL

        return EscalationSeverity.HIGH

    @staticmethod
    def _format_capability_scope(
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Represent the affected capability scope.
        """

        if not incident.affected_capabilities:
            return "provider-wide"

        return ", ".join(
            incident.affected_capabilities
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_message(
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build an Operations Center escalation message.
        """

        capability_scope = (
            ProviderHealthEscalationService
            ._format_capability_scope(
                incident
            )
        )

        return (
            f"Provider {incident.provider} is "
            f"currently unavailable. "
            f"Affected capabilities: "
            f"{capability_scope}. "
            f"Consecutive health failures: "
            f"{incident.consecutive_failures}. "
            f"Incident started at "
            f"{incident.started_at.isoformat()}."
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _build_recommendation(
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Build an advisory recommendation.

        No routing change is executed.
        """

        capability_scope = (
            ProviderHealthEscalationService
            ._format_capability_scope(
                incident
            )
        )

        return (
            f"Review alternative provider options "
            f"for affected capabilities "
            f"({capability_scope}) and keep the "
            f"current provider change under human "
            f"approval until recovery is confirmed."
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_history_repository.py =====
"""Provider health history repository."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
)


class ProviderHealthHistoryRepository:
    """In-memory provider-health observation repository for Beta."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._observations: dict[
            str, list[ProviderHealthObservation]
        ] = defaultdict(list)

    def save(self, observation: ProviderHealthObservation) -> None:
        if observation is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health observation cannot be None."
            )
        self._observations[observation.provider].append(observation)

    def get_all(self) -> tuple[ProviderHealthObservation, ...]:
        result: list[ProviderHealthObservation] = []
        for observations in self._observations.values():
            result.extend(observations)
        return tuple(result)

    def get_by_provider(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> tuple[ProviderHealthObservation, ...]:
        if not provider.strip():
            raise ValueError("Provider cannot be empty.")
        return tuple(self._observations.get(provider, []))

    def latest(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> ProviderHealthObservation | None:
        observations = self.get_by_provider(provider)
        if not observations:
            return None
        return observations[-1]

    def count(self) -> int:
        return sum(
            len(items)
            for items in self._observations.values()
        )


__all__ = ["ProviderHealthHistoryRepository"]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_models.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health models.

Defines the domain contracts used to monitor AI service
provider availability and operational health.

Architecture:

Provider Health Probe
        |
        v
ProviderHealthObservation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        v
ProviderIncidentService
        |
        v
Escalation Service
        |
        v
AI Operations Center


Responsibilities:

- represent provider health states;
- represent individual health observations;
- represent provider outage information;
- represent health transitions;
- provide immutable domain contracts.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- perform health probes;
- persist data;
- send emails;
- modify Runtime routing;
- create autonomous failover.

Human approval remains mandatory for operational changes.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime
from enum import Enum


class ProviderHealthStatus(
    str,
    Enum,
):
    """
    Current operational health state of an AI provider.
    """

    HEALTHY = "healthy"

    DEGRADED = "degraded"

    UNAVAILABLE = "unavailable"

    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


class ProviderHealthTransition(
    str,
    Enum,
):
    """
    Significant transition in provider health.
    """

    RECOVERED = "recovered"

    DEGRADED = "degraded"

    UNAVAILABLE = "unavailable"

    UNKNOWN = "unknown"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthObservation:
    """
    Immutable observation produced by a provider health
    probe or equivalent Runtime health signal.

    Represents one point-in-time observation.

    Additional intelligence fields are optional to preserve
    backward compatibility with existing probes and tests.
    """

    provider: str

    model: str | None

    capability: str | None

    status: ProviderHealthStatus

    observed_at: datetime

    latency_ms: int | None = None

    error_type: str | None = None

    error_message: str | None = None

    consecutive_failures: int = 0

    consecutive_successes: int = 0

    #
    # Provider Intelligence fields
    #

    last_success_at: datetime | None = None

    last_failure_at: datetime | None = None

    average_latency_ms: float | None = None

    availability_percentage: float | None = None

    total_checks: int = 0

    successful_checks: int = 0

    failed_checks: int = 0


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthState:
    """
    Current calculated health state of a provider.

    Represents operational state derived from one or more
    health observations.

    It does not represent historical incident storage.
    """

    provider: str

    status: ProviderHealthStatus

    checked_at: datetime

    consecutive_failures: int

    consecutive_successes: int

    last_success_at: datetime | None = None

    last_failure_at: datetime | None = None

    degraded_since: datetime | None = None

    unavailable_since: datetime | None = None

    average_latency_ms: float | None = None

    availability_percentage: float | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderIncident:
    """
    Provider availability incident.

    Represents a sustained operational problem,
    not an individual failed request.
    """

    incident_id: str

    provider: str

    status: ProviderHealthStatus

    started_at: datetime

    last_observed_at: datetime

    recovered_at: datetime | None = None

    affected_capabilities: tuple[
        str,
        ...,
    ] = ()

    consecutive_failures: int = 0

    resolution_message: str | None = None


    @property
    def is_open(self) -> bool:
        """
        Return whether the provider incident remains open.
        """

        return self.recovered_at is None


    @property
    def downtime_seconds(self) -> float | None:
        """
        Return completed downtime in seconds.
        """

        if self.recovered_at is None:
            return None

        return (
            self.recovered_at
            -
            self.started_at
        ).total_seconds()


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthTransitionEvent:
    """
    Significant provider health transition.

    Used by application services to determine whether an
    incident should be created, updated, recovered, or
    escalated.
    """

    provider: str

    transition: ProviderHealthTransition

    previous_status: ProviderHealthStatus

    current_status: ProviderHealthStatus

    occurred_at: datetime

    consecutive_failures: int

    consecutive_successes: int

    affected_capabilities: tuple[
        str,
        ...,
    ] = ()

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_monitor_service.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health monitor.

Coordinates provider health observations and their
read-only operational projections.

Architecture:

Provider Health Observation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        v
ProviderHealthMonitorService
        |
        v
ProviderHealthReadModel
        |
        v
Operations Center


Responsibilities:

- accept provider health observations;
- delegate health calculation to ProviderHealthService;
- retain the latest observation metadata;
- expose dashboard-safe provider health read models;
- expose all currently observed providers.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- perform health probes;
- persist incidents;
- send emails;
- change Runtime routing;
- approve recommendations;
- perform autonomous failover.

ProviderHealthService remains the authority for health
state calculation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthState,
    ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_read_model import (
    ProviderHealthReadModel,
    ProviderHealthReadModelService,
)

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_service import (
    ProviderHealthService,
)


class ProviderHealthMonitorService:
    """
    Coordinates provider health state and operational
    read-model generation.

    The service deliberately keeps the latest observation
    separately from calculated health state because the
    current ProviderHealthState contract does not contain
    model or capability metadata.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        health_service: ProviderHealthService | None = None,
        read_model_service: (
            ProviderHealthReadModelService | None
        ) = None,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the provider health monitor.
        """

        self._health_service = (
            health_service
            if health_service is not None
            else ProviderHealthService()
        )

        self._read_model_service = (
            read_model_service
            if read_model_service is not None
            else ProviderHealthReadModelService()
        )

        self._latest_observations: dict[
            str,
            ProviderHealthObservation,
        ] = {}

    def observe(
        self,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
    ) -> ProviderHealthTransitionEvent:
        """
        Process a provider health observation.

        Health calculation remains delegated to
        ProviderHealthService.
        """

        if observation is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health observation cannot be None."
            )

        transition = self._health_service.observe(
            observation
        )

        self._latest_observations[
            observation.provider
        ] = observation

        return transition

    def get_provider_health(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> ProviderHealthReadModel | None:
        """
        Return the current operational health projection
        for one provider.
        """

        if not provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )

        state = self._health_service.get_state(
            provider
        )

        observation = self._latest_observations.get(
            provider
        )

        if (
            state is None
            or observation is None
        ):
            return None

        return self._read_model_service.build(
            observation=observation,
            state=state,
        )

    def get_all_provider_health(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[
        ProviderHealthReadModel,
        ...
    ]:
        """
        Return operational health projections for all
        observed providers.

        Results are deterministic and sorted by provider.
        """

        results: list[
            ProviderHealthReadModel
        ] = []

        for state in (
            self._health_service.get_all_states()
        ):
            observation = (
                self._latest_observations.get(
                    state.provider
                )
            )

            if observation is None:
                continue

            results.append(
                self._read_model_service.build(
                    observation=observation,
                    state=state,
                )
            )

        return tuple(
            sorted(
                results,
                key=lambda item: item.provider,
            )
        )

    def clear(self) -> None:
        """
        Clear tracked health state and observations.

        Intended for isolated tests and demonstrations.
        """

        self._health_service.clear()

        self._latest_observations.clear()

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_probe.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health probe contract.

Defines the application-facing contract for provider health
observations.

Architecture:

Provider-specific Health Probe
        |
        v
ProviderHealthProbe
        |
        v
ProviderHealthObservation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthApplicationService
        |
        v
Operations Center

Responsibilities:

- define the contract implemented by provider health probes;
- expose provider/model/capability identity;
- produce one immutable health observation;
- keep provider-specific implementation details outside
  the application layer.

Does NOT:

- update the dashboard;
- create incidents;
- create escalations;
- modify Runtime routing;
- perform autonomous failover;
- expose provider SDK objects.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Protocol

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
)


class ProviderHealthProbe(Protocol):
    """
    Provider-independent health probe contract.

    Concrete implementations belong outside the application
    orchestration layer.

    Examples:

        OpenAIHealthProbe
        GeminiHealthProbe
        ParallelHealthProbe
        OllamaHealthProbe
    """

    @property
    def provider(self) -> str:
        """Return the stable provider identifier."""
        ...

    @property
    def model(self) -> str | None:
        """Return the model being health-checked."""
        ...

    @property
    def capability(self) -> str | None:
        """Return the capability being health-checked."""
        ...

    async def check(self) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
        """
        Execute one provider health observation.

        Implementations must return a provider-independent
        ProviderHealthObservation.

        They must not return provider SDK objects.
        """
        ...

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_probe_runner.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health probe runner.

Coordinates provider-independent health probes with the
existing ProviderHealthApplicationService.

Architecture:

ProviderHealthProbe
        |
        v
ProviderHealthProbeRunner
        |
        v
ProviderHealthApplicationService
        |
        v
ProviderHealthMonitorService
        |
        v
Operations Center

The runner is orchestration only.

It does NOT:

- implement provider-specific API calls;
- calculate health state;
- create incidents directly;
- modify Runtime routing;
- select alternative providers;
- perform autonomous remediation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from time import perf_counter
from typing import Iterable

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_application_service import (
    ProviderHealthApplicationResult,
    ProviderHealthApplicationService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_probe import (
    ProviderHealthProbe,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthProbeResult:
    """
    Result of processing one provider health probe.

    Contains both the raw provider-independent observation
    and the application result produced by the existing
    health application boundary.
    """

    observation: ProviderHealthObservation

    application_result: ProviderHealthApplicationResult


class ProviderHealthProbeRunner:
    """
    Coordinates provider health probes.

    The runner receives probes through dependency injection.
    It never constructs provider-specific clients itself.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        health_application_service: (
            ProviderHealthApplicationService
        ),
        probes: Iterable[ProviderHealthProbe],
    ) -> None:
        """
        Initialise the probe runner.
        """

        if health_application_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health application service "
                "cannot be None."
            )

        if probes is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health probes cannot be None."
            )

        self._health_application_service = (
            health_application_service
        )

        self._probes = tuple(probes)

        self._validate_probes()

    async def run_probe(
        self,
        probe: ProviderHealthProbe,
    ) -> ProviderHealthProbeResult:
        """
        Execute one health probe and submit its observation
        through the existing application boundary.
        """

        if probe is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health probe cannot be None."
            )

        started = perf_counter()

        try:
            observation = await probe.check()

        except Exception as exc:
            elapsed_ms = int(
                round(
                    (perf_counter() - started)
                    * 1000
                )
            )

            observation = (
                self._build_failure_observation(
                    probe=probe,
                    latency_ms=elapsed_ms,
                    error=exc,
                )
            )

        if observation.provider != probe.provider:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health probe returned an "
                "observation for a different provider."
            )

        result = (
            self._health_application_service.observe(
                observation
            )
        )

        return ProviderHealthProbeResult(
            observation=observation,
            application_result=result,
        )

    async def run_all(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[ProviderHealthProbeResult, ...]:
        """
        Execute all configured probes.

        Probes are executed sequentially for deterministic
        application behaviour and predictable provider load.

        Parallel execution can be introduced later only if
        measurement demonstrates that it is required.
        """

        results: list[
            ProviderHealthProbeResult
        ] = []

        for probe in self._probes:
            results.append(
                await self.run_probe(probe)
            )

        return tuple(results)

    def _validate_probes(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate configured probe identities.
        """

        providers: set[str] = set()

        for probe in self._probes:
            if probe is None:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Provider health probes cannot contain None."
                )

            provider = probe.provider.strip()

            if not provider:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Provider health probe provider "
                    "cannot be empty."
                )

            if provider in providers:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Duplicate provider health probe "
                    f"configured: {provider}."
                )

            providers.add(provider)

    @staticmethod
    def _build_failure_observation(
        *,
        probe: ProviderHealthProbe,
        latency_ms: int,
        error: Exception,
    ) -> ProviderHealthObservation:
        """
        Convert a probe exception into a provider-independent
        unavailable observation.

        Provider-specific exceptions do not cross the
        application boundary.
        """

        now = datetime.now(
            timezone.utc
        )

        return ProviderHealthObservation(
            provider=probe.provider,
            model=probe.model,
            capability=probe.capability,
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE,
            observed_at=now,
            latency_ms=max(
                latency_ms,
                0,
            ),
            error_type=type(error).__name__,
            error_message=str(error),
            consecutive_failures=1,
            consecutive_successes=0,
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_read_model.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health read model.

Provides a presentation-independent operational read model
for provider health information.

Architecture:

Provider Health Observation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        v
Provider Health Read Model
        |
        +-------------------+
        |                   |
        v                   v
Operations Center       Future Agent
Dashboard              Operations
        |                   |
        +---------+---------+
                  |
                  v
          Operational Intelligence


Responsibilities:

- expose provider health information in a dashboard-safe
  read model;
- retain provider/model/capability attribution;
- expose current health state;
- expose failure and recovery timing information;
- remain read-only.

Does NOT:

- execute providers;
- perform health probes;
- change Runtime routing;
- create incidents;
- send emails;
- approve recommendations;
- perform autonomous failover.

This module is an operational read model, not a second
health-management engine.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthState,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProviderHealthReadModel:
    """
    Dashboard-safe provider health representation.

    Model and capability attribution are retained from the
    latest provider health observation while operational
    health state comes from ProviderHealthService.
    """

    provider: str

    model: str | None

    capability: str | None

    status: str

    checked_at: datetime

    consecutive_failures: int

    consecutive_successes: int

    last_success_at: datetime | None

    last_failure_at: datetime | None

    degraded_since: datetime | None

    unavailable_since: datetime | None

    active: bool = True


class ProviderHealthReadModelService:
    """
    Builds read-only provider health projections.

    This service does not calculate provider health itself.
    Health state remains owned by ProviderHealthService.
    """

    def build(
        self,
        *,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
        state: ProviderHealthState,
    ) -> ProviderHealthReadModel:
        """
        Build a provider health read model from the latest
        observation and calculated health state.
        """

        if observation is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health observation cannot be None."
            )

        if state is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health state cannot be None."
            )

        if (
            observation.provider
            != state.provider
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Observation provider and health state "
                "provider must match."
            )

        return ProviderHealthReadModel(
            provider=state.provider,
            model=observation.model,
            capability=observation.capability,
            status=state.status.value,
            checked_at=state.checked_at,
            consecutive_failures=(
                state.consecutive_failures
            ),
            consecutive_successes=(
                state.consecutive_successes
            ),
            last_success_at=(
                state.last_success_at
            ),
            last_failure_at=(
                state.last_failure_at
            ),
            degraded_since=(
                state.degraded_since
            ),
            unavailable_since=(
                state.unavailable_since
            ),
            active=True,
        )

"""
EasyChatBot provider health refresh service.

Coordinates one live provider-health refresh cycle.

The service delegates provider execution to the existing
ProviderHealthProbeRunner and does not contain provider-specific
logic or dashboard rendering.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_probe_runner import (
    ProviderHealthProbeResult,
    ProviderHealthProbeRunner,
)


class ProviderHealthRefreshService:
    """
    Application service for one live provider-health refresh.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        probe_runner: ProviderHealthProbeRunner,
    ) -> None:
        if probe_runner is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health probe runner "
                "cannot be None."
            )

        self._probe_runner = probe_runner

    async def refresh(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[ProviderHealthProbeResult, ...]:
        """
        Execute one refresh cycle across all configured probes.

        Provider execution and observation processing remain
        owned by ProviderHealthProbeRunner.
        """

        return await self._probe_runner.run_all()

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_health_service.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider health service.

Transforms provider health observations into the current
provider health state and significant health transitions.

Architecture:

Provider Health Probe
        |
        v
ProviderHealthObservation
        |
        v
ProviderHealthService
        |
        +--------------------+
        |                    |
        v                    v
ProviderHealthState   HealthTransitionEvent
        |                    |
        +---------+----------+
                  |
                  v
        ProviderIncidentService
                  |
                  v
           EscalationService


Responsibilities:

- process provider health observations;
- maintain provider health state;
- track consecutive failures;
- track consecutive successes;
- detect degradation;
- detect unavailability;
- detect recovery;
- expose the current provider health state.

Does NOT:

- execute health probes;
- persist incidents;
- send emails;
- modify Runtime routing;
- perform autonomous failover.

The service is an application-layer health state
processor. Incident persistence and escalation remain
separate responsibilities.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import replace

from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderHealthObservation,
    ProviderHealthState,
    ProviderHealthStatus,
    ProviderHealthTransition,
    ProviderHealthTransitionEvent,
)


class ProviderHealthService:
    """
    Application service responsible for provider health
    state tracking.

    Health policy for the Hackathon:

    1. First failure:
       DEGRADED

    2. Three consecutive failures:
       UNAVAILABLE

    3. Successful observation after degradation:
       HEALTHY

    4. Successful observation after unavailability:
       HEALTHY / RECOVERED transition

    The thresholds are intentionally kept in the service
    for the Hackathon and can later become validated
    configuration.
    """

    DEGRADED_FAILURE_THRESHOLD = 1

    UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD = 3

    RECOVERY_SUCCESS_THRESHOLD = 2

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        """
        Initialise an empty provider health registry.
        """

        self._states: dict[
            str,
            ProviderHealthState,
        ] = {}

    def observe(
        self,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
    ) -> ProviderHealthTransitionEvent:
        """
        Process one provider health observation.

        Returns the health transition generated by the
        observation.

        The method updates only application-level health
        state. It does not create incidents or external
        side effects.
        """

        if observation is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider health observation cannot be None."
            )

        self._validate_observation(
            observation
        )

        previous_state = self._states.get(
            observation.provider
        )

        next_state = self._calculate_next_state(
            observation=observation,
            previous_state=previous_state,
        )

        self._states[
            observation.provider
        ] = next_state

        transition = (
            self._determine_transition(
                previous_state=previous_state,
                current_state=next_state,
            )
        )

        return ProviderHealthTransitionEvent(
            provider=observation.provider,
            transition=transition,
            previous_status=(
                previous_state.status
                if previous_state is not None
                else ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN
            ),
            current_status=next_state.status,
            occurred_at=observation.observed_at,
            consecutive_failures=(
                next_state.consecutive_failures
            ),
            consecutive_successes=(
                next_state.consecutive_successes
            ),
            affected_capabilities=(
                (observation.capability,)
                if observation.capability
                else ()
            ),
        )

    def get_state(
        self,
        provider: str,
    ) -> ProviderHealthState | None:
        """
        Return the current state of a provider.

        Returns None when the provider has not yet been
        observed.
        """

        if not provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )

        return self._states.get(
            provider
        )

    def get_all_states(
        self,
    ) -> tuple[ProviderHealthState, ...]:
        """
        Return current health states for all observed
        providers.
        """

        return tuple(
            self._states[
                provider
            ]
            for provider in sorted(
                self._states
            )
        )

    def clear(
        self,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Clear tracked health state.

        Intended primarily for tests and isolated
        demonstrations.
        """

        self._states.clear()

    def _calculate_next_state(
        self,
        *,
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
        previous_state: ProviderHealthState | None,
    ) -> ProviderHealthState:
        """
        Calculate the next provider health state.
        """

        if observation.status == (
            ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
        ):
            consecutive_failures = (
                observation.consecutive_failures
                if observation.consecutive_failures > 0
                else (
                    (
                        previous_state.consecutive_failures
                        if previous_state is not None
                        else 0
                    )
                    + 1
                )
            )

            return ProviderHealthState(
                provider=observation.provider,
                status=(
                    ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
                    if consecutive_failures
                    >= self.UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
                    else ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
                ),
                checked_at=observation.observed_at,
                consecutive_failures=(
                    consecutive_failures
                ),
                consecutive_successes=0,
                last_success_at=(
                    previous_state.last_success_at
                    if previous_state is not None
                    else None
                ),
                last_failure_at=(
                    observation.observed_at
                ),
                degraded_since=(
                    previous_state.degraded_since
                    if (
                        previous_state is not None
                        and previous_state.degraded_since
                        is not None
                    )
                    else observation.observed_at
                ),
                unavailable_since=(
                    (
                        previous_state.unavailable_since
                        if previous_state is not None
                        and previous_state.unavailable_since
                        is not None
                        else observation.observed_at
                    )
                    if consecutive_failures
                    >= self.UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
                    else None
                ),
            )

        if observation.status == (
            ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
        ):
            consecutive_failures = (
                observation.consecutive_failures
                if observation.consecutive_failures > 0
                else (
                    (
                        previous_state.consecutive_failures
                        if previous_state is not None
                        else 0
                    )
                    + 1
                )
            )

            return ProviderHealthState(
                provider=observation.provider,
                status=(
                    ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
                    if consecutive_failures
                    >= self.UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
                    else ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
                ),
                checked_at=observation.observed_at,
                consecutive_failures=(
                    consecutive_failures
                ),
                consecutive_successes=0,
                last_success_at=(
                    previous_state.last_success_at
                    if previous_state is not None
                    else None
                ),
                last_failure_at=(
                    observation.observed_at
                ),
                degraded_since=(
                    previous_state.degraded_since
                    if (
                        previous_state is not None
                        and previous_state.degraded_since
                        is not None
                    )
                    else observation.observed_at
                ),
                unavailable_since=(
                    (
                        previous_state.unavailable_since
                        if previous_state is not None
                        and previous_state.unavailable_since
                        is not None
                        else observation.observed_at
                    )
                    if consecutive_failures
                    >= self.UNAVAILABLE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD
                    else None
                ),
            )

        if observation.status == (
            ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
        ):
            consecutive_successes = (
                observation.consecutive_successes
                if observation.consecutive_successes > 0
                else (
                    (
                        previous_state.consecutive_successes
                        if previous_state is not None
                        else 0
                    )
                    + 1
                )
            )

            return ProviderHealthState(
                provider=observation.provider,
                status=ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY,
                checked_at=observation.observed_at,
                consecutive_failures=0,
                consecutive_successes=(
                    consecutive_successes
                ),
                last_success_at=(
                    observation.observed_at
                ),
                last_failure_at=(
                    previous_state.last_failure_at
                    if previous_state is not None
                    else None
                ),
                degraded_since=None,
                unavailable_since=None,
            )

        return ProviderHealthState(
            provider=observation.provider,
            status=ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN,
            checked_at=observation.observed_at,
            consecutive_failures=(
                previous_state.consecutive_failures
                if previous_state is not None
                else 0
            ),
            consecutive_successes=(
                previous_state.consecutive_successes
                if previous_state is not None
                else 0
            ),
            last_success_at=(
                previous_state.last_success_at
                if previous_state is not None
                else None
            ),
            last_failure_at=(
                previous_state.last_failure_at
                if previous_state is not None
                else None
            ),
            degraded_since=(
                previous_state.degraded_since
                if previous_state is not None
                else None
            ),
            unavailable_since=(
                previous_state.unavailable_since
                if previous_state is not None
                else None
            ),
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _determine_transition(
        *,
        previous_state: ProviderHealthState | None,
        current_state: ProviderHealthState,
    ) -> ProviderHealthTransition:
        """
        Determine the significant transition represented
        by the latest observation.
        """

        previous_status = (
            previous_state.status
            if previous_state is not None
            else ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN
        )

        current_status = current_state.status

        if (
            previous_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
            and current_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.HEALTHY
        ):
            return ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED

        if (
            current_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.UNAVAILABLE
        ):
            return ProviderHealthTransition.UNAVAILABLE

        if (
            current_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.DEGRADED
        ):
            return ProviderHealthTransition.DEGRADED

        if (
            current_status
            == ProviderHealthStatus.UNKNOWN
        ):
            return ProviderHealthTransition.UNKNOWN

        return ProviderHealthTransition.RECOVERED

    @staticmethod
    def _validate_observation(
        observation: ProviderHealthObservation,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Validate required health observation fields.
        """

        if not observation.provider.strip():
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider cannot be empty."
            )

        if observation.latency_ms is not None:
            if observation.latency_ms < 0:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Latency cannot be negative."
                )

        if observation.consecutive_failures < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Consecutive failures cannot be negative."
            )

        if observation.consecutive_successes < 0:
            raise ValueError(
                "Consecutive successes cannot be negative."
            )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\application\provider_management\provider_operational_event_service.py =====
"""
EasyChatBot AI Operations Center provider operational event service.

Provides the provider-management adapter between provider-specific
operational lifecycle facts and the generic OperationalEventService.

Architecture:

Provider Incident Service
        |
        v
ProviderOperationalEventService
        |
        v
OperationalEventService
        |
        v
OperationalEventRepository
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.application.provider_management.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventSeverity,
    OperationalEventType,
)
from app.application.provider_management.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)
from app.application.provider_management.provider_health_models import (
    ProviderIncident,
)


class ProviderOperationalEventService:
    """
    Translate provider-management lifecycle facts into the
    generic operational-event contract.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        operational_event_service: OperationalEventService,
    ) -> None:
        if operational_event_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Operational event service cannot be None."
            )

        self._operational_event_service = (
            operational_event_service
        )

    def record_incident_created(
        self,
        *,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> OperationalEvent:
        """
        Record a provider incident-created operational fact.
        """

        if incident is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider incident cannot be None."
            )

        event = OperationalEvent.create(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_INCIDENT_CREATED
            ),
            severity=OperationalEventSeverity.CRITICAL,
            source="provider_management",
            component="ProviderIncidentService",
            occurred_at=incident.started_at,
            message=(
                f"Provider incident created "
                f"for {incident.provider}."
            ),
            correlation_id=incident.incident_id,
            metadata={
                "provider": incident.provider,
                "incident_id": incident.incident_id,
                "status": incident.status.value,
                "affected_capabilities": list(
                    incident.affected_capabilities
                ),
                "consecutive_failures": (
                    incident.consecutive_failures
                ),
            },
        )

        return self._operational_event_service.record(
            event
        )

    def record_provider_recovered(
        self,
        *,
        incident: ProviderIncident,
    ) -> OperationalEvent:
        """
        Record a provider-recovered operational fact.
        """

        if incident is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Provider incident cannot be None."
            )

        if incident.recovered_at is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "Recovered provider incident must "
                "have a recovery timestamp."
            )

        event = OperationalEvent.create(
            event_type=(
                OperationalEventType.PROVIDER_RECOVERED
            ),
            severity=OperationalEventSeverity.INFO,
            source="provider_management",
            component="ProviderIncidentService",
            occurred_at=incident.recovered_at,
            message=(
                f"Provider {incident.provider} "
                f"recovered."
            ),
            correlation_id=incident.incident_id,
            metadata={
                "provider": incident.provider,
                "incident_id": incident.incident_id,
                "status": incident.status.value,
                "affected_capabilities": list(
                    incident.affected_capabilities
                ),
                "downtime_seconds": (
                    incident.downtime_seconds
                ),
            },
        )

        return self._operational_event_service.record(
            event
        )

"""
Voice Call Execution Coordinator.

Application orchestration boundary
for voice call execution.

Responsibilities:
- Prepare voice room.
- Coordinate runtime execution.

Does NOT:
- Handle HTTP.
- Manage providers.
- Persist data.
- Connect LiveKit directly.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


from dataclasses import dataclass


from app.application.uai.voice.voice_room_service import (
    VoiceRoomService,
)


from app.ai_runtime.voice.voice_call_execution_runtime import (
    VoiceCallExecutionRuntime,
)


from app.domain.uai.voice.voice_call_execution_context import (
    VoiceCallExecutionContext,
)



@dataclass(
    frozen=True,
    slots=True,
)
class VoiceCallExecutionCoordinator:
    """
    Coordinates voice call execution.

    Keeps compatibility with the current
    application contracts.

    Future migration target:

        Lifecycle
            |
            Ã¢â€ â€œ
        ExecutionService
            |
            Ã¢â€ â€œ
        Runtime
    """


    room_service: VoiceRoomService

    execution_runtime: VoiceCallExecutionRuntime



    def start(
        self,
        *,
        context: VoiceCallExecutionContext,
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Prepare room and execute runtime.
        """


        room = (
            self.room_service
            .prepare_room(
                context=context,
            )
        )


        session = (
            self.execution_runtime
            .start(
                context=context,
            )
        )


        return {
            "room": room,
            "session": session,
        }



    def prepare(
        self,
        *,
        context: VoiceCallExecutionContext,
    ) -> dict:
        """
        Prepare room only.
        """


        return (
            self.room_service
            .prepare_room(
                context=context,
            )
        )



__all__ = [
    "VoiceCallExecutionCoordinator",
]
