"""
Voice Call Execution Service.

Application service responsible for
controlling execution of prepared
voice calls.

Responsibilities:
- Coordinate voice execution runtime.
- Build execution context.
- Expose application-level actions.

Does NOT:
- Handle HTTP.
- Manage LiveKit.
- Execute speech providers directly.
- Persist data.
"""

from __future__ import annotations


from dataclasses import dataclass



from app.ai_runtime.voice.voice_call_execution_runtime import (
    VoiceCallExecutionRuntime,
)


from app.domain.uai.voice.voice_call import (
    VoiceCall,
)


from app.domain.uai.voice.voice_call_execution_context import (
    VoiceCallExecutionContext,
)


from app.domain.uai.voice.voice_call_room import (
    VoiceCallRoom,
)


from app.domain.uai.voice.voice_call_session import (
    VoiceCallSession,
)


from app.domain.uai.voice.voice_call_participant import (
    VoiceCallParticipant,
    VoiceParticipantRole,
)



@dataclass(
    frozen=True,
    slots=True,
)
class VoiceCallExecutionService:
    """
    Application boundary for
    voice call execution.
    """


    execution_runtime: (
        VoiceCallExecutionRuntime
    )



    def _build_context(
        self,
        *,
        session: VoiceCallSession,
        voice_call: VoiceCall | None = None,
    ) -> VoiceCallExecutionContext:
        """
        Build runtime execution context.

        Beta composition:

        Learner
            +
        Character
        """


        learner = VoiceCallParticipant(
            participant_id=(
                session.learner_id
            ),
            role=(
                VoiceParticipantRole.LEARNER
            ),
            display_name="Learner",
        )


        character = VoiceCallParticipant(
            participant_id=(
                session.character_id
            ),
            role=(
                VoiceParticipantRole.CHARACTER
            ),
            display_name=(
                session.character_id
            ),
        )


        room = VoiceCallRoom(
            room_id=(
                session.session_id
            ),
            participants=(
                learner,
                character,
            ),
        )


        return VoiceCallExecutionContext(
            session=session,
            room=room,
            voice_call=voice_call,
        )



    def start_call(
        self,
        *,
        session: VoiceCallSession,
        voice_call: VoiceCall | None = None,
    ) -> VoiceCallSession:
        """
        Start voice call execution.
        """


        context = self._build_context(
            session=session,
            voice_call=voice_call,
        )


        return (
            self.execution_runtime
            .start(
                context=context,
            )
        )



    def stop_call(
        self,
        *,
        session: VoiceCallSession,
        voice_call: VoiceCall | None = None,
    ) -> VoiceCallSession:
        """
        Stop voice call execution.
        """


        context = self._build_context(
            session=session,
            voice_call=voice_call,
        )


        return (
            self.execution_runtime
            .stop(
                context=context,
            )
        )



    def fail_call(
        self,
        *,
        session: VoiceCallSession,
        voice_call: VoiceCall | None = None,
    ) -> VoiceCallSession:
        """
        Mark voice call execution failed.
        """


        context = self._build_context(
            session=session,
            voice_call=voice_call,
        )


        return (
            self.execution_runtime
            .fail(
                context=context,
            )
        )



__all__ = [
    "VoiceCallExecutionService",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\announcements\sqlalchemy_publication_execution_repository.py =====
"""SQLAlchemy durable execution repository for WS-22.

Uses the canonical EasyChatBot SQLAlchemy Base. This module deliberately does
not create a second DeclarativeBase/metadata registry.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import DateTime, Integer, String, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from app.application.announcements.execution.durable_contracts import (
    DurableExecutionStatus,
    PublicationExecutionRecord,
    PublicationExecutionRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base


class PublicationExecutionRecordModel(Base):
    """Durable operational state for a WS-22 publication execution."""

    __tablename__ = "announcement_publication_executions"

    publication_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(36), nullable=False)
    idempotency_key: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(255), primary_key=True
    )
    correlation_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(255),
        nullable=False,
    )
    attempt: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer,
        nullable=False,
        default=1,
    )
    status: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(32),
        nullable=False,
    )
    provider_message_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(255),
        nullable=True,
    )
    error_code: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100),
        nullable=True,
    )
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        nullable=False,
    )
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        nullable=False,
    )


class SqlAlchemyPublicationExecutionRepository(
    PublicationExecutionRepository
):
    """Durable repository using the existing injected async session factory."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory cannot be None.")

        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def get_by_idempotency_key(
        self,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord | None:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )
            model = result.scalar_one_or_none()

            return self._to_domain(model) if model else None

    async def create_started(
        self,
        *,
        publication_id,
        idempotency_key,
        correlation_id,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Create the first durable execution attempt."""

        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

        model = PublicationExecutionRecordModel(
            publication_id=str(publication_id),
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=correlation_id,
            attempt=1,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED.value,
            created_at=now,
            updated_at=now,
        )

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            session.add(model)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    async def mark_succeeded(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key,
        provider_message_id,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Persist successful completion without changing the attempt count."""

        return await self._update(
            idempotency_key,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.SUCCEEDED.value,
            provider_message_id=provider_message_id,
            error_code=None,
        )

    async def mark_failed(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key,
        error_code,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Persist failure while retaining the current attempt number."""

        return await self._update(
            idempotency_key,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED.value,
            error_code=error_code,
        )

    async def start_retry(
        self,
        *,
        idempotency_key: str,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        """Increment the durable attempt counter for a retry.

        A retry is valid only for an existing FAILED execution. The same
        idempotency record is retained; no second database row is created.
        """

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            model = result.scalar_one()

            if model.status != DurableExecutionStatus.FAILED.value:
                raise ValueError(
                    "Only failed executions can be retried."
                )

            model.attempt += 1
            model.status = DurableExecutionStatus.STARTED.value
            model.error_code = None
            model.provider_message_id = None
            model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    async def _update(
        self,
        idempotency_key,
        **values,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(
                select(PublicationExecutionRecordModel).where(
                    PublicationExecutionRecordModel.idempotency_key
                    == idempotency_key
                )
            )

            model = result.scalar_one()

            for key, value in values.items():
                setattr(model, key, value)

            model.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(model)

            return self._to_domain(model)

    @staticmethod
    def _to_domain(
        model: PublicationExecutionRecordModel,
    ) -> PublicationExecutionRecord:
        return PublicationExecutionRecord(
            publication_id=UUID(model.publication_id),
            idempotency_key=model.idempotency_key,
            correlation_id=model.correlation_id,
            attempt=model.attempt,
            status=DurableExecutionStatus(model.status),
            provider_message_id=model.provider_message_id,
            error_code=model.error_code,
            created_at=model.created_at,
            updated_at=model.updated_at,
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\developer_admin\postgresql_developer_admin_audit_repository.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_audit import (
    DeveloperAdminAuditEvent,
)


class PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminAuditRepository:
    """PostgreSQL persistence adapter for Developer Admin audit events."""

    _TABLE_NAME = "developer_admin_audit_events"

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        self._session_factory = session_factory

    async def record(
        self,
        event: DeveloperAdminAuditEvent,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminAuditEvent:
        """Persist one audit event using its own transaction.

        The audit table is explicitly represented here because this method
        is the standalone transaction boundary for this repository.
        """
        _audit_table = self._TABLE_NAME
        assert _audit_table == "developer_admin_audit_events"

        async with self._session_factory() as session:
            async with session.begin():
                await self._insert(session, event)

        return event

    async def record_in_session(
        self,
        session: AsyncSession,
        event: DeveloperAdminAuditEvent,
    ) -> DeveloperAdminAuditEvent:
        """Persist within an already-open caller-owned transaction."""
        await self._insert(session, event)
        return event

    async def _insert(
        self,
        session: AsyncSession,
        event: DeveloperAdminAuditEvent,
    ) -> None:
        metadata: dict[str, str] = dict(event.metadata)

        await session.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO developer_admin_audit_events (
                    id,
                    event_type,
                    actor_type,
                    actor_user_id,
                    target_user_id,
                    actor_email,
                    target_email,
                    occurred_at,
                    correlation_id,
                    outcome,
                    metadata
                )
                VALUES (
                    :id,
                    :event_type,
                    :actor_type,
                    :actor_user_id,
                    :target_user_id,
                    :actor_email,
                    :target_email,
                    :occurred_at,
                    :correlation_id,
                    :outcome,
                    CAST(:metadata AS jsonb)
                )
                """
            ),
            {
                "id": event.event_id,
                "event_type": event.event_type,
                "actor_type": event.actor_type,
                "actor_user_id": event.actor_user_id,
                "target_user_id": event.target_user_id,
                "actor_email": event.actor_email,
                "target_email": event.target_email,
                "occurred_at": event.occurred_at,
                "correlation_id": event.correlation_id,
                "outcome": event.outcome,
                "metadata": __import__("json").dumps(metadata),
            },
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\developer_admin\postgresql_developer_admin_uow.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any
from uuid import UUID

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_audit import DeveloperAdminAuditEvent
from app.infrastructure.auth.postgresql_auth_repository import AuthUserRecord
from app.infrastructure.developer_admin.postgresql_developer_admin_audit_repository import (
    PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminAuditRepository,
)


class PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWork:
    """Single PostgreSQL transaction for Developer Admin role + audit changes.

    A PostgreSQL transaction-scoped advisory lock serializes Developer Admin
    mutations so the last-admin invariant cannot race with another mutation.
    """

    _MUTATION_LOCK_KEY = 84172931

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
        *,
        audit_repository: PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminAuditRepository | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory is required.")
        self._session_factory = session_factory
        self._audit_repository = audit_repository
        self._session: AsyncSession | None = None
        self._transaction = None

    async def __aenter__(self) -> "PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWork":
        self._session = self._session_factory()
        self._transaction = await self._session.begin()
        await self._session.execute(
            text("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(:lock_key)"),
            {"lock_key": self._MUTATION_LOCK_KEY},
        )
        return self

    async def __aexit__(self, exc_type, exc, traceback) -> None:
        try:
            if self._transaction is not None and self._transaction.is_active:
                if exc_type is None:
                    await self._transaction.commit()
                else:
                    await self._transaction.rollback()
        finally:
            if self._session is not None:
                await self._session.close()
            self._session = None
            self._transaction = None

    @property
    def session(self) -> AsyncSession:
        if self._session is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Developer Admin unit of work is not active.")
        return self._session

    async def get_user(self, user_id: UUID) -> AuthUserRecord | None:
        result = await self.session.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT id, email, username, status, email_verified
                FROM users
                WHERE id = :user_id
                LIMIT 1
                FOR UPDATE
                """
            ),
            {"user_id": user_id},
        )
        row = result.mappings().first()
        if row is None:
            return None
        return AuthUserRecord(
            id=row["id"],
            email=row["email"],
            username=row["username"],
            status=row["status"],
            email_verified=bool(row["email_verified"]),
        )

    async def get_user_roles(self, user_id: UUID) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        result = await self.session.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT role
                FROM auth_user_roles
                WHERE user_id = :user_id
                ORDER BY role
                """
            ),
            {"user_id": user_id},
        )
        return tuple(row[0] for row in result.all())

    async def count_users_with_role(self, role: str) -> int:
        result = await self.session.execute(
            text(
                """
                SELECT COUNT(*)
                FROM auth_user_roles
                WHERE role = :role
                """
            ),
            {"role": role},
        )
        return int(result.scalar_one())

    async def add_user_role(self, *, user_id: UUID, role: str) -> bool:
        result = await self.session.execute(
            text(
                """
                INSERT INTO auth_user_roles (user_id, role)
                VALUES (:user_id, :role)
                ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING
                """
            ),
            {"user_id": user_id, "role": role},
        )
        return result.rowcount == 1

    async def remove_user_role(self, *, user_id: UUID, role: str) -> bool:
        result = await self.session.execute(
            text(
                """
                DELETE FROM auth_user_roles
                WHERE user_id = :user_id
                  AND role = :role
                """
            ),
            {"user_id": user_id, "role": role},
        )
        return result.rowcount == 1

    async def record_audit(self, event: DeveloperAdminAuditEvent) -> DeveloperAdminAuditEvent:
        if self._audit_repository is None:
            raise RuntimeError("Developer Admin audit repository is required.")
        return await self._audit_repository.record_in_session(self.session, event)

from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession, async_sessionmaker

from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_uow_factory import (
    DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory,
)
from app.infrastructure.developer_admin.postgresql_developer_admin_audit_repository import (
    PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminAuditRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.developer_admin.postgresql_developer_admin_uow import (
    PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWork,
)


class PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory(
    DeveloperAdminUnitOfWorkFactory,
):
    """Creates a fresh PostgreSQL Developer Admin UoW per operation."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        session_factory: async_sessionmaker[AsyncSession],
    ) -> None:
        if session_factory is None:
            raise ValueError("session_factory is required.")

        self._session_factory = session_factory

    def create(self) -> PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWork:
        """Create a new UoW with no shared transaction/session state."""

        audit_repository = PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminAuditRepository(
            self._session_factory,
        )

        return PostgreSQLDeveloperAdminUnitOfWork(
            self._session_factory,
            audit_repository=audit_repository,
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\health\llm_provider_health_check.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import os
from typing import Any

from app.application.health.health_result import (
    HealthCheckResult,
    HealthStatus,
)


class LLMProviderHealthCheck:
    """Read-only health probe for the configured LLM provider.

    Ollama uses /api/tags.

    OpenAI uses the models endpoint.

    Neither probe generates a learner response or sends
    learner content to the provider.
    """

    name = "llm_provider"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        provider: str | None = None,
        request_get: Any | None = None,
        openai_client_factory: Any | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._provider = (
            provider
            or os.getenv(
                "LLM_PROVIDER",
                "ollama",
            )
        ).strip().lower()

        self._request_get = request_get
        self._openai_client_factory = (
            openai_client_factory
        )

    async def check(self) -> HealthCheckResult:
        if self._provider == "ollama":
            return await self._check_ollama()

        if self._provider == "openai":
            return await self._check_openai()

        return HealthCheckResult(
            name=self.name,
            status=HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
            details={
                "reason": "unsupported_provider",
            },
        )

    async def _check_ollama(
        self,
    ) -> HealthCheckResult:
        base_url = os.getenv(
            "OLLAMA_BASE_URL",
            "http://localhost:11434",
        ).rstrip("/")

        try:
            if self._request_get is not None:
                response = await self._request_get(
                    f"{base_url}/api/tags"
                )

                status_code = getattr(
                    response,
                    "status_code",
                    200,
                )

                if status_code >= 400:
                    raise RuntimeError(
                        "Ollama health request failed."
                    )

            else:
                import requests

                response = await asyncio.to_thread(
                    requests.get,
                    f"{base_url}/api/tags",
                    timeout=10,
                )

                response.raise_for_status()

            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.HEALTHY,
                details={
                    "provider": "ollama",
                    "reachable": True,
                },
            )

        except Exception:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
                details={
                    "provider": "ollama",
                    "reason": "provider_unavailable",
                },
            )

    async def _check_openai(
        self,
    ) -> HealthCheckResult:
        if not os.getenv(
            "OPENAI_API_KEY"
        ):
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.NOT_CONFIGURED,
                details={
                    "provider": "openai",
                    "configured": False,
                },
            )

        try:
            if self._openai_client_factory is None:
                from openai import AsyncOpenAI

                client = AsyncOpenAI(
                    api_key=os.environ[
                        "OPENAI_API_KEY"
                    ],
                )

            else:
                client = (
                    self._openai_client_factory()
                )

            await client.models.list()

            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.HEALTHY,
                details={
                    "provider": "openai",
                    "reachable": True,
                },
            )

        except Exception:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
                details={
                    "provider": "openai",
                    "reason": "provider_unavailable",
                },
            )


__all__ = [
    "LLMProviderHealthCheck",
]








===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\health\postgresql_health_check.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.health.health_result import (
    HealthCheckResult,
    HealthStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_database_engine,
)


class PostgreSQLHealthCheck:
    """Read-only PostgreSQL connectivity probe.

    Reuses the existing database factory and performs only SELECT 1.
    """

    name = "postgresql"

    def __init__(self, *, engine=None) -> None:
        self._engine = engine

    async def check(self) -> HealthCheckResult:
        owns_engine = self._engine is None
        engine = self._engine or create_database_engine()

        try:
            await asyncio.to_thread(
                self._select_one,
                engine,
            )

            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.HEALTHY,
                details={
                    "connectivity": "ok",
                },
            )

        except Exception:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
                details={
                    "reason": "database_unavailable",
                },
            )

        finally:
            if owns_engine:
                await asyncio.to_thread(
                    engine.dispose,
                )

    @staticmethod
    def _select_one(engine) -> None:
        with engine.connect() as connection:
            connection.execute(
                text("SELECT 1")
            )


__all__ = [
    "PostgreSQLHealthCheck",
]










===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\infrastructure\health\telegram_health_check.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from typing import Any

from app.application.health.health_result import (
    HealthCheckResult,
    HealthStatus,
)


class TelegramHealthCheck:
    """Read-only Telegram Bot API readiness probe.

    Uses Telegram getMe to verify that the configured bot token
    is valid and the Telegram API is reachable.

    No classroom or announcement-group message is sent.
    """

    name = "telegram"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        bot_factory: Any | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._bot_factory = bot_factory

    async def check(self) -> HealthCheckResult:
        token = os.getenv(
            "TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN"
        )

        if not token:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.NOT_CONFIGURED,
                details={
                    "configured": False,
                },
            )

        try:
            if self._bot_factory is None:
                from telegram import Bot

                bot = Bot(
                    token=token,
                )
            else:
                bot = self._bot_factory(
                    token,
                )

            try:
                user = await bot.get_me()

            finally:
                shutdown = getattr(
                    bot,
                    "shutdown",
                    None,
                )

                if shutdown is not None:
                    result = shutdown()

                    if hasattr(
                        result,
                        "__await__",
                    ):
                        await result

            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.HEALTHY,
                details={
                    "configured": True,
                    "bot_reachable": True,
                    "bot_id_present": bool(
                        getattr(
                            user,
                            "id",
                            None,
                        )
                    ),
                },
            )

        except Exception:
            return HealthCheckResult(
                name=self.name,
                status=HealthStatus.UNHEALTHY,
                details={
                    "configured": True,
                    "reason": "telegram_api_unavailable",
                },
            )


__all__ = [
    "TelegramHealthCheck",
]

"""
LiveKit Voice Worker.

Infrastructure entry point for realtime voice sessions.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
import json
import logging
import os
from dataclasses import dataclass

from dotenv import load_dotenv

from app.application.provider_management.functionality_control_composition import (
    create_standalone_functionality_control_service,
)
from app.application.uai.voice.voice_call_context_factory import (
    VoiceCallContextFactory,
)
from app.application.uai.voice.voice_call_request import (
    VoiceCallRequest,
)
from app.application.uai.context.interaction_context_builder import (
    InteractionContextBuilder,
)
from app.domain.uai.context.learner_context import (
    LearnerContext,
)
from app.domain.uai.context.voice_context import (

    VoiceSpeechRuntime,

    LiveKitRealtimeAgent,
)


PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]
load_dotenv(PROJECT_ROOT / ".env")


def _validate_livekit_configuration() -> None:
    """Validate required LiveKit configuration."""

    required = (
        "LIVEKIT_URL",
        "LIVEKIT_API_KEY",
        "LIVEKIT_API_SECRET",
    )

    missing = [
        item
        for item in required
        if not os.getenv(item)
    ]

    if missing:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "Missing LiveKit configuration: "
            + ", ".join(missing)
        )


_validate_livekit_configuration()


logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class LiveKitVoiceWorker:
    """Infrastructure worker for EasyChatBot realtime voice."""

    context_factory: VoiceCallContextFactory
    interaction_context_builder: InteractionContextBuilder
    conversation_runtime: ConversationRuntime
    speech_runtime: VoiceSpeechRuntime

    async def run(
        self,
        *,
        ctx: JobContext,
    ) -> None:
        """Connect to LiveKit and start a realtime session."""

        metadata = self._read_job_metadata(ctx)

        request = self._create_voice_call_request(metadata)

        execution_context = self.context_factory.create(
            request=request,
        )

        learner = LearnerContext(
            learner_id=request.learner_id,
            learning_goals=("conversation",),
        )

        voice = VoiceContext(
            language_code=request.language_code,
            voice_provider="openai",
            voice_profile=request.character_id,
        )

        interaction_context = self.interaction_context_builder.build(
            learner=learner,
            world_id=request.world_id,
            character_id=request.character_id,
            voice=voice,
        )

        character = interaction_context.character

        instructions = (
            f"You are {character.name}. "
            "You are a French learning character. "
            "Your personality reference is "
            f"{character.personality_reference}. "
            "Your teaching style is "
            f"{character.teaching_style}. "
            "Speak naturally and help the learner "
            "practice the target language."
        )

        realtime_agent = LiveKitRealtimeAgent(
            context=interaction_context,
            instructions=instructions,
            conversation_runtime=self.conversation_runtime,
            speech_runtime=self.speech_runtime,
        )

        ctx.log_context_fields = {
            "room_name": ctx.room.name,
            "learner_id": request.learner_id,
            "world_id": request.world_id,
            "character_id": request.character_id,
            "language_code": request.language_code,
        }

        logger.info(
            "Starting EasyChatBot LiveKit voice agent",
            extra={
                "room_name": ctx.room.name,
                "learner_id": request.learner_id,
                "world_id": request.world_id,
                "character_id": request.character_id,
                "language_code": request.language_code,
            },
        )

        await ctx.connect()

        agent = realtime_agent.create_agent()
        session = realtime_agent.create_session()

        await session.start(
            agent=agent,
            room=ctx.room,
        )

    @staticmethod
    def _read_job_metadata(
        ctx: JobContext,
    ) -> dict[str, object]:
        """Read and validate LiveKit job metadata."""

        raw_metadata = ctx.job.metadata

        if not raw_metadata:
            raise ValueError(
                "LiveKit job metadata is required."
            )

        try:
            metadata = json.loads(raw_metadata)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ValueError(
                "LiveKit job metadata must be valid JSON."
            ) from exc

        if not isinstance(metadata, dict):
            raise ValueError(
                "LiveKit job metadata must be a JSON object."
            )

        return metadata

    @staticmethod
    def _create_voice_call_request(
        metadata: dict[str, object],
    ) -> VoiceCallRequest:
        """Convert LiveKit metadata into application request."""

        required_fields = (
            "learner_id",
            "world_id",
            "character_id",
            "language_code",
        )

        values: dict[str, str] = {}

        for field in required_fields:
            value = metadata.get(field)

            if not isinstance(value, str):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"LiveKit metadata field "
                    f"'{field}' must be a string."
                )

            if not value.strip():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"LiveKit metadata field "
                    f"'{field}' cannot be empty."
                )

            values[field] = value.strip()

        return VoiceCallRequest(
            learner_id=values["learner_id"],
            world_id=values["world_id"],
            character_id=values["character_id"],
            language_code=values["language_code"],
        )


server = AgentServer()

_functionality_control_service, _control_engine = (
    create_standalone_functionality_control_service()
)

_worker = LiveKitVoiceWorker(
    context_factory=VoiceCallContextFactory(),
    interaction_context_builder=InteractionContextBuilder(
        world_resolver=WorldResolver(
            world_repository=YamlWorldRepository(
                worlds_path=(
                    PROJECT_ROOT
                    / "backend"
                    / "app"
                    / "configuration"
                    / "worlds"
                )
            )
        ),
        character_resolver=CharacterResolver(
            character_repository=YamlCharacterRepository(
                profiles_path=(
                    Path(
                        r"C:\AI Folder\FrenchTeacherAI"
                    )
                    / "docs"
                    / "specifications"
                    / "characters"
                    / "profiles"
                )
            )
        ),
    ),
    conversation_runtime=build_conversation_runtime(),
    speech_runtime=build_voice_speech_runtime(
        functionality_control_service=(
            _functionality_control_service
        ),
    ),
)


@server.rtc_session(
    agent_name="easychatbot-voice",
)
async def entrypoint(
    ctx: JobContext,
) -> None:
    """LiveKit Agents entrypoint."""

    await _worker.run(ctx=ctx)


def run() -> None:
    """Start LiveKit Agents worker."""

    try:
        cli.run_app(server)
    finally:
        _control_engine.dispose()


__all__ = [
    "LiveKitVoiceWorker",
    "entrypoint",
    "run",
]


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()



===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\project_control_center\infrastructure\health_source.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app.application.health.health_result import HealthStatus
from app.application.health.production_health_service import (
    ProductionHealthService,
)

from ..domain.models import (
    ActionSnapshot,
    IssueSnapshot,
    MetricSnapshot,
    MilestoneSnapshot,
    RiskSnapshot,
    SourceSnapshot,
    SourceStatus,
    WorkstreamSnapshot,
)
from ..domain.ports import ProjectStatusSource


class ProductionHealthProjectStatusSource(ProjectStatusSource):
    """Read-only PCD adapter over ProductionHealthService.

    This adapter does not access databases, Telegram, LLM providers,
    or infrastructure health checks directly.

    ProductionHealthService remains the authoritative application-level
    entry point for production health.

    PCD-2A integrates health only. It deliberately does not invent or
    duplicate project workstream, issue, risk, action, or milestone data.
    """

    _SOURCE_ID = "production-health"
    _SOURCE_NAME = "Production Health"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        health_service: ProductionHealthService,
    ) -> None:
        if health_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "health_service cannot be None."
            )

        self._health_service = health_service

    @property
    def source_id(self) -> str:
        return self._SOURCE_ID

    @property
    def source_name(self) -> str:
        return self._SOURCE_NAME

    def health(self) -> SourceSnapshot:
        """Report adapter availability without probing dependencies.

        ProjectStatusSource.health() is synchronous, while the real
        production health check is asynchronous. Therefore this method
        intentionally performs no external health probe.

        The actual production health result is obtained through
        fetch_health().
        """
        return SourceSnapshot(
            source_id=self.source_id,
            name=self.source_name,
            status=SourceStatus.AVAILABLE,
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

    async def fetch_health(self) -> SourceSnapshot:
        """Execute ProductionHealthService and translate its result."""
        try:
            result = (
                await self._health_service
                .check_production_health()
            )
        except Exception:
            return SourceSnapshot(
                source_id=self.source_id,
                name=self.source_name,
                status=SourceStatus.UNAVAILABLE,
                updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
                error="production_health_check_failed",
            )

        if result.status is HealthStatus.HEALTHY:
            return SourceSnapshot(
                source_id=self.source_id,
                name=self.source_name,
                status=SourceStatus.AVAILABLE,
                updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            )

        return SourceSnapshot(
            source_id=self.source_id,
            name=self.source_name,
            status=SourceStatus.UNAVAILABLE,
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            error="production_health_not_ready",
        )

    async def fetch_metrics(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MetricSnapshot]:
        """PCD-2A does not create project metrics."""
        return []

    def fetch_workstreams(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[WorkstreamSnapshot]:
        """PCD-2A does not create project workstream data."""
        return []

    def fetch_issues(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[IssueSnapshot]:
        """PCD-2A does not create project issue data."""
        return []

    def fetch_risks(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[RiskSnapshot]:
        """PCD-2A does not create project risk data."""
        return []

    def fetch_actions(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[ActionSnapshot]:
        """PCD-2A does not create project action data."""
        return []

    def fetch_milestones(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MilestoneSnapshot]:
        """PCD-2A does not create project milestone data."""
        return []


__all__ = [
    "ProductionHealthProjectStatusSource",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\project_control_center\infrastructure\operations_source.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from app.application.health.operational_dashboard_service import (

from app.application.health.operational_incident import (

from ..domain.models import (

    RiskSnapshot,
    Severity,

from ..domain.ports import ProjectStatusSource


class OperationsProjectStatusSource(ProjectStatusSource):
    """Read-only PCD adapter over the existing Operations Center state.

    OperationalDashboardService remains the application-owned Operations
    boundary. This adapter does not create another incident store and does
    not access infrastructure directly.

    PCD-2B integrates operational incidents as dashboard issues because
    incidents represent actionable operational problems. It deliberately
    does not invent project workstreams, risks, actions, or milestones
    where the Operations Center has no authoritative contract for them.
    """

    _SOURCE_ID = "operations-center"
    _SOURCE_NAME = "Operations Center"

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        dashboard_service: OperationalDashboardService,
    ) -> None:
        if dashboard_service is None:
            raise ValueError(
                "dashboard_service cannot be None."
            )

        self._dashboard_service = dashboard_service

    @property
    def source_id(self) -> str:
        return self._SOURCE_ID

    @property
    def source_name(self) -> str:
        return self._SOURCE_NAME

    def health(self) -> SourceSnapshot:
        """Report adapter availability without performing a new probe.

        The Operations dashboard itself owns fresh health evaluation.
        ProjectStatusSource.health() is synchronous, so this method does
        not attempt to bridge into the asynchronous dashboard service.
        """
        return SourceSnapshot(
            source_id=self.source_id,
            name=self.source_name,
            status=SourceStatus.AVAILABLE,
            updated_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

    async def fetch_dashboard(
        self,
    ):
        """Return the authoritative Operations dashboard snapshot."""
        return await (
            self._dashboard_service
            .create_dashboard_snapshot()
        )

    def fetch_workstreams(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[WorkstreamSnapshot]:
        """Operations Center does not own project workstreams."""
        return []

    async def fetch_metrics(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MetricSnapshot]:
        """Translate authoritative operational metrics into PCD metrics."""
        dashboard = await self.fetch_dashboard()

        return [
            MetricSnapshot(
                key="operations.open_incidents",
                label="Open Operational Incidents",
                value=dashboard.private_classroom.incidents.open
                + dashboard.announcement_group.incidents.open,
                source=self.source_id,
                observed_at=dashboard.generated_at,
            ),
            MetricSnapshot(
                key="operations.critical_incidents",
                label="Critical Operational Incidents",
                value=dashboard.private_classroom.incidents.critical
                + dashboard.announcement_group.incidents.critical,
                source=self.source_id,
                observed_at=dashboard.generated_at,
            ),
            MetricSnapshot(
                key="operations.message_failures",
                label="Operational Message Failures",
                value=(
                    dashboard.private_classroom.metrics.message_failures
                    + dashboard.announcement_group.metrics.message_failures
                ),
                source=self.source_id,
                observed_at=dashboard.generated_at,
            ),
            MetricSnapshot(
                key="operations.rate_limit_events",
                label="Rate Limit Events",
                value=(
                    dashboard.private_classroom.metrics.rate_limit_events
                    + dashboard.announcement_group.metrics.rate_limit_events
                ),
                source=self.source_id,
                observed_at=dashboard.generated_at,
            ),
            MetricSnapshot(
                key="operations.abuse_events",
                label="Abuse Events",
                value=(
                    dashboard.private_classroom.metrics.abuse_events
                    + dashboard.announcement_group.metrics.abuse_events
                ),
                source=self.source_id,
                observed_at=dashboard.generated_at,
            ),
        ]

    async def fetch_issues(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[IssueSnapshot]:
        """Translate current operational incidents into PCD issues.

        Incident content/events are intentionally not copied into the PCD
        issue model. Only dashboard-safe operational identifiers and state
        are exposed.
        """
        dashboard = await self.fetch_dashboard()

        issues: list[IssueSnapshot] = []

        for incident in dashboard.incidents:
            issues.append(
                IssueSnapshot(
                    issue_id=incident.incident_id,
                    project_id=project_id,
                    title=(
                        f"Operational incident "
                        f"{incident.incident_id}"
                    ),
                    severity=self._map_severity(
                        incident.severity
                    ),
                    status=self._map_issue_status(
                        incident.status
                    ),
                    owner=None,
                    source=self.source_id,
                )
            )

        return issues

    def fetch_risks(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[RiskSnapshot]:
        """Operations Center does not own project risks."""
        return []

    def fetch_actions(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[ActionSnapshot]:
        """Operations Center does not own project actions."""
        return []

    def fetch_milestones(
        self,
        project_id: str,
    ) -> list[MilestoneSnapshot]:
        """Operations Center does not own project milestones."""
        return []

    @staticmethod
    def _map_severity(
        severity: str,
    ) -> Severity:
        try:
            return Severity(severity.lower())
        except ValueError:
            return Severity.MEDIUM

    @staticmethod
    def _map_issue_status(
        status: str,
    ) -> IssueStatus:
        normalized = status.lower()

        if normalized in {
            IncidentStatus.RESOLVED.value,
            IncidentStatus.CLOSED.value,
        }:
            return IssueStatus.RESOLVED

        if normalized in {
            IncidentStatus.INVESTIGATING.value,
            IncidentStatus.ACKNOWLEDGED.value,
            IncidentStatus.OPEN.value,
            IncidentStatus.DETECTED.value,
        }:
            return IssueStatus.IN_PROGRESS

        return IssueStatus.OPEN


__all__ = [
    "OperationsProjectStatusSource",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\api\announcement_operations_approval.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.application.announcements.approval.async_approval_service import (
    AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


announcement_publish_permission = require_permission(
    Permission.ANNOUNCEMENT_PUBLISH
)


class PublicationApprovalRequest(BaseModel):
    pass


class PublicationApprovalResponse(BaseModel):
    publication_id: str
    status: str
    approved_at: str | None


def create_announcement_operations_approval_router(
    *,
    approval_service: AsyncAnnouncementPublicationApprovalService,
) -> APIRouter:
    if approval_service is None:
        raise ValueError("approval_service cannot be None.")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/announcement-operations",
        tags=["announcement-operations"],
    )

    @router.post(
        "/publications/{publication_id}/approve",
        response_model=PublicationApprovalResponse,
        status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    )
    async def approve_publication(
        publication_id: str,
        _request: PublicationApprovalRequest,
        principal: Principal = Depends(announcement_publish_permission),
    ) -> PublicationApprovalResponse:
        try:
            result = await approval_service.approve(
                publication_id=publication_id,
                actor=principal.user_id,
            )
        except LookupError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc

        publication = result.publication

        return PublicationApprovalResponse(
            publication_id=publication.publication_id,
            status=publication.status.value,
            approved_at=(
                publication.approved_at.isoformat()
                if publication.approved_at is not None
                else None
            ),
        )

    return router


__all__ = [
    "PublicationApprovalRequest",
    "PublicationApprovalResponse",
    "announcement_publish_permission",
    "create_announcement_operations_approval_router",
]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\api\operations_center.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    OperationsCenterComposition,
    create_operations_center_composition,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.application.provider_management.environment import (
    OperationsEnvironment,
)
from app.application.provider_management.functionality_operational_control_service import (
    FunctionalityOperationalControlService,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


class FunctionalityStopRequest(BaseModel):
    environment: OperationsEnvironment
    reason: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=2000)


class FunctionalityResumeRequest(BaseModel):
    environment: OperationsEnvironment


def _control_unavailable() -> HTTPException:
    return HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
        detail="Functionality operational control is not configured.",
    )


def _result_as_dict(result: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "functionality_id": result.functionality_id,
        "environment": result.environment,
        "availability": result.availability,
        "action": result.action,
        "reason": result.reason,
        "changed_at": result.changed_at.isoformat(),
        "changed_by": result.changed_by,
    }


def create_operations_center_router(
    *,
    composition: OperationsCenterComposition | None = None,
) -> APIRouter:
    resolved_composition = composition or create_operations_center_composition()

    dashboard_api = resolved_composition.dashboard_api
    chat_operations_api = resolved_composition.chat_operations_api
    functionality_health_service = resolved_composition.functionality_health_service
    functionality_control_service = (
        resolved_composition.functionality_control_service
    )
    event_service = resolved_composition.event_service
    provider_usage_dashboard_service = (
        resolved_composition.provider_usage_dashboard_service
    )
    platform_intelligence_review_api = (
        resolved_composition.platform_intelligence_review_api
    )

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/operations-center",
        tags=["operations-center"],
    )

    @router.get("/dashboard")
    async def get_dashboard() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await dashboard_api.get_dashboard()

    @router.get("/chat-operations")
    async def get_chat_operations_dashboard() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_dashboard()

    @router.get("/chat-operations/private-classroom")
    async def get_private_classroom_operations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_private_classroom_details()

    @router.get("/chat-operations/announcement-group")
    async def get_announcement_group_operations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await chat_operations_api.get_announcement_group_details()

    @router.get("/functionality-health")
    async def get_functionality_health(
        environment: OperationsEnvironment | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return await functionality_health_service.get_health(
            environment=environment
        )

    @router.post(
        "/functionality/{functionality_id}/stop",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL))],
    )
    def stop_functionality(
        functionality_id: str,
        request: FunctionalityStopRequest,
        principal: Principal = Depends(
            require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL)
        ),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if functionality_control_service is None:
            raise _control_unavailable()

        try:
            result = functionality_control_service.stop(
                actor=principal,
                actor_name=principal.user_id,
                environment=request.environment.value,
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
                reason=request.reason,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except PermissionError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

        return _result_as_dict(result)

    @router.post(
        "/functionality/{functionality_id}/resume",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL))],
    )
    def resume_functionality(
        functionality_id: str,
        request: FunctionalityResumeRequest,
        principal: Principal = Depends(
            require_permission(Permission.FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL)
        ),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if functionality_control_service is None:
            raise _control_unavailable()

        try:
            result = functionality_control_service.resume(
                actor=principal,
                actor_name=principal.user_id,
                environment=request.environment.value,
                functionality_id=functionality_id,
            )
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from exc
        except PermissionError:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
                detail="Insufficient permission.",
            ) from None

        return _result_as_dict(result)



    @router.get(
        "/platform-intelligence/reviews",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))],
    )
    def get_platform_intelligence_reviews() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return platform_intelligence_review_api.list_reviews()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    @router.get(
        "/platform-intelligence/reviews/{review_item_id}",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))],
    )
    def get_platform_intelligence_review(
        review_item_id: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            result = platform_intelligence_review_api.get_review(review_item_id)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        if result is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail="Platform Intelligence review item not found.",
            )
        return result

    @router.get(
        "/platform-intelligence/escalations",
        dependencies=[Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))],
    )
    def get_platform_intelligence_escalations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return platform_intelligence_review_api.list_escalations()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    @router.get("/operational-events")
    def get_operational_events() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        incidents = event_service.list_incidents()
        return [
            event.as_dict()
            for incident in incidents
            for event in incident.events
        ]

    @router.get("/provider-usage")
    def get_provider_usage() -> dict[str, Any]:
        if provider_usage_dashboard_service is None:
            return {
                "status": "not_configured",
                "reason": "provider_usage_dashboard_service_not_configured",
            }
        return provider_usage_dashboard_service.get_snapshot()

    return router


router = create_operations_center_router()

from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request

from app.application.operations_center.email_workflow_status_service import (
    EmailWorkflowStatusService,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_email_workflow_registry import (
    PostgreSQLEmailWorkflowRegistry,
)
from cybersecurity.email.email_provider_manager import EmailProviderManager


def create_email_workflows_router(
    *,
    session_factory,
    operator_dependency: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    if session_factory is None:
        raise ValueError("session_factory is required.")
    if operator_dependency is None:
        raise ValueError("operator_dependency is required.")

    registry = PostgreSQLEmailWorkflowRegistry(session_factory)

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/operations-center/email-workflows",
        tags=["Operations Center", "Email Workflows"],
    )

    @router.get("")
    async def get_email_workflows(
        request: Request,
        _operator: Any = Depends(operator_dependency),
    ):
        service = EmailWorkflowStatusService(
            scheduler_runtime=request.app.state.scheduler_runtime,
            provider_manager=EmailProviderManager(),
            registry=registry,
        )
        return service.snapshot()

    @router.get("/{workflow_id}")
    async def get_email_workflow(
        workflow_id: str,
        request: Request,
        _operator: Any = Depends(operator_dependency),
    ):
        service = EmailWorkflowStatusService(
            scheduler_runtime=request.app.state.scheduler_runtime,
            provider_manager=EmailProviderManager(),
            registry=registry,
        )
        workflow = service.get(workflow_id)

        if workflow is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=404,
                detail="Email workflow not found.",
            )

        return workflow

    return router

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\api\platform_intelligence_review.py =====
"""Operations Center HTTP routes for Platform Intelligence human review."""
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, status

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission


def create_platform_intelligence_review_router(
    *,
    api: PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
) -> APIRouter:
    """Create the read-only PI review router.

    The caller owns composition and dependency injection. No global runtime or
    repository is constructed here.
    """
    if api is None:
        raise ValueError("api is required.")

    router = APIRouter(
        prefix="/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence",
        tags=["operations-center", "platform-intelligence"],
    )
    read_dependency = Depends(require_permission(Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ))

    @router.get("/reviews", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_reviews() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return api.list_reviews()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    @router.get("/reviews/{review_item_id}", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_review(review_item_id: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            result = api.get_review(review_item_id)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

        if result is None:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
                detail="Platform Intelligence review item not found.",
            )
        return result

    @router.get("/escalations", dependencies=[read_dependency])
    def get_escalations() -> dict[str, Any]:
        try:
            return api.list_escalations()
        except PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured as exc:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
                detail=str(exc),
            ) from None

    return router


__all__ = ["create_platform_intelligence_review_router"]

from __future__ import annotations

from uuid import UUID

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request, status

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.api.routes.developer_admin_transaction_contracts import (
    DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse,
    DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse,
    DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_service import (
    DeveloperAdminLastAdminError,
    DeveloperAdminService,
    DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError,
    DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.developer_admin_transaction_service import (
    DeveloperAdminTransactionService,
)
from app.application.developer_admin.trusted_approval_provider import (
    TrustedApprovalRequest,
    TrustedApprovalUnavailableError,
    UnavailableTrustedApprovalProvider,
)
from app.application.identity.transaction_authorization import (
    TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import Principal


def _get_service(request: Request) -> DeveloperAdminService:
    service = getattr(request.app.state, "developer_admin_service", None)
    if service is None:
        raise RuntimeError("developer_admin_service is not configured.")
    return service


def _get_transaction_service(
    request: Request,
) -> DeveloperAdminTransactionService:
    service = getattr(
        request.app.state,
        "developer_admin_transaction_service",
        None,
    )
    if service is None:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "developer_admin_transaction_service is not configured."
        )
    return service


def _get_approval_provider(request: Request):
    return getattr(
        request.app.state,
        "developer_admin_trusted_approval_provider",
        UnavailableTrustedApprovalProvider(),
    )


router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/operations-center/developer-administrators",
    tags=["operations-center-developer-administrators"],
)


@router.get("")
async def list_developer_administrators(
    request: Request,
    _principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
):
    administrators = await _get_service(request).list_developer_administrators()

    return {
        "administrators": [
            {
                "user_id": item.user_id,
                "email": item.email,
                "username": item.username,
                "status": item.status,
                "email_verified": item.email_verified,
            }
            for item in administrators
        ]
    }


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Legacy collection mutation retirement boundary
#
# Direct Developer Administrator mutations are no longer supported.
#
# The only supported mutation path is:
#
#   create transaction
#       ->
#   trusted approval
#       ->
#   execute transaction
#
# This explicit POST tombstone exists only for the former collection
# mutation endpoint. It performs no mutation.
#
# The former DELETE /{user_id} endpoint intentionally does NOT exist.
# Keeping that route out of the router is part of the API contract.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@router.post("")
async def legacy_direct_create_developer_administrator() -> None:
    raise HTTPException(
        status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
        detail=(
            "Developer Administrator direct mutation endpoint "
            "is no longer available."
        ),
    )


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Transaction-authorized Developer Admin mutations
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


@router.post(
    "/transactions/grant",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
)
async def create_grant_transaction(
    payload: DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse:
    try:
        authorization = _get_transaction_service(request).create_grant_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=payload.target_user_id,
        )
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    transaction = authorization.transaction
    risk_context = dict(transaction.risk_context)

    return DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=transaction.transaction_id,
        operation=transaction.operation,
        audience=risk_context["audience"],
        target_user_id=UUID(risk_context["target_user_id"]),
        state=authorization.state.value,
        expires_at=authorization.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/revoke",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
)
async def create_revoke_transaction(
    payload: DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse:
    try:
        authorization = _get_transaction_service(request).create_revoke_transaction(
            principal=principal,
            target_user_id=payload.target_user_id,
        )
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    transaction = authorization.transaction
    risk_context = dict(transaction.risk_context)

    return DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=transaction.transaction_id,
        operation=transaction.operation,
        audience=risk_context["audience"],
        target_user_id=UUID(risk_context["target_user_id"]),
        state=authorization.state.value,
        expires_at=authorization.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/{authorization_id}/approve",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse,
)
async def approve_transaction(
    authorization_id: str,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse:
    transaction_service = _get_transaction_service(request)

    authorization = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    if authorization is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Transaction authorization was not found.",
        )

    provider = _get_approval_provider(request)

    try:
        approval = await provider.approve(
            request=TrustedApprovalRequest(
                authorization_id=authorization.authorization_id,
                principal_id=principal.user_id,
                audience=transaction_service.AUDIENCE,
                transaction_id=authorization.transaction.transaction_id,
                requested_at=authorization.created_at,
            )
        )

        approved = transaction_service.attach_trusted_approval(
            principal=principal,
            approval=approval,
        )
    except TrustedApprovalUnavailableError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    return DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse(
        authorization_id=approved.authorization_id,
        transaction_id=approved.transaction.transaction_id,
        state=approved.state.value,
        expires_at=approved.expires_at,
    )


@router.post(
    "/transactions/{authorization_id}/execute",
    response_model=DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse,
)
async def execute_transaction(
    authorization_id: str,
    request: Request,
    principal: Principal = Depends(
        require_permission(Permission.DEVELOPER_ADMIN_MANAGE)
    ),
) -> DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse:
    transaction_service = _get_transaction_service(request)

    authorization = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    if authorization is None:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail="Transaction authorization was not found.",
        )

    try:
        transaction = authorization.transaction

        if transaction.operation == transaction_service.GRANT_OPERATION:
            await transaction_service.execute_grant(
                principal=principal,
                authorization_id=authorization_id,
                transaction=transaction,
            )
        elif transaction.operation == transaction_service.REVOKE_OPERATION:
            await transaction_service.execute_revoke(
                principal=principal,
                authorization_id=authorization_id,
                transaction=transaction,
            )
        else:
            raise PermissionError(
                "Developer Administrator transaction operation is invalid."
            )

    except DeveloperAdminUserNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        DeveloperAdminUserInactiveError,
        DeveloperAdminLastAdminError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None
    except (
        PermissionError,
        TransactionAuthorizationApplicationError,
        ValueError,
    ) as exc:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=status.HTTP_403_FORBIDDEN,
            detail=str(exc),
        ) from None

    consumed = transaction_service.get_authorization(
        authorization_id=authorization_id
    )

    state = (
        consumed.state.value
        if consumed is not None
        else "CONSUMED"
    )

    return DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse(
        authorization_id=authorization_id,
        state=state,
    )


__all__ = ["router"]

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\app\api\routes\developer_admin_transaction_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime
from uuid import UUID

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict


class DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    target_user_id: UUID


class DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionRequest(BaseModel):
    """Request contract for executing one already-authorized transaction.

    The client supplies only the authorization reference.
    The canonical transaction, including its target, is resolved server-side
    from the stored transaction authorization.
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    authorization_id: str


class DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    authorization_id: str
    transaction_id: str
    operation: str
    audience: str
    target_user_id: UUID
    state: str
    expires_at: datetime


class DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    authorization_id: str
    transaction_id: str
    state: str
    expires_at: datetime


class DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")

    authorization_id: str
    state: str


__all__ = [
    "DeveloperAdminApprovalResponse",
    "DeveloperAdminCreateTransactionRequest",
    "DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionRequest",
    "DeveloperAdminExecuteTransactionResponse",
    "DeveloperAdminPendingTransactionResponse",
]

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query

from app.application.persistence_operations import PersistenceDiagnosticsService


def create_persistence_operations_router(
    service: PersistenceDiagnosticsService,
    *,
    operator_dependency: Callable[..., Any],
) -> APIRouter:
    router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/operations-center/persistence", tags=["operations-center-persistence"])

    @router.get("", summary="Get live persistence overview")
    def get_persistence_diagnostics(
        environment: str = Query("dev", pattern="^(dev|staging|production)$"),
        _operator: Any = Depends(operator_dependency),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return service.snapshot(environment=environment)

    @router.get("/health", summary="Get live PostgreSQL health and operations")
    def get_database_health(
        environment: str = Query("dev", pattern="^(dev|staging|production)$"),
        _operator: Any = Depends(operator_dependency),
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return service.health_snapshot(environment=environment)

    return router
