===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\academy\test_academy_real_lifespan_scheduler_smoke.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from app.infrastructure.telegram.telegram_announcement_client import (
    TelegramAnnouncementClient,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_academy_real_shared_scheduler_lifespan_smoke():
    """
    Controlled end-to-end scheduler lifecycle smoke test.

    Uses:
    - real FastAPI lifespan;
    - real CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime;
    - real CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter;
    - real APScheduler.

    Academy execution and external delivery remain mocked.

    No real email or Telegram delivery occurs.
    """

    fake_engine = MagicMock(
        name="auth_engine",
    )

    fake_session_factory = MagicMock(
        name="auth_session_factory",
    )

    telegram_client = MagicMock(
        spec=TelegramAnnouncementClient,
        name="announcement_telegram_client",
    )

    announcement_publisher = MagicMock(
        name="announcement_publisher",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_execution_count = 0

    async def academy_run():
        nonlocal academy_execution_count

        academy_execution_count += 1

        return {
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        }

    academy_workflow.run = academy_run

    with (
        patch.dict(
            "os.environ",
            {
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_SCHEDULER_ENABLED": "true",
                "EASYCHATBOT_ACADEMY_FIRST_RUN_ENABLED": "true",
            },
            clear=False,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_database_engine",
            return_value=fake_engine,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_async_session_factory",
            return_value=fake_session_factory,
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.PostgreSQLAuthenticationRepository",
            return_value=MagicMock(
                name="auth_repository",
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main._create_announcement_components",
            return_value=(
                telegram_client,
                announcement_publisher,
            ),
        ),
        patch(
            "app.main.create_academy_one_week_workflow",
            return_value=academy_workflow,
        ),
    ):
        from app.main import create_application

        application = create_application()

        runtime = application.state.scheduler_runtime
        adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter
        scheduler = adapter.scheduler

        #
        # Application construction must not start scheduling.
        #

        assert scheduler.running is False

        async with application.router.lifespan_context(
            application,
        ):
            #
            # FastAPI lifespan starts the existing scheduler.
            #

            assert scheduler.running is True

            jobs = scheduler.get_jobs()

            job_ids = {
                job.id
                for job in jobs
            }

            #
            # Existing cybersecurity jobs remain registered.
            #

            assert (
                "cybersecurity_daily_report"
                in job_ids
            )

            assert (
                "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
                in job_ids
            )

            #
            # Academy is registered on the same scheduler.
            #

            assert (
                "academy_one_week_milestone"
                in job_ids
            )

            assert (
                adapter.academy_one_week_workflow
                is academy_workflow
            )

            #
            # Starting the scheduler must not execute the Academy
            # cron job immediately.
            #

            await asyncio.sleep(0.05)

            assert academy_execution_count == 0

            #
            # No Telegram delivery may occur.
            #
            # close() is intentionally excluded because it is normal
            # FastAPI lifespan cleanup and is not a delivery operation.
            #

            telegram_delivery_calls = [
                call
                for call in telegram_client.method_calls
                if call[0] not in {"close"}
            ]

            assert telegram_delivery_calls == []

        #
        # FastAPI lifespan has exited and requested scheduler shutdown.
        #

        for _ in range(20):
            if scheduler.running is False:
                break

            await asyncio.sleep(0.01)

        assert scheduler.running is False

    #
    # Academy must never have executed during this smoke test.
    #

    assert academy_execution_count == 0

    #
    # Telegram close is expected lifecycle cleanup.
    #

    telegram_client.close.assert_called_once()

    #
    # No Telegram delivery operation occurred.
    #

    telegram_delivery_calls = [
        call
        for call in telegram_client.method_calls
        if call[0] not in {"close"}
    ]

    assert telegram_delivery_calls == []


===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\academy\test_academy_scheduler_invocation.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_apscheduler_adapter import (
    CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def test_academy_scheduler_job_points_to_workflow_run():
    """
    The Academy scheduler job must point to the actual async
    AcademyOneWeekWorkflow.run() method.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    workflow_service = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow_service",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow.run = AsyncMock(
        name="academy_workflow_run",
        return_value={
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        },
    )

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=workflow_service,
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_calls = [
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id") == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    ]

    assert len(academy_calls) == 1

    academy_call = academy_calls[0]

    assert academy_call.args[0] is academy_workflow.run
    assert academy_call.kwargs["trigger"] == "cron"
    assert academy_call.kwargs["hour"] == 8
    assert academy_call.kwargs["minute"] == 15


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_registered_academy_job_can_invoke_workflow_without_external_delivery():
    """
    Invoke the registered callback directly.

    This simulates APScheduler executing the job while avoiding
    production scheduler startup and all external delivery.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    workflow_service = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow_service",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow.run = AsyncMock(
        name="academy_workflow_run",
        return_value={
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        },
    )

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=workflow_service,
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_call = next(
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id") == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    )

    registered_callback = academy_call.args[0]

    result = await registered_callback()

    assert result == {
        "candidates": 0,
        "attempted": 0,
        "succeeded": 0,
        "failed": 0,
        "skipped": 0,
    }

    academy_workflow.run.assert_awaited_once_with()

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime import (
    CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_shared_scheduler_lifecycle_starts_and_stops_cleanly():
    """
    The shared scheduler runtime must start and stop cleanly.

    Academy is already injected into the existing scheduler boundary.
    """

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_adapter",
    )

    adapter.start = MagicMock(
        name="start",
    )

    adapter.shutdown = MagicMock(
        name="shutdown",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    await runtime.start()

    adapter.start.assert_called_once()

    await runtime.stop()

    adapter.shutdown.assert_called_once()


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_scheduler_lifecycle_does_not_execute_academy_workflow():
    """
    Starting/stopping the scheduler lifecycle must not directly execute
    the Academy workflow.
    """

    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    scheduler.running = False

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="scheduler_adapter",
    )

    adapter.scheduler = scheduler
    adapter.academy_one_week_workflow = academy_workflow

    adapter.start = MagicMock(
        name="start",
    )

    adapter.shutdown = MagicMock(
        name="shutdown",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    await runtime.start()
    await runtime.stop()

    academy_workflow.run.assert_not_called()


def test_scheduler_runtime_uses_existing_adapter():
    """
    Runtime must use the supplied scheduler adapter rather than creating
    scheduler infrastructure itself.
    """

    adapter = MagicMock(
        name="existing_scheduler_adapter",
    )

    runtime = CybersecuritySchedulerRuntime(
        scheduler_adapter=adapter,
    )

    assert runtime.scheduler_adapter is adapter

from __future__ import annotations

from unittest.mock import MagicMock, patch

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory import (
    create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


def _create_controlled_scheduler():
    scheduler = MagicMock(
        name="shared_scheduler",
    )

    scheduler.running = False

    return scheduler


def test_academy_workflow_is_injected_into_existing_scheduler():
    """
    Academy workflow must be injected into the existing scheduler
    runtime boundary.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=MagicMock(
                name="cybersecurity_workflow",
            ),
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    assert adapter.scheduler is scheduler

    assert (
        adapter.academy_one_week_workflow
        is academy_workflow
    )


def test_academy_job_registration_does_not_start_scheduler():
    """
    Academy registration must add exactly one Academy job and must not
    start or execute the scheduler.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=MagicMock(
                name="cybersecurity_workflow",
            ),
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_calls = [
        call
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
        if call.kwargs.get("id")
        == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    ]

    assert len(academy_calls) == 1

    academy_call = academy_calls[0]

    assert (
        academy_call.args[0]
        is academy_workflow.run
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["trigger"]
        == "cron"
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["hour"]
        == adapter.academy_one_week_hour
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["minute"]
        == adapter.academy_one_week_minute
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["id"]
        == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    )

    assert (
        academy_call.kwargs["replace_existing"]
        is True
    )

    assert scheduler.start.called is False

    assert scheduler.running is False

    assert academy_workflow.run.called is False


def test_academy_and_cybersecurity_jobs_share_one_scheduler():
    """
    Academy and existing cybersecurity jobs must be registered on
    the same scheduler instance.
    """

    scheduler = _create_controlled_scheduler()

    cybersecurity_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="cybersecurity_workflow",
    )

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    with patch(
        "cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory.AsyncIOScheduler",
        return_value=scheduler,
    ):

        runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
            workflow_service=cybersecurity_workflow,
            academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
        )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    assert adapter.scheduler is scheduler

    adapter.register_jobs()

    registered_ids = {
        call.kwargs.get("id")
        for call in scheduler.add_job.call_args_list
    }

    assert (
        "cybersecurity_daily_report"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert (
        "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert (
        "academy_one_week_milestone"
        in registered_ids
    )

    assert scheduler.start.called is False
    assert scheduler.running is False

    assert cybersecurity_workflow.create_daily_report.called is False
    assert cybersecurity_workflow.create_weekly_report.called is False
    assert academy_workflow.run.called is False


===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\academy\test_academy_scheduler_smoke.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
from unittest.mock import MagicMock

import pytest

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_scheduler_factory import (
    create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.integration


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_shared_scheduler_start_registers_academy_and_stops_cleanly():
    """
    Controlled real APScheduler lifecycle smoke test.

    Verifies:
    - one shared scheduler;
    - Academy job registration;
    - existing cybersecurity jobs registration;
    - scheduler starts inside a running asyncio event loop;
    - scheduler becomes running;
    - Academy workflow is not executed;
    - shutdown is invoked cleanly.

    No real email or Telegram delivery is performed.
    """

    academy_workflow = MagicMock(
        name="academy_one_week_workflow",
    )

    runtime = create_cybersecurity_scheduler_runtime(
        workflow_service=MagicMock(
            name="cybersecurity_workflow",
        ),
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy_workflow,
    )

    adapter = runtime.scheduler_adapter

    try:
        adapter.register_jobs()

        jobs = adapter.scheduler.get_jobs()

        job_ids = {
            job.id
            for job in jobs
        }

        assert (
            "academy_one_week_milestone"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert (
            "cybersecurity_daily_report"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert (
            "cybersecurity_weekly_report"
            in job_ids
        )

        assert adapter.scheduler.running is False

        adapter.start()

        assert adapter.scheduler.running is True

        # Starting the scheduler must not execute Academy immediately.
        assert academy_workflow.run.call_count == 0

    finally:
        adapter.shutdown()

        # Give the asyncio scheduler event loop one opportunity to
        # process its shutdown state.
        await asyncio.sleep(0)

    # The important lifecycle contract is that shutdown was invoked
    # and no Academy execution occurred.
    assert academy_workflow.run.call_count == 0

from types import SimpleNamespace

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from fastapi import FastAPI

from app.api.operations_center import create_operations_center_router
from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    create_operations_center_composition,
)


class Source:
    def _item(self):
        return SimpleNamespace(
            review_item_id="R1",
            risk_decision_id="D1",
            risk_assessment_id="A1",
            scope="platform",
            risk_level="HIGH",
            risk_score=0.8,
            priority="HIGH",
            review_status="PENDING",
            decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
            quality_gate_status="PASSED",
            evidence_references=("E1",),
            historical_references=("H1",),
            policy_id="P",
            policy_version="1",
            created_at=SimpleNamespace(
                isoformat=lambda: "2026-09-22T00:00:00+00:00"
            ),
            assigned_reviewer=None,
            assigned_at=None,
            review_due_at=None,
            rationale="test",
            recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
            recommendation_route="HUMAN_REVIEW",
            recommendation_reasons=("risk",),
            recommendation_id="REC1",
        )

    def list_reviews(self):
        return [self._item()]

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return self._item() if review_item_id == "R1" else None

    def list_escalations(self):
        return [self._item()]


def _client(source=None, monkeypatch=None):
    app = FastAPI()
    if monkeypatch is not None:
        import app.api.operations_center as operations_center_module
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            operations_center_module,
            "require_permission",
            lambda _permission: (lambda: None),
        )
    composition = create_operations_center_composition(
        platform_intelligence_review_source=source
    )
    app.include_router(create_operations_center_router(composition=composition))
    return TestClient(app)


def test_existing_operations_center_dashboard_route_is_preserved(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/dashboard")
    assert response.status_code == 200


def test_pi_reviews_are_exposed_through_existing_operations_center_router(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["count"] == 1


def test_pi_escalations_are_exposed_through_existing_operations_center_router(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/escalations")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["count"] == 1


def test_pi_review_detail_not_found_is_404(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(Source(), monkeypatch)
    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews/UNKNOWN"
    )
    assert response.status_code == 404


def test_pi_integration_fails_closed_when_source_is_not_configured(monkeypatch):
    client = _client(monkeypatch=monkeypatch)
    response = client.get("/api/operations-center/platform-intelligence/reviews")
    assert response.status_code == 503

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from types import SimpleNamespace

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_api import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI,
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_review_source import (
    PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource,
)


class FakeSource(PlatformIntelligenceReviewSource):
    def __init__(self, items):
        self.items = tuple(items)

    def list_reviews(self):
        return self.items

    def get_review(self, review_item_id):
        return next(
            (item for item in self.items if item.review_item_id == review_item_id),
            None,
        )

    def list_escalations(self):
        return tuple(
            item
            for item in self.items
            if getattr(
                getattr(item, "recommendation_status", None),
                "value",
                getattr(item, "recommendation_status", None),
            )
            == "RECOMMENDED"
        )


def projection(*, recommended=False):
    return SimpleNamespace(
        review_item_id="review-1",
        risk_decision_id="decision-1",
        risk_assessment_id="assessment-1",
        scope="platform",
        risk_level=SimpleNamespace(value="HIGH"),
        risk_score=0.8,
        priority=SimpleNamespace(value="HIGH"),
        review_status=SimpleNamespace(value="IN_REVIEW"),
        decision_status=SimpleNamespace(value="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW"),
        quality_gate_status=SimpleNamespace(value="PASSED"),
        evidence_references=("e1",),
        historical_references=("h1",),
        policy_id="risk-policy",
        policy_version="1.0",
        created_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        assigned_reviewer="reviewer-1",
        assigned_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
        review_due_at=None,
        rationale="Governed review required.",
        recommendation_status=(
            SimpleNamespace(value="RECOMMENDED") if recommended else None
        ),
        recommendation_route=(
            SimpleNamespace(value="RISK_OWNER") if recommended else None
        ),
        recommendation_reasons=("risk_level=HIGH",) if recommended else (),
        recommendation_id="esc-1" if recommended else None,
        metadata={"sensitive": "must-not-leak"},
    )


def test_list_serializes_only_approved_projection_fields():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=FakeSource([projection()]))

    result = api.list_reviews()

    assert result["status"] == "ready"
    assert result["count"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["risk_decision_id"] == "decision-1"
    assert "metadata" not in result["items"][0]


def test_get_missing_returns_none():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=FakeSource([]))

    assert api.get_review("missing") is None


def test_escalations_only_include_recommended_items():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(
        source=FakeSource([projection(), projection(recommended=True)])
    )

    result = api.list_escalations()

    assert result["count"] == 1
    assert result["items"][0]["recommendation_status"] == "RECOMMENDED"


def test_unconfigured_source_is_explicitly_not_configured():
    api = PlatformIntelligenceReviewAPI(source=None)

    with pytest.raises(PlatformIntelligenceReviewNotConfigured):
        api.list_reviews()

from __future__ import annotations

from cybersecurity.scheduler.cybersecurity_apscheduler_adapter import (
    CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter,
)


class FakeScheduler:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.jobs: list[dict] = []
        self.running = False

    def add_job(self, function, **kwargs):
        self.jobs.append(
            {
                "function": function,
                **kwargs,
            }
        )

    def start(self) -> None:
        self.running = True

    def shutdown(self) -> None:
        self.running = False


class FakeCybersecurityWorkflow:
    async def create_daily_report(self):
        return {"type": "daily"}

    async def create_weekly_report(self):
        return {"type": "weekly"}


class FakeAcademyWorkflow:
    async def run(self):
        return {
            "candidates": 0,
            "attempted": 0,
            "succeeded": 0,
            "failed": 0,
            "skipped": 0,
        }


def test_academy_workflow_is_registered_on_existing_scheduler() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()
    academy = FakeAcademyWorkflow()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
        academy_one_week_workflow=academy,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    assert len(scheduler.jobs) == 3

    academy_jobs = [
        job
        for job in scheduler.jobs
        if job["id"] == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    ]

    assert len(academy_jobs) == 1

    job = academy_jobs[0]

    assert job["function"] == academy.run
    assert job["trigger"] == "cron"
    assert job["hour"] == 8
    assert job["minute"] == 15
    assert job["replace_existing"] is True


def test_existing_jobs_remain_registered() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
        academy_one_week_workflow=FakeAcademyWorkflow(),
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    job_ids = {
        job["id"]
        for job in scheduler.jobs
    }

    assert job_ids == {
        "cybersecurity_daily_report",
        "cybersecurity_weekly_report",
        "academy_one_week_milestone",
    }


def test_academy_registration_is_optional() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    assert len(scheduler.jobs) == 2

    job_ids = {
        job["id"]
        for job in scheduler.jobs
    }

    assert "academy_one_week_milestone" not in job_ids


def test_academy_schedule_is_configurable() -> None:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()

    adapter = CybersecurityAPSchedulerAdapter(
        scheduler=scheduler,
        workflow_service=FakeCybersecurityWorkflow(),
        academy_one_week_workflow=FakeAcademyWorkflow(),
        academy_one_week_hour=9,
        academy_one_week_minute=30,
    )

    adapter.register_jobs()

    academy_job = next(
        job
        for job in scheduler.jobs
        if job["id"] == "academy_one_week_milestone"
    )

    assert academy_job["hour"] == 9
    assert academy_job["minute"] == 30

"""
Tests for the LiveKit Voice Worker.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from unittest.mock import AsyncMock, Mock, patch

import pytest
from livekit.agents import JobContext

from app.ai_runtime.conversation.conversation_runtime import (
    ConversationRuntime,
)
from app.ai_runtime.voice.voice_speech_runtime import (
    VoiceSpeechRuntime,
)
from app.infrastructure.voice.livekit_voice_worker import (
    LiveKitVoiceWorker,
)
from app.application.uai.context.interaction_context_builder import (
    InteractionContextBuilder,
)


def create_job_context(
    *,
    metadata: dict[str, object] | None = None,
) -> Mock:
    ctx = Mock(spec=JobContext)

    ctx.job = Mock()
    ctx.job.metadata = (
        json.dumps(metadata)
        if metadata is not None
        else ""
    )

    ctx.room = Mock()
    ctx.room.name = "test-room"

    ctx.connect = AsyncMock()

    return ctx


def create_interaction_context() -> Mock:
    character = Mock()
    character.name = "Yeti"
    character.personality_reference = "friendly"
    character.teaching_style = "supportive"

    context = Mock()
    context.character = character

    return context


def create_worker() -> LiveKitVoiceWorker:
    return LiveKitVoiceWorker(
        context_factory=Mock(),
        interaction_context_builder=Mock(
            spec=InteractionContextBuilder,
        ),
        conversation_runtime=Mock(
            spec=ConversationRuntime,
        ),
        speech_runtime=Mock(
            spec=VoiceSpeechRuntime,
        ),
    )


def test_read_job_metadata_requires_metadata():
    ctx = create_job_context()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="LiveKit job metadata is required",
    ):
        LiveKitVoiceWorker._read_job_metadata(ctx)


def test_read_job_metadata_requires_valid_json():
    ctx = create_job_context()
    ctx.job.metadata = "{invalid-json"

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="must be valid JSON",
    ):
        LiveKitVoiceWorker._read_job_metadata(ctx)


def test_read_job_metadata_requires_json_object():
    ctx = create_job_context()
    ctx.job.metadata = json.dumps(
        ["invalid"],
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="must be a JSON object",
    ):
        LiveKitVoiceWorker._read_job_metadata(ctx)


def test_create_voice_call_request_requires_all_fields():
    metadata = {
        "learner_id": "learner-1",
        "world_id": "academy",
        "character_id": "yeti",
    }

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="'language_code' must be a string",
    ):
        LiveKitVoiceWorker._create_voice_call_request(
            metadata,
        )


def test_create_voice_call_request_rejects_empty_values():
    metadata = {
        "learner_id": "learner-1",
        "world_id": "academy",
        "character_id": "yeti",
        "language_code": " ",
    }

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="'language_code' cannot be empty",
    ):
        LiveKitVoiceWorker._create_voice_call_request(
            metadata,
        )


def test_create_voice_call_request_preserves_values():
    metadata = {
        "learner_id": " learner-1 ",
        "world_id": " academy ",
        "character_id": " yeti ",
        "language_code": " fr ",
    }

    request = (
        LiveKitVoiceWorker._create_voice_call_request(
            metadata,
        )
    )

    assert request.learner_id == "learner-1"
    assert request.world_id == "academy"
    assert request.character_id == "yeti"
    assert request.language_code == "fr"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_run_wires_conversation_and_speech_runtime():
    worker = create_worker()

    ctx = create_job_context(
        metadata={
            "learner_id": "learner-1",
            "world_id": "academy",
            "character_id": "yeti",
            "language_code": "fr",
        }
    )

    execution_context = Mock()
    interaction_context = (
        create_interaction_context()
    )

    worker.context_factory.create.return_value = (
        execution_context
    )
    worker.interaction_context_builder.build.return_value = (
        interaction_context
    )

    agent = Mock()
    session = Mock()
    session.start = AsyncMock()

    with patch(
        "app.infrastructure.voice.livekit_voice_worker.LiveKitRealtimeAgent"
    ) as realtime_agent_class:
        realtime_agent = (
            realtime_agent_class.return_value
        )
        realtime_agent.create_agent.return_value = agent
        realtime_agent.create_session.return_value = (
            session
        )

        await worker.run(ctx=ctx)

    realtime_agent_class.assert_called_once_with(
        context=interaction_context,
        instructions=(
            "You are Yeti. "
            "You are a French learning character. "
            "Your personality reference is friendly. "
            "Your teaching style is supportive. "
            "Speak naturally and help the learner "
            "practice the target language."
        ),
        conversation_runtime=worker.conversation_runtime,
        speech_runtime=worker.speech_runtime,
    )

    ctx.connect.assert_awaited_once()

    realtime_agent.create_agent.assert_called_once()
    realtime_agent.create_session.assert_called_once()

    session.start.assert_awaited_once_with(
        agent=agent,
        room=ctx.room,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_run_rejects_invalid_metadata_before_connecting():
    worker = create_worker()

    ctx = create_job_context(
        metadata={
            "learner_id": "learner-1",
            "world_id": "academy",
            "character_id": "yeti",
        }
    )

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="'language_code' must be a string",
    ):
        await worker.run(ctx=ctx)

    ctx.connect.assert_not_awaited()

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)

pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def required():
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


async def test_delivery_lifecycle_is_durably_audited():
    required()
    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-audit-lifecycle-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        factory = create_async_session_factory(engine)
        delivery = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(factory)
        audit = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRepository(factory)

        await delivery.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id="REC-AUDIT",
            risk_decision_id="DEC-AUDIT",
            review_item_id="REV-AUDIT",
            correlation_id="CORR-AUDIT",
        )
        await delivery.mark_failed(
            notification_key=key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )
        await delivery.start_retry(notification_key=key)
        await delivery.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=key,
            provider_message_id="provider-audit-2",
        )

        events = await audit.list_by_notification_key(key)

        assert [event.status for event in events] == [
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED,
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.FAILED,
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED,
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.SUCCEEDED,
        ]
        assert [event.attempt for event in events] == [1, 1, 2, 2]
        assert events[1].error_code == "PROVIDER_ERROR"
        assert events[-1].provider_message_id == "provider-audit-2"
    finally:
        async with create_async_session_factory(engine)() as session:
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit
                    WHERE notification_key = :key
                    """
                ),
                {"key": key},
            )
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key = :key
                    """
                ),
                {"key": key},
            )
            await session.commit()
        await engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _database_required() -> None:
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


async def test_get_missing_notification_returns_none() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        assert await repository.get_by_notification_key(
            f"pi-test-missing-{uuid4()}"
        ) is None
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_started_success_and_idempotent_record_round_trip() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-test-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        started = await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id="recommendation-test",
            risk_decision_id="decision-test",
            review_item_id="review-test",
            correlation_id="correlation-test",
        )

        assert started.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        assert started.attempt == 1

        succeeded = await repository.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=key,
            provider_message_id="provider-message-1",
        )

        assert (
            succeeded.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        )
        assert succeeded.provider_message_id == "provider-message-1"
        assert succeeded.attempt == 1

        loaded = await repository.get_by_notification_key(key)

        assert loaded is not None
        assert (
            loaded.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        )
        assert loaded.provider_message_id == "provider-message-1"
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_failed_delivery_can_retry_with_incremented_attempt() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-test-retry-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id="recommendation-retry",
            risk_decision_id="decision-retry",
            review_item_id="review-retry",
            correlation_id="correlation-retry",
        )

        failed = await repository.mark_failed(
            notification_key=key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )

        assert failed.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        assert failed.attempt == 1
        assert failed.error_code == "PROVIDER_ERROR"

        retried = await repository.start_retry(
            notification_key=key,
        )

        assert retried.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        assert retried.attempt == 2
        assert retried.provider_message_id is None
        assert retried.error_code is None
    finally:
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_list_retry_candidates_returns_only_failed_and_started_in_deterministic_order() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    suffix = uuid4()

    started_key = f"pi-test-candidate-started-{suffix}"
    failed_key = f"pi-test-candidate-failed-{suffix}"
    succeeded_key = f"pi-test-candidate-succeeded-{suffix}"

    test_keys = (
        started_key,
        failed_key,
        succeeded_key,
    )

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=started_key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-started-{suffix}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-started-{suffix}",
            review_item_id=f"review-started-{suffix}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-started-{suffix}",
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=failed_key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-failed-{suffix}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-failed-{suffix}",
            review_item_id=f"review-failed-{suffix}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-failed-{suffix}",
        )

        await repository.mark_failed(
            notification_key=failed_key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=succeeded_key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-succeeded-{suffix}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-succeeded-{suffix}",
            review_item_id=f"review-succeeded-{suffix}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-succeeded-{suffix}",
        )

        await repository.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=succeeded_key,
            provider_message_id="provider-message-succeeded",
        )

        # Establish a deterministic ordering for this test's records.
        #
        # The production repository deliberately returns the global retry
        # candidate set. This test therefore retrieves a sufficiently large
        # bounded batch and then isolates its own records for assertions.
        older = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=5)
        newer = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(minutes=1)

        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    UPDATE platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    SET updated_at = :updated_at
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "updated_at": older,
                    "notification_key": failed_key,
                },
            )

            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    UPDATE platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    SET updated_at = :updated_at
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "updated_at": newer,
                    "notification_key": started_key,
                },
            )

            await session.commit()

        # The table is shared across integration-test runs, so previous
        # FAILED/STARTED records may legitimately exist. Do not let those
        # unrelated records consume the small production-style limit used
        # by the test.
        candidates = await repository.list_retry_candidates(
            limit=1000,
        )

        candidate_by_key = {
            record.notification_key: record
            for record in candidates
            if record.notification_key in test_keys
        }

        assert failed_key in candidate_by_key
        assert started_key in candidate_by_key
        assert succeeded_key not in candidate_by_key

        own_candidate_keys = [
            record.notification_key
            for record in candidates
            if record.notification_key in test_keys
        ]

        assert own_candidate_keys == [
            failed_key,
            started_key,
        ]

        assert all(
            record.status
            in (
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
            )
            for record in (
                candidate_by_key[failed_key],
                candidate_by_key[started_key],
            )
        )
    finally:
        # Clean up only this test's own records.
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key IN (
                        :started_key,
                        :failed_key,
                        :succeeded_key
                    )
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "started_key": started_key,
                    "failed_key": failed_key,
                    "succeeded_key": succeeded_key,
                },
            )
            await session.commit()

        await engine.dispose()


async def test_list_retry_candidates_respects_limit() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    suffix = uuid4()

    keys = [
        f"pi-test-candidate-limit-{suffix}-{index}"
        for index in range(3)
    ]

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        for index, key in enumerate(keys):
            await repository.create_started(
                notification_key=key,
                recommendation_id=f"recommendation-limit-{suffix}-{index}",
                risk_decision_id=f"decision-limit-{suffix}-{index}",
                review_item_id=f"review-limit-{suffix}-{index}",
                correlation_id=f"correlation-limit-{suffix}-{index}",
            )

            await repository.mark_failed(
                notification_key=key,
                error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
            )

        # Use a sufficiently large query window so unrelated historical
        # integration-test rows cannot prevent this test's own records from
        # being discovered.
        candidates = await repository.list_retry_candidates(
            limit=1000,
        )

        own_candidates = [
            record
            for record in candidates
            if record.notification_key in keys
        ]

        assert len(own_candidates) == 3

        # Validate the actual repository limit independently.
        limited_candidates = await repository.list_retry_candidates(
            limit=2,
        )

        assert len(limited_candidates) <= 2
    finally:
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key IN (
                        :key_0,
                        :key_1,
                        :key_2
                    )
                    """
                ),
                {
                    "key_0": keys[0],
                    "key_1": keys[1],
                    "key_2": keys[2],
                },
            )
            await session.commit()

        await engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import os
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _database_required() -> None:
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


class FakeEmailWorkflow:
    """Deterministic email-workflow double; no external provider is called."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        provider_message_id: str = "integration-provider-message-1",
        error: Exception | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.calls: list[object] = []
        self.provider_message_id = provider_message_id
        self.error = error

    async def send(self, request: object) -> dict[str, str]:
        self.calls.append(request)

        if self.error is not None:
            raise self.error

        return {
            "status": "accepted",
            "provider_message_id": self.provider_message_id,
        }


def _make_request(
    recommendation_id: str,
    *,
    correlation_id: str | None = None,
) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest:
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id=recommendation_id,
        risk_decision_id=f"risk-{recommendation_id}",
        review_item_id=f"review-{recommendation_id}",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:"
            f"{recommendation_id}"
        ),
        correlation_id=correlation_id or f"correlation-{recommendation_id}",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk level.",),
    )


def _new_repository(engine):
    return SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
        create_async_session_factory(engine)
    )


async def _dispose(engine) -> None:
    await engine.dispose()


async def test_dispatch_persists_success_and_replays_after_new_service_instance() -> None:
    _database_required()

    recommendation_id = f"pi-e2e-replay-{uuid4()}"
    engine_one = create_async_database_engine()

    try:
        repository_one = _new_repository(engine_one)
        workflow_one = FakeEmailWorkflow(
            provider_message_id="provider-message-restart-safe",
        )
        service_one = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
            delivery_repository=repository_one,
            email_workflow=workflow_one,
        )

        first = await service_one.dispatch(
            request=_make_request(recommendation_id),
            email_request=object(),
        )

        assert first.status.value == "SENT"
        assert first.provider_message_id == "provider-message-restart-safe"
        assert len(workflow_one.calls) == 1

        persisted = await repository_one.get_by_notification_key(
            first.notification_key
        )
        assert persisted is not None
        assert persisted.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        assert persisted.attempt == 1
        assert persisted.provider_message_id == "provider-message-restart-safe"
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine_one)

    # Simulate a new application/repository instance after restart.
    engine_two = create_async_database_engine()

    try:
        repository_two = _new_repository(engine_two)
        workflow_two = FakeEmailWorkflow(
            provider_message_id="must-not-be-used",
        )
        service_two = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
            delivery_repository=repository_two,
            email_workflow=workflow_two,
        )

        second = await service_two.dispatch(
            request=_make_request(recommendation_id),
            email_request=object(),
        )

        assert second.status.value == "IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY"
        assert second.provider_message_id == "provider-message-restart-safe"
        assert second.attempt == 1
        assert workflow_two.calls == []

        persisted_after_restart = (
            await repository_two.get_by_notification_key(
                second.notification_key
            )
        )
        assert persisted_after_restart is not None
        assert (
            persisted_after_restart.status
            is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        )
        assert persisted_after_restart.attempt == 1
        assert (
            persisted_after_restart.provider_message_id
            == "provider-message-restart-safe"
        )
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine_two)


async def test_started_delivery_is_retried_after_service_restart() -> None:
    _database_required()

    recommendation_id = f"pi-e2e-started-retry-{uuid4()}"
    request = _make_request(recommendation_id)
    engine_one = create_async_database_engine()

    try:
        repository_one = _new_repository(engine_one)

        started = await repository_one.create_started(
            notification_key=request.notification_key,
            recommendation_id=request.recommendation_id,
            risk_decision_id=request.risk_decision_id,
            review_item_id=request.review_item_id,
            correlation_id=request.correlation_id,
        )

        assert started.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        assert started.attempt == 1
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine_one)

    # The STARTED record represents an interrupted attempt. A new service
    # instance must recover it and complete delivery without creating a row.
    engine_two = create_async_database_engine()

    try:
        repository_two = _new_repository(engine_two)
        workflow_two = FakeEmailWorkflow(
            provider_message_id="provider-message-recovered",
        )
        service_two = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
            delivery_repository=repository_two,
            email_workflow=workflow_two,
        )

        result = await service_two.dispatch(
            request=request,
            email_request=object(),
        )

        assert result.status.value == "SENT"
        assert result.provider_message_id == "provider-message-recovered"
        assert result.attempt == 1
        assert len(workflow_two.calls) == 1

        persisted = await repository_two.get_by_notification_key(
            request.notification_key
        )
        assert persisted is not None
        assert persisted.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        assert persisted.attempt == 1
        assert persisted.provider_message_id == "provider-message-recovered"
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine_two)


async def test_duplicate_notification_key_is_protected_by_postgresql() -> None:
    _database_required()

    recommendation_id = f"pi-e2e-duplicate-{uuid4()}"
    notification_key = (
        "platform_intelligence_escalation:"
        f"{recommendation_id}"
    )
    engine = create_async_database_engine()

    async def create(repository):
        return await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=notification_key,
            recommendation_id=recommendation_id,
            risk_decision_id=f"risk-{recommendation_id}",
            review_item_id=f"review-{recommendation_id}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-{recommendation_id}",
        )

    try:
        repository_one = _new_repository(engine)
        repository_two = _new_repository(engine)

        results = await asyncio.gather(
            create(repository_one),
            create(repository_two),
            return_exceptions=True,
        )

        successes = [result for result in results if not isinstance(result, Exception)]
        failures = [result for result in results if isinstance(result, Exception)]

        assert len(successes) == 1
        assert len(failures) == 1

        persisted = await repository_one.get_by_notification_key(
            notification_key
        )
        assert persisted is not None
        assert persisted.status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        assert persisted.attempt == 1
    finally:
        await _dispose(engine)

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.infrastructure.operations_center.sqlalchemy_platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_repository import (
    SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import (
    create_async_database_engine,
    create_async_session_factory,
)


pytestmark = pytest.mark.asyncio


def _database_required() -> None:
    if not os.getenv("DATABASE_URL"):
        pytest.skip("DATABASE_URL is required for PostgreSQL integration tests.")


async def test_claim_retry_candidate_is_atomic_and_reclaimable_after_lease_expiry() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-claim-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-{uuid4()}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-{uuid4()}",
            review_item_id=f"review-{uuid4()}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-{uuid4()}",
        )
        await repository.mark_failed(
            notification_key=key,
            error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
        )

        first_token = str(uuid4())
        first_claimed_at = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        first_expiry = first_claimed_at + timedelta(minutes=5)

        first = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=first_token,
            claimed_at=first_claimed_at,
            lease_expires_at=first_expiry,
        )
        assert first is True

        second = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=str(uuid4()),
            claimed_at=first_claimed_at + timedelta(seconds=1),
            lease_expires_at=first_expiry + timedelta(seconds=1),
        )
        assert second is False

        third_token = str(uuid4())
        third_claimed_at = first_expiry + timedelta(seconds=1)
        third = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=third_token,
            claimed_at=third_claimed_at,
            lease_expires_at=third_claimed_at + timedelta(minutes=5),
        )
        assert third is True

        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            row = (
                await session.execute(
                    text(
                        """
                        SELECT claim_token, claimed_at, lease_expires_at
                        FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                        WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                        """
                    ),
                    {"notification_key": key},
                )
            ).mappings().one()

        assert row["claim_token"] == third_token
        assert row["claimed_at"] == third_claimed_at
    finally:
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": key},
            )
            await session.commit()
        await engine.dispose()


async def test_claim_does_not_claim_succeeded_delivery() -> None:
    _database_required()

    engine = create_async_database_engine()
    key = f"pi-claim-success-{uuid4()}"

    try:
        repository = SqlAlchemyPlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryRepository(
            create_async_session_factory(engine)
        )

        await repository.create_started(
            notification_key=key,
            recommendation_id=f"recommendation-{uuid4()}",
            risk_decision_id=f"decision-{uuid4()}",
            review_item_id=f"review-{uuid4()}",
            correlation_id=f"correlation-{uuid4()}",
        )
        await repository.mark_succeeded(
            notification_key=key,
            provider_message_id="provider-message",
        )

        claimed = await repository.claim_retry_candidate(
            notification_key=key,
            claim_token=str(uuid4()),
            claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
            lease_expires_at=datetime(
                2026, 9, 22, 12, 5, tzinfo=timezone.utc
            ),
        )

        assert claimed is False
    finally:
        async with repository._session_factory() as session:  # noqa: SLF001
            await session.execute(
                text(
                    """
                    DELETE FROM platform_intelligence_escalation_deliveries
                    WHERE notification_key = :notification_key
                    """
                ),
                {"notification_key": key},
            )
            await session.commit()
        await engine.dispose()

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.api.dependencies.authorization import require_permission
from app.api.dependencies.identity import get_current_principal
from app.api.operations_center_email_workflows import (
    create_email_workflows_router,
)
from app.domain.authorization.permissions import Permission
from app.domain.identity.principal import AccountStatus, Principal


class FakeScalarResult:
    """Minimal scalar result returned by the fake SQLAlchemy session."""

    def all(self):
        return []


class FakeSession:
    """
    Minimal read-only SQLAlchemy Session contract required by the
    PostgreSQL email workflow registry.
    """

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc_value, traceback):
        return False

    def scalars(self, _statement):
        return FakeScalarResult()


class FakeSessionFactory:
    """Deterministic session factory for authorization tests."""

    def __call__(self):
        return FakeSession()


class FakeScheduler:
    """Minimal scheduler contract required by EmailWorkflowPortfolioService."""

    running = True

    def get_jobs(self):
        return []


class FakeSchedulerAdapter:
    scheduler = FakeScheduler()


class FakeSchedulerRuntime:
    scheduler_adapter = FakeSchedulerAdapter()


def make_principal(
    *,
    roles: frozenset[str],
) -> Principal:
    return Principal(
        user_id="email-workflow-authorization-test-user",
        email="operator@example.test",
        email_verified=True,
        account_status=AccountStatus.ACTIVE,
        roles=roles,
    )


def make_router():
    return create_email_workflows_router(
        session_factory=FakeSessionFactory(),
        operator_dependency=require_permission(
            Permission.INTERNAL_DATA_READ,
        ),
    )


def make_app(
    *,
    principal: Principal,
) -> FastAPI:
    application = FastAPI()

    application.state.scheduler_runtime = FakeSchedulerRuntime()

    application.include_router(
        make_router(),
    )

    # Override only the identity dependency.
    #
    # This preserves the real authorization path:
    #
    # get_current_principal
    #       -> require_permission(...)
    #       -> AuthorizationService
    #       -> PermissionDeniedError
    #       -> HTTP 403
    application.dependency_overrides[
        get_current_principal
    ] = lambda: principal

    return application


def test_beta_user_cannot_read_email_workflows():
    application = make_app(
        principal=make_principal(
            roles=frozenset({"beta_user"}),
        ),
    )

    with TestClient(application) as client:
        response = client.get(
            "/api/operations-center/email-workflows",
        )

    assert response.status_code == 403
    assert response.json() == {
        "detail": "Insufficient permission.",
    }


def test_operator_can_read_email_workflows():

        ),
    )

    with TestClient(application) as client:

        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    payload = response.json()

    assert payload["status"] == "ready"
    assert payload["count"] == 0
    assert payload["workflows"] == []

def test_email_workflow_router_protects_collection_endpoint():
    router = make_router()

    routes = [
        route
        for route in router.routes
        if hasattr(route, "path")
        and route.path
        == "/api/operations-center/email-workflows"
    ]

    assert len(routes) == 1

    route = routes[0]

    assert route.methods == {"GET"}

    dependency_calls = {
        dependency.call
        for dependency in route.dependant.dependencies
    }

    assert len(dependency_calls) == 1

    permission_dependency = next(
        iter(dependency_calls)
    )

    assert permission_dependency is not None


def test_email_workflow_router_protects_item_endpoint():
    router = make_router()

    routes = [
        route
        for route in router.routes
        if hasattr(route, "path")
        and route.path
        == "/api/operations-center/email-workflows/{workflow_id}"
    ]

    assert len(routes) == 1

    route = routes[0]

    assert route.methods == {"GET"}

    dependency_calls = {
        dependency.call
        for dependency in route.dependant.dependencies
    }

    assert len(dependency_calls) == 1

    permission_dependency = next(
        iter(dependency_calls)
    )

    assert permission_dependency is not None

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.api.dependencies.identity import get_current_principal
from app.api.operations_center import create_operations_center_router
from app.application.provider_management.functionality_operational_control_service import (
    OperationalControlResult,
)
from app.domain.identity.principal import (
    AccountStatus,
    Principal,
)


class FakeControlService:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls: list[dict] = []

    def stop(
        self,
        *,
        actor,
        actor_name: str,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
        reason: str,
    ) -> OperationalControlResult:
        self.calls.append(
            {
                "action": "stop",
                "actor": actor,
                "actor_name": actor_name,
                "environment": environment,
                "functionality_id": functionality_id,
                "reason": reason,
            }
        )

        return OperationalControlResult(
            functionality_id=functionality_id,
            environment=environment,
            availability="disabled",
            action="stop",
            reason=reason,
            changed_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            changed_by=actor_name,
        )

    def resume(
        self,
        *,
        actor,
        actor_name: str,
        environment: str,
        functionality_id: str,
    ) -> OperationalControlResult:
        self.calls.append(
            {
                "action": "resume",
                "actor": actor,
                "actor_name": actor_name,
                "environment": environment,
                "functionality_id": functionality_id,
            }
        )

        return OperationalControlResult(
            functionality_id=functionality_id,
            environment=environment,
            availability="enabled",
            action="resume",
            reason=None,
            changed_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
            changed_by=actor_name,
        )


class FakeComposition:
    def __init__(self, control_service) -> None:
        self.dashboard_api = object()
        self.chat_operations_api = object()
        self.functionality_health_service = object()
        self.functionality_control_service = control_service
        self.event_service = object()
        self.provider_usage_dashboard_service = None


def make_principal(role: str) -> Principal:
    return Principal(
        user_id="test-user",
        email="test@example.com",
        email_verified=True,
        account_status=AccountStatus.ACTIVE,
        roles=frozenset({role}),
    )


def create_test_app(
    *,
    principal: Principal | None,
    control_service=None,
) -> FastAPI:
    if control_service is None:
        control_service = FakeControlService()

    composition = FakeComposition(control_service)

    app = FastAPI()

    app.include_router(
        create_operations_center_router(
            composition=composition,
        )
    )

    async def override_principal() -> Principal:
        if principal is None:
            from fastapi import HTTPException, status

            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
                detail="Authentication required.",
            )

        return principal

    app.dependency_overrides[get_current_principal] = override_principal

    return app

    control_service = FakeControlService()

    app = create_test_app(
        principal=make_principal("operator"),
        control_service=control_service,
    )

    with TestClient(app) as client:
        response = client.post(
            "/api/operations-center/functionality/conversation/stop",
            json={
                "environment": "kids",
                "reason": "Provider incident",
            },
        )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    payload = response.json()

    assert payload["functionality_id"] == "conversation"
    assert payload["environment"] == "kids"
    assert payload["availability"] == "disabled"
    assert payload["action"] == "stop"
    assert payload["reason"] == "Provider incident"

    assert len(control_service.calls) == 1
