        )

    assert response.status_code == 503


===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\operations_center\test_m2_1_functionality_control_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest.mock import Mock

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Repository import boundary
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
#
# The Hackathon repository has two legitimate Python roots:
#
#   <repo>\backend -> app.*
#   <repo>        -> src.*
#
# backend MUST take precedence for app.* while the repository root must also
# be available for legitimate app.* -> src.* dependencies.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

TEST_FILE = Path(__file__).resolve()
BACKEND_ROOT = TEST_FILE.parents[2]
REPOSITORY_ROOT = BACKEND_ROOT.parent

backend_root = str(BACKEND_ROOT)
repository_root = str(REPOSITORY_ROOT)

sys.path[:] = [
    path
    for path in sys.path
    if path not in {backend_root, repository_root}
]

sys.path.insert(0, backend_root)
sys.path.insert(1, repository_root)


from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.api.dependencies.identity import get_current_principal
from app.api.operations_center import create_operations_center_router
from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    OperationsCenterComposition,
)
from app.domain.identity.principal import AccountStatus, Principal


FUNCTIONALITY_ID = "voice_tts"
ENVIRONMENT = "kids"
ACTOR_NAME = "test-admin"

STOP_URL = (
    f"/api/operations-center/functionality/{FUNCTIONALITY_ID}/stop"
)

RESUME_URL = (
    f"/api/operations-center/functionality/{FUNCTIONALITY_ID}/resume"
)


def _principal() -> Principal:
    """Return a valid active principal for authorization tests."""
    return Principal(
        user_id=ACTOR_NAME,
        email="test-admin@example.test",
        email_verified=True,
        account_status=AccountStatus.ACTIVE,
        roles=frozenset({"admin"}),
    )


def _operational_result(
    *,
    functionality_id: str = FUNCTIONALITY_ID,
    environment: str = ENVIRONMENT,
    availability: str = "disabled",
    action: str = "stop",
    reason: str | None = "maintenance",
    changed_by: str = ACTOR_NAME,
) -> SimpleNamespace:
    """Create the result contract consumed by the API serializer."""
    return SimpleNamespace(
        functionality_id=functionality_id,
        environment=environment,
        availability=availability,
        action=action,
        reason=reason,
        changed_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        changed_by=changed_by,
    )


def _build_client(
    *,
    functionality_control_service: object | None,
) -> tuple[TestClient, object | None]:
    """
    Build the API using the real OperationsCenterComposition.

    This deliberately avoids a partial SimpleNamespace composition.

    The real composition owns the complete Operations Center dependency
    graph. The functionality-control service is the only dependency replaced
    by the test double because that is the capability under test.
    """
    composition = OperationsCenterComposition(
        functionality_control_service=functionality_control_service,
    )

    router = create_operations_center_router(
        composition=composition,
    )

    application = FastAPI()
    application.include_router(router)

    # Keep the real require_permission() dependency active.
    #
    # The identity dependency is the only authentication boundary replaced
    # here. AuthorizationService and FUNCTIONALITY_CONTROL permission
    # evaluation remain production code.
    application.dependency_overrides[get_current_principal] = _principal

    return TestClient(application), functionality_control_service


def test_stop_functionality_returns_operational_control_result() -> None:
    service = Mock(name="functionality_control_service")
    service.stop.return_value = _operational_result()

    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=service,
    )

    response = client.post(
        STOP_URL,
        json={
            "environment": ENVIRONMENT,
            "reason": "maintenance",
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert body["functionality_id"] == FUNCTIONALITY_ID
    assert body["environment"] == ENVIRONMENT
    assert body["availability"] == "disabled"
    assert body["action"] == "stop"
    assert body["reason"] == "maintenance"
    assert body["changed_by"] == ACTOR_NAME
    assert body["changed_at"]

    assert control_service is service

    service.stop.assert_called_once_with(
        actor=_principal(),
        actor_name=ACTOR_NAME,
        environment=ENVIRONMENT,
        functionality_id=FUNCTIONALITY_ID,
        reason="maintenance",
    )


def test_resume_functionality_returns_operational_control_result() -> None:
    service = Mock(name="functionality_control_service")
    service.resume.return_value = _operational_result(
        availability="enabled",
        action="resume",
        reason=None,
    )

    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=service,
    )

    response = client.post(
        RESUME_URL,
        json={
            "environment": ENVIRONMENT,
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert body["functionality_id"] == FUNCTIONALITY_ID
    assert body["environment"] == ENVIRONMENT
    assert body["availability"] == "enabled"
    assert body["action"] == "resume"
    assert body["reason"] is None
    assert body["changed_by"] == ACTOR_NAME
    assert body["changed_at"]

    assert control_service is service

    service.resume.assert_called_once_with(
        actor=_principal(),
        actor_name=ACTOR_NAME,
        environment=ENVIRONMENT,
        functionality_id=FUNCTIONALITY_ID,
    )


def test_stop_requires_reason() -> None:
    service = Mock(name="functionality_control_service")

    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=service,
    )

    response = client.post(
        STOP_URL,
        json={
            "environment": ENVIRONMENT,
            "reason": "",
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 422
    service.stop.assert_not_called()

    assert control_service is service


def test_stop_maps_service_value_error_to_bad_request() -> None:
    service = Mock(name="functionality_control_service")
    service.stop.side_effect = ValueError(
        "A reason is required to stop a functionality."
    )

    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=service,
    )

    response = client.post(
        STOP_URL,
        json={
            "environment": ENVIRONMENT,
            "reason": "maintenance",
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 400
    assert response.json()["detail"] == (
        "A reason is required to stop a functionality."
    )

    assert control_service is service

    service.stop.assert_called_once_with(
        actor=_principal(),
        actor_name=ACTOR_NAME,
        environment=ENVIRONMENT,
        functionality_id=FUNCTIONALITY_ID,
        reason="maintenance",
    )


def test_resume_maps_service_permission_error_to_forbidden() -> None:
    service = Mock(name="functionality_control_service")
    service.resume.side_effect = PermissionError(
        "Not authorised to control this functionality."
    )

    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=service,
    )

    response = client.post(
        RESUME_URL,
        json={
            "environment": ENVIRONMENT,
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 403
    assert response.json()["detail"] == "Insufficient permission."

    assert control_service is service

    service.resume.assert_called_once_with(
        actor=_principal(),
        actor_name=ACTOR_NAME,
        environment=ENVIRONMENT,
        functionality_id=FUNCTIONALITY_ID,
    )


def test_stop_maps_service_permission_error_to_forbidden() -> None:
    service = Mock(name="functionality_control_service")
    service.stop.side_effect = PermissionError(
        "Not authorised to control this functionality."
    )

    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=service,
    )

    response = client.post(
        STOP_URL,
        json={
            "environment": ENVIRONMENT,
            "reason": "maintenance",
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 403
    assert response.json()["detail"] == "Insufficient permission."

    assert control_service is service

    service.stop.assert_called_once_with(
        actor=_principal(),
        actor_name=ACTOR_NAME,
        environment=ENVIRONMENT,
        functionality_id=FUNCTIONALITY_ID,
        reason="maintenance",
    )


def test_invalid_environment_is_rejected_by_request_schema() -> None:
    service = Mock(name="functionality_control_service")

    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=service,
    )

    response = client.post(
        STOP_URL,
        json={
            "environment": "invalid",
            "reason": "maintenance",
        },
    )

    # OperationsEnvironment is a Pydantic request-schema enum.
    # Invalid values therefore fail before the application service executes.
    assert response.status_code == 422

    assert control_service is service
    service.stop.assert_not_called()


def test_control_service_unavailable_returns_503() -> None:
    # None is intentional. This exercises the real endpoint guard:
    #
    # if functionality_control_service is None:
    #     raise _control_unavailable()
    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=None,
    )

    response = client.post(
        STOP_URL,
        json={
            "environment": ENVIRONMENT,
            "reason": "maintenance",
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 503
    assert response.json()["detail"] == (
        "Functionality operational control is not configured."
    )

    assert control_service is None


def test_resume_does_not_require_reason() -> None:
    service = Mock(name="functionality_control_service")
    service.resume.return_value = _operational_result(
        availability="enabled",
        action="resume",
        reason=None,
    )

    client, control_service = _build_client(
        functionality_control_service=service,
    )

    response = client.post(
        RESUME_URL,
        json={
            "environment": ENVIRONMENT,
        },
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    body = response.json()

    assert body["action"] == "resume"
    assert body["availability"] == "enabled"
    assert body["reason"] is None

    assert control_service is service

    service.resume.assert_called_once_with(
        actor=_principal(),
        actor_name=ACTOR_NAME,
        environment=ENVIRONMENT,
        functionality_id=FUNCTIONALITY_ID,
    )

from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.api.operations_center import create_operations_center_router
from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import OperationsCenterComposition


def client() -> TestClient:
    app = FastAPI()

def test_legacy_endpoint_defaults_to_adults() -> None:
    response = client().get("/api/operations-center/functionality-health")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["environment"] == "adults"


def test_kids_environment() -> None:
    response = client().get("/api/operations-center/functionality-health?environment=kids")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["environment"] == "kids"


def test_adults_environment() -> None:
    response = client().get("/api/operations-center/functionality-health?environment=adults")
    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json()["environment"] == "adults"


def test_unknown_environment_is_rejected() -> None:
    response = client().get("/api/operations-center/functionality-health?environment=unknown")
    assert response.status_code == 422

from __future__ import annotations

from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.api.operations_center import (
    create_operations_center_router,
)
from app.application.health.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventType,
    OperationalSeverity,
    OperationalSurface,
)
from app.application.operations_center.dashboard_composition import (
    OperationsCenterComposition,
)


def make_client() -> tuple[TestClient, OperationsCenterComposition]:
    composition = OperationsCenterComposition()

    from fastapi import FastAPI

    app = FastAPI()
    app.include_router(
        create_operations_center_router(
            composition=composition,
        )
    )

    return TestClient(app), composition


def test_operational_events_endpoint_returns_empty_list_when_no_events():
    client, _composition = make_client()

    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/operational-events"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200
    assert response.json() == []


def test_operational_events_endpoint_serializes_recorded_events():
    client, composition = make_client()

    composition.event_service.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE,
            surface=OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM,
            correlation_id="api-test-001",
            severity=OperationalSeverity.HIGH,
            metadata={
                "safe_status": "timeout",
            },
        )
    )

    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/operational-events"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    payload = response.json()

    assert len(payload) == 1
    assert payload[0]["event_type"] == "message_failure"
    assert payload[0]["surface"] == "private_classroom"
    assert payload[0]["correlation_id"] == "api-test-001"
    assert payload[0]["severity"] == "high"
    assert payload[0]["metadata"]["safe_status"] == "timeout"


def test_operational_events_endpoint_flattens_correlated_events():
    client, composition = make_client()

    for correlation_id in (
        "api-test-002",
        "api-test-003",
    ):
        composition.event_service.record_event(
            OperationalEvent(
                event_type=OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE,
                surface=OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM,
                correlation_id=correlation_id,
            )
        )

    response = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/operational-events"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 200

    payload = response.json()

    assert len(payload) == 2

    correlation_ids = {
        event["correlation_id"]
        for event in payload
    }

    assert correlation_ids == {
        "api-test-002",
        "api-test-003",
    }


===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\operations_center\test_persistence_operations_api.py =====
from fastapi import FastAPI
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.api.routes.persistence_operations_routes import (
    create_persistence_operations_router,
)


class FakeService:
    def snapshot(self, *, environment: str):
        return {"environment": environment, "kind": "snapshot"}

    def health_snapshot(self, *, environment: str):
        return {"environment": environment, "kind": "health"}


def make_client():
    app = FastAPI()

    def operator_dependency():
        return {"role": "operator"}

    app.include_router(
        create_persistence_operations_router(
            FakeService(),
            operator_dependency=operator_dependency,
        )
    )

    return TestClient(app)


def test_operator_can_read_overview_and_health():
    client = make_client()

    overview = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/persistence?environment=dev"
    )
    health = client.get(
        "/api/operations-center/persistence/health?environment=staging"
    )

    assert overview.status_code == 200
    assert overview.json() == {
        "environment": "dev",
        "kind": "snapshot",
    }

    assert health.status_code == 200
    assert health.json() == {
        "environment": "staging",
        "kind": "health",
    }


def test_environment_contract_rejects_unknown_values():
    response = make_client().get(
        "/api/operations-center/persistence?environment=qa"
    )

    assert response.status_code == 422

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\operations_center\test_ws21_17_private_classroom_api.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.application.health.dependency_checks import (
    AsyncDependencyCheck,
    ConfigurationCheck,
)
from app.application.health.operational_event import (
    OperationalEvent,
    OperationalEventType,
    OperationalSeverity,
    OperationalSurface,
)
from app.application.health.operational_event_service import (
    OperationalEventService,
)
from app.application.health.operational_health_snapshot import (
    OperationalHealthSnapshotService,
)
from app.application.health.operational_dashboard_service import (
    OperationalDashboardService,
)
from app.application.health.production_readiness_service import (
    ProductionReadinessService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.private_classroom_operations_api import (
    PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI,
)


async def healthy_probe():
    return True


def make_api():
    readiness = ProductionReadinessService(
        checks=(
            ConfigurationCheck(
                name="telegram",
                configured=True,
            ),
            AsyncDependencyCheck(
                name="postgresql",
                probe=healthy_probe,
            ),
            AsyncDependencyCheck(
                name="llm_provider",
                probe=healthy_probe,
            ),
        )
    )

    health = OperationalHealthSnapshotService(
        readiness_service=readiness,
    )
    events = OperationalEventService()
    dashboard = OperationalDashboardService(
        health_snapshot_service=health,
        event_service=events,
    )

    return PrivateClassroomOperationsAPI(
        dashboard_service=dashboard,
    ), events


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_17_returns_fresh_private_classroom_snapshot():
    api, _events = make_api()

    first = await api.get_details()
    second = await api.get_details()

    assert first["surface"] == "private_classroom"
    assert first["status"] == "healthy"
    assert first["health_score"] == 100
    assert first["generated_at"] != second["generated_at"]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_17_exposes_private_incidents_and_events_only():
    api, events = make_api()

    events.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE,
            surface=OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM,
            correlation_id="private-001",
            severity=OperationalSeverity.HIGH,
            world_id="world-fr",
        )
    )
    events.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.TELEGRAM_FAILURE,
            surface=OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP,
            correlation_id="announcement-001",
        )
    )

    result = await api.get_details()

    assert result["incidents"]["summary"]["total"] == 1
    assert len(result["incidents"]["items"]) == 1
    assert result["incidents"]["items"][0]["surface"] == "private_classroom"

    assert len(result["events"]) == 1
    assert result["events"][0]["surface"] == "private_classroom"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_17_contains_dependency_and_metric_details():
    api, events = make_api()

    events.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE,
            surface=OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM,
            correlation_id="message-001",
        )
    )
    events.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.RATE_LIMIT,
            surface=OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM,
            correlation_id="rate-001",
        )
    )
    events.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.ABUSE,
            surface=OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM,
            correlation_id="abuse-001",
        )
    )

    result = await api.get_details()

    assert {
        item["name"] for item in result["dependencies"]
    } == {"telegram", "postgresql", "llm_provider"}

    assert result["metrics"] == {
        "message_failures": 1,
        "rate_limit_events": 1,
        "abuse_events": 1,
    }


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_17_does_not_expose_sensitive_event_metadata():
    api, events = make_api()

    events.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.MESSAGE_FAILURE,
            surface=OperationalSurface.PRIVATE_CLASSROOM,
            correlation_id="safe-001",
            metadata={
                "message": "PRIVATE LEARNER CONTENT",
                "prompt": "PRIVATE PROMPT",
                "response": "PRIVATE AI RESPONSE",
                "api_key": "SECRET",
                "safe_status": "timeout",
            },
        )
    )

    result = await api.get_details()
    payload = str(result).lower()

    assert "private learner content" not in payload
    assert "private prompt" not in payload
    assert "private ai response" not in payload
    assert "secret" not in payload
    assert "safe_status" in payload

        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.TELEGRAM_FAILURE,
            surface=OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP,
            correlation_id="safe-001",
            metadata={
                "message": "PRIVATE MESSAGE CONTENT",
                "prompt": "PRIVATE PROMPT",
                "response": "PRIVATE AI RESPONSE",
                "api_key": "SECRET",
                "safe_status": "timeout",
            },
        )
    )

    result = await api.get_details()

    payload = str(result).lower()

    assert "private message content" not in payload
    assert "private prompt" not in payload
    assert "private ai response" not in payload
    assert "secret" not in payload

    assert "safe_status" in payload


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_18_empty_state_is_safe():
    api, _events = make_api()

    result = await api.get_details()

    assert result["incidents"]["summary"]["total"] == 0
    assert result["incidents"]["items"] == []
    assert result["events"] == []


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_ws21_18_preserves_announcement_surface():
    api, events = make_api()

    events.record_event(
        OperationalEvent(
            event_type=OperationalEventType.TELEGRAM_FAILURE,
            surface=OperationalSurface.ANNOUNCEMENT_GROUP,
            correlation_id="announcement-002",
            severity=OperationalSeverity.MEDIUM,
        )
    )

    result = await api.get_details()

    assert result["surface"] == "announcement_group"

    assert all(
        item["surface"] == "announcement_group"
        for item in result["incidents"]["items"]
    )

    assert all(
        event["surface"] == "announcement_group"
        for event in result["events"]
    )

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.domain import (
    EmailWorkflowDefinition,
    EmailWorkflowLifecycle,
    EmailWorkflowType,
)


def make_workflow(
    *,
    workflow_type=EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED,
    lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
    schedule="0 8 * * *",
):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    return EmailWorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_id="test.workflow",
        name="Test Workflow",
        description="Test email workflow",
        workflow_type=workflow_type,
        lifecycle=lifecycle,
        owner="test",
        trigger="scheduled",
        schedule=schedule,
        template_id="test-template",
        version=1,
        created_at=now,
        updated_at=now,
    )


def test_scheduled_workflow_requires_schedule():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="must define a schedule"):
        make_workflow(schedule=None)


def test_non_scheduled_workflow_cannot_define_schedule():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="Only SCHEDULED workflows",
    ):
        make_workflow(
            workflow_type=EmailWorkflowType.EVENT_DRIVEN,
            schedule="0 8 * * *",
        )


def test_active_scheduled_workflow_can_pause():
    workflow = make_workflow()

    assert workflow.can_pause() is True


def test_event_driven_workflow_cannot_pause():
    workflow = make_workflow(
        workflow_type=EmailWorkflowType.EVENT_DRIVEN,
        schedule=None,
    )

    assert workflow.can_pause() is False


def test_paused_workflow_can_resume():
    workflow = make_workflow(
        lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED,
    )

    assert workflow.can_resume() is True


def test_disabled_workflow_can_enable():
    workflow = make_workflow(
        lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.DISABLED,
    )

    assert workflow.can_enable() is True


def test_archived_workflow_cannot_be_archived_again():
    workflow = make_workflow(
        lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ARCHIVED,
    )

    assert workflow.can_archive() is False


def test_lifecycle_change_is_immutable_and_increments_version():
    workflow = make_workflow()

    updated = workflow.with_lifecycle(
        EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED
    )

    assert workflow.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE
    assert updated.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED
    assert updated.version == 2
    assert updated.workflow_id == workflow.workflow_id

from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.domain import (
    EmailWorkflowDefinition,
    EmailWorkflowLifecycle,
    EmailWorkflowType,
)
from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.in_memory_registry import (
    InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry,
)
from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.lifecycle_service import (
    EmailWorkflowLifecycleService,
)


def make_workflow(
    workflow_id: str = "workflow.test",
    *,
    workflow_type: EmailWorkflowType = EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED,
    lifecycle: EmailWorkflowLifecycle = EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    return EmailWorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_id=workflow_id,
        name="Test Workflow",
        description="Lifecycle test",
        workflow_type=workflow_type,
        lifecycle=lifecycle,
        owner="test",
        trigger="scheduled",
        schedule="0 8 * * *"
        if workflow_type is EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED
        else None,
        template_id="test-template",
        version=1,
        created_at=now,
        updated_at=now,
    )


def service_with(workflow):
    registry = InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry()
    registry.create(workflow)
    return EmailWorkflowLifecycleService(registry), registry


def test_pause_active_scheduled_workflow():
    service, registry = service_with(make_workflow())

    result = service.pause("workflow.test")

    assert result.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED
    assert registry.get("workflow.test").lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED

        make_workflow(
            lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED,
        )
    )

    assert result.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE

    result = service.disable("workflow.test")

    assert result.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.DISABLED


def test_enable_disabled_workflow():
    service, _ = service_with(
        make_workflow(
            lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.DISABLED,
        )
    )

    result = service.enable("workflow.test")

    assert result.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE

    service, _ = service_with(make_workflow())

    result = service.archive("workflow.test")

    assert result.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ARCHIVED

        make_workflow(
            lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED,
        )
    )

    result = service.archive("workflow.test")

    assert result.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ARCHIVED

from __future__ import annotations

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_email import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailService,
)


class Sender:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.calls = []

    def send(
        self,
        request,
        *,
        subject,
        body,
        html_body,
    ):
        self.calls.append(
            (
                request,
                subject,
                body,
                html_body,
            )
        )
        return {
            "status": "accepted",
            "message_id": "msg-1",
        }


def make_request(**overrides):
    values = dict(
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        scope="operations-center",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=(
            "risk_level=HIGH",
            "historical_recurrence_threshold_reached",
        ),
        evidence_references=("EVID-1",),
        historical_references=("HIST-1",),
        policy_id="PI-ESCALATION",
        policy_version="1.0",
        rationale=(
            "Controlled escalation recommended from governed evidence."
        ),
        generated_at="2026-09-22T10:00:00+00:00",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
    )

    values.update(overrides)

    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailRequest(
        **values
    )


def test_build_email_contains_governed_traceability_and_control_language():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailService(
        sender=Sender(),
        frontend_base_url="http://localhost:5173",
    )

    req = make_request()

    body = service.build_body(req)
    html = service.build_html(req)

    for value in (
        "REC-1",
        "DEC-1",
        "REV-1",
        "EVID-1",
        "HIST-1",
        "PI-ESCALATION",
        "human review",
    ):
        assert value.lower() in (
            body + html
        ).lower()

    assert "does not constitute" in body
    assert "/operations-center/compliance/reviews" in html
    assert "Notification key" not in body
    assert "Notification key" not in html
    assert "action required" in body.lower()
    assert "sla or response target" in body.lower()


def test_send_delegates_to_injected_sender():
    sender = Sender()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationEmailService(
        sender=sender,
    )

    service.send(make_request())

    assert len(sender.calls) == 1
    assert sender.calls[0][0].recommendation_id == "REC-1"
    assert "HIGH" in sender.calls[0][1]


def test_non_review_state_is_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="pending human decision",
    ):
        make_request(
            decision_status="ACCEPTED"
        )


def test_failed_quality_gate_is_rejected():
    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="passed quality gate",
    ):
        make_request(
            quality_gate_status="FAILED"
        )

from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import create_engine
from sqlalchemy.orm import sessionmaker

from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.domain import (
    EmailWorkflowDefinition,
    EmailWorkflowLifecycle,
    EmailWorkflowType,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.database import Base
from app.infrastructure.persistence.email_workflow_models import (
    EmailWorkflowRecord,
)
from app.infrastructure.persistence.postgresql_email_workflow_registry import (
    PostgreSQLEmailWorkflowRegistry,
)


def make_workflow(
    workflow_id: str = "workflow.test",
    lifecycle: EmailWorkflowLifecycle = EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
):
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)

    return EmailWorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_id=workflow_id,
        name="Test Workflow",
        description="PostgreSQL registry test",
        workflow_type=EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED,
        lifecycle=lifecycle,
        owner="test",
        trigger="scheduled",
        schedule="0 8 * * *",
        template_id="test-template",
        version=1,
        created_at=now,
        updated_at=now,
    )


def make_registry():
    engine = create_engine("sqlite:///:memory:")

    Base.metadata.create_all(
        engine,
        tables=[EmailWorkflowRecord.__table__],
    )

    return PostgreSQLEmailWorkflowRegistry(
        sessionmaker(
            bind=engine,
            expire_on_commit=False,
        )
    )


def assert_same_instant(
    actual: datetime,
    expected: datetime,
) -> None:
    """Compare persisted timestamps by instant, independent of SQLite tz metadata."""
    actual_utc = (
        actual.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        if actual.tzinfo is None
        else actual.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    )

    expected_utc = (
        expected.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        if expected.tzinfo is None
        else expected.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    )

    assert actual_utc == expected_utc


def test_create_and_get():
    registry = make_registry()
    workflow = make_workflow()

    registry.create(workflow)

    result = registry.get(workflow.workflow_id)

    assert result is not None
    assert result.workflow_id == workflow.workflow_id
    assert result.name == workflow.name
    assert result.description == workflow.description
    assert result.workflow_type is workflow.workflow_type
    assert result.lifecycle is workflow.lifecycle
    assert result.owner == workflow.owner
    assert result.trigger == workflow.trigger
    assert result.schedule == workflow.schedule
    assert result.template_id == workflow.template_id
    assert result.version == workflow.version

    assert_same_instant(
        result.created_at,
        workflow.created_at,
    )
    assert_same_instant(
        result.updated_at,
        workflow.updated_at,
    )


def test_missing_workflow_returns_none():
    registry = make_registry()

    assert registry.get("missing.workflow") is None


def test_duplicate_workflow_is_rejected():
    registry = make_registry()
    workflow = make_workflow()

    registry.create(workflow)

    try:
        registry.create(workflow)
    except ValueError as exc:
        assert "Workflow already exists" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Expected duplicate workflow rejection")


def test_list_returns_persisted_workflows():
    registry = make_registry()

    registry.create(make_workflow("workflow.b"))
    registry.create(make_workflow("workflow.a"))

    result = registry.list()

    assert [workflow.workflow_id for workflow in result] == [
        "workflow.a",
        "workflow.b",
    ]


def test_update_persists_lifecycle_and_version():
    registry = make_registry()
    workflow = make_workflow()

    registry.create(workflow)

    updated = workflow.with_lifecycle(
        EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED,
    )

    registry.update(updated)

    result = registry.get(workflow.workflow_id)

    assert result is not None
    assert result.lifecycle is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.PAUSED
    assert result.version == 2


def test_update_missing_workflow_is_rejected():
    registry = make_registry()

    try:
        registry.update(make_workflow())
    except KeyError as exc:
        assert "Workflow not found" in str(exc)
    else:
        raise AssertionError("Expected missing workflow rejection")

def make_workflow(

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\unit\operations_center\email_workflows\test_registry_bootstrap.py =====
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.domain import (
    EmailWorkflowLifecycle,
)
from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.in_memory_registry import (
    InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry,
)
from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.registry_bootstrap import (
    EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap,
)


def test_bootstrap_creates_validated_built_in_workflows():
    registry = InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry()

    result = EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap(registry).ensure_registered(
        now=datetime(2026, 9, 18, 20, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )

    assert result.created == (
        "cybersecurity_daily_report",
        "cybersecurity_weekly_report",
        "cybersecurity_critical_alert",
        "platform_intelligence_escalation",
    )

    assert (
        registry.get("cybersecurity_daily_report").schedule
        == "daily_08:00"
    )
    assert (
        registry.get("cybersecurity_weekly_report").schedule
        == "weekly_monday_08:00"
    )
    assert (
        registry.get("cybersecurity_critical_alert").schedule
        is None
    )

    platform_intelligence = registry.get(
        "platform_intelligence_escalation"
    )

    assert platform_intelligence is not None
    assert platform_intelligence.schedule is None
    assert (
        platform_intelligence.lifecycle
        is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE
    )
    assert platform_intelligence.owner == "platform_intelligence"
    assert platform_intelligence.trigger == "escalation_recommendation"
    assert (
        platform_intelligence.template_id
        == "platform_intelligence_escalation"
    )

    assert (
        registry.get("cybersecurity_daily_report").lifecycle
        is EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE
    )


def test_bootstrap_is_idempotent_and_does_not_overwrite():
    registry = InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry()
    bootstrap = EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap(registry)

    first = bootstrap.ensure_registered(
        now=datetime(2026, 9, 18, 20, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )

    before = registry.get("cybersecurity_daily_report")

    second = bootstrap.ensure_registered(
        now=datetime(2026, 9, 19, 20, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    )

    after = registry.get("cybersecurity_daily_report")

    assert len(first.created) == 4
    assert second.created == ()
    assert len(second.already_present) == 4
    assert before == after


def test_bootstrap_rejects_naive_clock():
    registry = InMemoryEmailWorkflowRegistry()

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        EmailWorkflowRegistryBootstrap(registry).ensure_registered(
            now=datetime(2026, 9, 18, 20, 0)
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\unit\operations_center\test_email_workflow_status_service.py =====

from types import SimpleNamespace

from app.application.operations_center.email_workflow_status_service import (
    EmailWorkflowStatusService,
)
from app.application.operations_center.email_workflows.domain import (
    EmailWorkflowLifecycle,
    EmailWorkflowType,
)


class Provider:
    def status(self):
        return {
            "provider": "test",
            "configured": True,
            "available": True,
            "status": "ready",
        }


class Scheduler:
    running = True

    def get_jobs(self):
        return [
            SimpleNamespace(
                id="cybersecurity_daily_report",
                next_run_time=None,
            ),
            SimpleNamespace(
                id="academy_one_week_milestone",
                next_run_time=None,
            ),
        ]


class Adapter:
    scheduler = Scheduler()


class Runtime:
    scheduler_adapter = Adapter()


class Registry:
    """Deterministic test registry implementing the application contract."""

    def __init__(self):
        self._workflows = [
            SimpleNamespace(
                workflow_id="cybersecurity_daily_report",
                name="Cybersecurity Daily Report",
                description="Test workflow.",
                workflow_type=EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED,
                lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
                owner="test",
                trigger="scheduled",
                schedule="daily",
                template_id="test-template",
                version=1,
            ),
            SimpleNamespace(
                workflow_id="academy_one_week_milestone",
                name="Academy One Week Milestone",
                description="Test workflow.",
                workflow_type=EmailWorkflowType.SCHEDULED,
                lifecycle=EmailWorkflowLifecycle.ACTIVE,
                owner="test",
                trigger="scheduled",
                schedule="daily",
                template_id="test-template",
                version=1,
            ),
        ]

    def list(self):
        return list(self._workflows)

    def get(self, workflow_id):
        return next(
            (
                workflow
                for workflow in self._workflows
                if workflow.workflow_id == workflow_id
            ),
            None,
        )


def build_service():
    return EmailWorkflowStatusService(
        Runtime(),
        Provider(),
        Registry(),
    )


def test_snapshot_reads_actual_scheduler_jobs():
    result = build_service().snapshot()

    assert result["scheduler"]["running"] is True
    assert result["count"] == 2
    assert {workflow["id"] for workflow in result["workflows"]} == {
        "cybersecurity_daily_report",
        "academy_one_week_milestone",
    }


def test_provider_status_is_non_sensitive():
    result = build_service().snapshot()

    assert result["provider"] == {
        "provider": "test",
        "configured": True,

from datetime import datetime, timezone
from uuid import uuid4

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery_audit import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus,
)


def common(status):
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
        audit_id=uuid4(),
        notification_key="platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1",
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=1,
        status=status,
        occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        provider_message_id="provider-1"
        if status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.SUCCEEDED
        else None,
        error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR"
        if status is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.FAILED
        else None,
    )


def test_audit_record_is_immutable():
    record = common(PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.STARTED)
    with pytest.raises((AttributeError, TypeError)):
        record.attempt = 2


def test_success_requires_provider_message_id():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="provider_message_id"):
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
            audit_id=uuid4(),
            notification_key="k",
            recommendation_id="r",
            risk_decision_id="d",
            review_item_id="v",
            correlation_id="c",
            attempt=1,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.SUCCEEDED,
            occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )


def test_failure_requires_error_code():
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="error_code"):
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditRecord(
            audit_id=uuid4(),
            notification_key="k",
            recommendation_id="r",
            risk_decision_id="d",
            review_item_id="v",
            correlation_id="c",
            attempt=1,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryAuditStatus.FAILED,
            occurred_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),
        )

===== FILE: C:\AI Folder\EasyChatBot-Hackathon\backend\tests\unit\operations_center\test_platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, replace

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService,
)


@dataclass
class DeliveryRecord:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None


class FakeRepository:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.records: dict[str, DeliveryRecord] = {}
        self.created = 0
        self.retries = 0
        self.succeeded = 0
        self.failed = 0

    async def get_by_notification_key(
        self,
        notification_key: str,
    ) -> DeliveryRecord | None:
        return self.records.get(notification_key)

    async def create_started(self, **kwargs) -> DeliveryRecord:
        self.created += 1

        record = DeliveryRecord(
            **kwargs,
            attempt=1,
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
        )

        self.records[kwargs["notification_key"]] = record
        return record

    async def mark_succeeded(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        provider_message_id: str | None,
    ) -> DeliveryRecord:
        self.succeeded += 1

        record = self.records[notification_key]
        record.status = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED
        record.provider_message_id = provider_message_id

        return record

    async def mark_failed(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
        error_code: str,
    ) -> DeliveryRecord:
        self.failed += 1

        record = self.records[notification_key]
        record.status = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        record.error_code = error_code

        return record

    async def start_retry(
        self,
        *,
        notification_key: str,
    ) -> DeliveryRecord:
        self.retries += 1

        record = self.records[notification_key]
        record.attempt += 1
        record.status = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED

        return record


@dataclass
class WorkflowResult:
    delivery: object


class FakeWorkflow:
    def __init__(
        self,
        result=None,
        error: Exception | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.calls: list[object] = []
        self.result = result or {
            "provider_message_id": "resend-123",
        }
        self.error = error

    def send(self, request: object) -> WorkflowResult:
        self.calls.append(request)

        if self.error:
            raise self.error

        return WorkflowResult(delivery=self.result)


def make_request(
    **overrides,
) -> PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest:
    values = {
        "recommendation_id": "rec-1",
        "risk_decision_id": "risk-1",
        "review_item_id": "review-1",
        "risk_level": "HIGH",
        "risk_score": 0.82,
        "quality_gate_status": "PASSED",
        "review_status": "IN_REVIEW",
        "decision_status": "PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        "notification_key": (
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:rec-1"
        ),
        "correlation_id": "corr-1",
        "route": "RISK_OWNER",
        "reasons": ("High risk level.",),
    }

    values.update(overrides)

    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(**values)


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_dispatch_sends_and_persists_success():
    repo = FakeRepository()
    workflow = FakeWorkflow()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=workflow,
    )

    result = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    assert result.status.value == "SENT"
    assert result.provider_message_id == "resend-123"
    assert repo.created == 1
    assert repo.succeeded == 1
    assert len(workflow.calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_successful_delivery_is_terminal_idempotent_replay():
    repo = FakeRepository()
    workflow = FakeWorkflow()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=workflow,
    )

    first = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    second = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    assert first.status.value == "SENT"
    assert second.status.value == "IDEMPOTENT_REPLAY"
    assert second.provider_message_id == "resend-123"
    assert len(workflow.calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_failed_delivery_is_retryable():
    repo = FakeRepository()

    failing = FakeWorkflow(
        error=RuntimeError("provider unavailable"),
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=failing,
    )

    first = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    assert first.status.value == "FAILED"
    assert repo.failed == 1

    succeeding = FakeWorkflow()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=succeeding,
    )

    second = await service.dispatch(
        request=make_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )

    assert second.status.value == "SENT"
    assert repo.retries == 1
    assert len(succeeding.calls) == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_invalid_state_is_blocked_before_repository_or_email():
    repo = FakeRepository()
    workflow = FakeWorkflow()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService(
        delivery_repository=repo,
        email_workflow=workflow,
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="quality gate"):
        await service.dispatch(
            request=make_request(
                quality_gate_status="FAILED",
            ),
            email_request=object(),
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="review"):
        await service.dispatch(
            request=make_request(
                review_status="COMPLETED",
            ),
            email_request=object(),
        )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="decision"):
        await service.dispatch(
            request=make_request(
                decision_status="ACCEPTED",
            ),
            email_request=object(),
        )

    assert repo.created == 0
    assert workflow.calls == []


def test_notification_key_is_recommendation_scoped():
    assert (
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchService.build_notification_key(
            "rec-7",
        )
        == "platform_intelligence_escalation:rec-7"
    )


def test_notification_key_must_match_recommendation():
    valid_request = make_request()

    with pytest.raises(
        ValueError,
        match="notification_key",
    ):
        replace(
            valid_request,
            notification_key=(
                "platform_intelligence_escalation:other"
            ),
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus,
)


REFERENCE = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    0,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = REFERENCE
    updated_at: datetime | None = REFERENCE


def policy(**overrides):
    values = {
        "policy_id": "PI-RETRY",
        "policy_version": "1.0",
        "status": (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
            .APPROVED
        ),
        "max_attempts": 4,
        "base_backoff_seconds": 60,
        "max_backoff_seconds": 300,
        "retryable_error_codes": frozenset(
            {
                "TIMEOUT",
                "CONNECTION_ERROR",
                "PROVIDER_ERROR",
            }
        ),
        "retry_interrupted_started": True,
    }

    values.update(overrides)

    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy(
        **values
    )


def record(
    *,
    status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
    attempt=1,
    error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
):
    return Record(
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=attempt,
        status=status,
        error_code=error_code,
        created_at=REFERENCE,
        updated_at=REFERENCE,
    )


def test_retryable_failed_delivery_is_eligible_with_deterministic_backoff():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=1),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )

    assert result.policy_id == "PI-RETRY"
    assert result.policy_version == "1.0"


def test_failed_delivery_is_not_due_before_backoff():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=1),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=59),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert result.backoff_seconds == 60

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )


def test_backoff_doubles_and_is_capped():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=1) == 60
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=2) == 120
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=3) == 240
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=4) == 300
    assert service.backoff_seconds(attempt=10) == 300


def test_non_retryable_error_is_not_eligible():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            error_code="INVALID_RECIPIENT"
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "not retryable" in result.reason


def test_missing_error_code_is_not_eligible():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(error_code=None),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "no error code" in result.reason


def test_maximum_attempts_is_enforced():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(attempt=4),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "Maximum delivery attempts" in result.reason


def test_draft_policy_cannot_authorize_retry():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy(
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
                .DRAFT
            )
        )
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert "not APPROVED" in result.reason


def test_interrupted_started_delivery_is_policy_controlled():
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=policy()
    )

    result = service.evaluate(
        record=record(
            status=(

                .STARTED
            ),
            attempt=1,
            error_code=None,
        ),

    )

    assert result.next_eligible_at == (
        REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60)
    )


def test_started_recovery_can_be_disabled_by_policy():

        policy=policy(
            retry_interrupted_started=False
        )
    )

    result = service.evaluate(

            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
                .STARTED
            ),
            error_code=None,
        ),
        reference_at=REFERENCE + timedelta(seconds=60),
    )

    assert (
        result.decision
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryDecision.NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_candidate_discovery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService,
)

T1 = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 10, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
T2 = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 10, 1, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = T1
    updated_at: datetime | None = T1


class Repository:
    def __init__(self, records):
        self.records = records
        self.limit = None

    async def list_retry_candidates(self, *, limit):
        self.limit = limit
        return self.records


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_returns_only_failed_and_started_candidates():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-succeeded",
                "rec-s",
                "dec-s",
                "rev-s",
                "corr-s",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.SUCCEEDED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-failed",
                "rec-f",
                "dec-f",
                "rev-f",
                "corr-f",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
                updated_at=T2,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-started",
                "rec-t",
                "dec-t",
                "rev-t",
                "corr-t",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=50)

    assert repository.limit == 50
    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.candidates] == [
        "key-started",
        "key-failed",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_is_deterministically_ordered():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-b",
                "rec-b",
                "dec-b",
                "rev-b",
                "corr-b",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
            Record(
                "key-a",
                "rec-a",
                "dec-a",
                "rev-a",
                "corr-a",
                1,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                updated_at=T1,
            ),
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=10)

    assert [item.notification_key for item in result.candidates] == [
        "key-a",
        "key-b",
    ]


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_preserves_retry_metadata():
    repository = Repository(
        [
            Record(
                "key-1",
                "rec-1",
                "dec-1",
                "rev-1",
                "corr-1",
                2,
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
                error_code="TIMEOUT",
                updated_at=T2,
            )
        ]
    )

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    result = await service.discover(limit=10)
    candidate = result.candidates[0]

    assert candidate.attempt == 2
    assert candidate.error_code == "TIMEOUT"
    assert candidate.updated_at == T2
    assert candidate.status == "FAILED"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_discovery_rejects_invalid_limit():
    repository = Repository([])

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryCandidateDiscoveryService(
        repository=repository
    )

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="at least 1"):

from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claims import (
    RetryCandidateClaimStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_claim_service import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService,
)


class FakeClaimRepository:
    def __init__(self, result: bool) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls = []

    async def claim_retry_candidate(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls.append(kwargs)
        return self.result


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_returns_bounded_lease() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(True)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(
        repository,
        lease_seconds=60,
    )
    reference = datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)

    claim = await service.claim(
        notification_key="pi-key",
        claimed_at=reference,
    )

    assert claim is not None
    assert claim.notification_key == "pi-key"
    assert claim.claimed_at == reference
    assert claim.lease_expires_at == reference + timedelta(seconds=60)
    assert claim.status is RetryCandidateClaimStatus.CLAIMED
    assert repository.calls[0]["notification_key"] == "pi-key"


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_returns_none_when_candidate_is_already_claimed() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(False)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(repository)

    claim = await service.claim(
        notification_key="pi-key",
        claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc),
    )

    assert claim is None


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_claim_rejects_naive_reference_time() -> None:
    repository = FakeClaimRepository(True)
    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryClaimService(repository)

    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="timezone-aware"):
        await service.claim(
            notification_key="pi-key",
            claimed_at=datetime(2026, 9, 22, 12, 0),
        )

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_delivery import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_dispatch import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus,
)
from app.application.operations_center.platform_intelligence_escalation_retry_execution import (
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService,
    PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus,
)


DELIVERY_TIME = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    0,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)

REFERENCE = datetime(
    2026,
    9,
    22,
    10,
    1,
    tzinfo=timezone.utc,
)


@dataclass
class Record:
    notification_key: str
    recommendation_id: str
    risk_decision_id: str
    review_item_id: str
    correlation_id: str
    attempt: int
    status: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus
    provider_message_id: str | None = None
    error_code: str | None = None
    created_at: datetime | None = DELIVERY_TIME
    updated_at: datetime | None = DELIVERY_TIME


class Repository:
    def __init__(self, record):
        self.record = record
        self.retries = 0

    async def get_by_notification_key(self, key):
        if self.record is None:
            return None

        if self.record.notification_key == key:
            return self.record

        return None

    async def start_retry(self, *, notification_key):
        self.retries += 1

        self.record.attempt += 1
        self.record.status = (
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED
        )
        self.record.error_code = None
        self.record.updated_at = REFERENCE

        return self.record


class Dispatch:
    def __init__(self):
        self.calls = 0

    async def dispatch(self, *, request, email_request):
        self.calls += 1

        return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchStatus.SENT,
            notification_key=request.notification_key,
            provider_message_id="provider-1",
            attempt=2,
        )


def policy():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyService(
        policy=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicy(
            policy_id="PI-RETRY",
            policy_version="1.0",
            status=(
                PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryPolicyStatus
                .APPROVED
            ),
            max_attempts=4,
            base_backoff_seconds=60,
            max_backoff_seconds=300,
            retryable_error_codes=frozenset(
                {"PROVIDER_ERROR"}
            ),
            retry_interrupted_started=True,
        )
    )


def request():
    return PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDispatchRequest(
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        risk_level="HIGH",
        risk_score=0.82,
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        route="RISK_OWNER",
        reasons=("High risk.",),
    )


def record(
    status,
    attempt=1,
    error_code="PROVIDER_ERROR",
):
    return Record(
        notification_key=(
            "platform_intelligence_escalation:REC-1"
        ),
        recommendation_id="REC-1",
        risk_decision_id="DEC-1",
        review_item_id="REV-1",
        correlation_id="CORR-1",
        attempt=attempt,
        status=status,
        error_code=error_code,
        created_at=DELIVERY_TIME,
        updated_at=DELIVERY_TIME,
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_failed_delivery_retries_only_when_due():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    early = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=datetime(
            2026,
            9,
            22,
            10,
            0,
            30,
            tzinfo=timezone.utc,
        ),
    )

    assert (
        early.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_DUE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0

    due = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        due.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .RETRIED
    )

    assert repository.retries == 1
    assert dispatch.calls == 1


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_non_retryable_failure_does_not_dispatch():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
            error_code="INVALID_RECIPIENT",
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(
        delivery_repository=repository,
        retry_policy=policy(),
        dispatch_service=dispatch,
    )

    result = await service.retry(
        request=request(),
        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_started_recovery_does_not_increment_attempt():
    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.STARTED,
            error_code=None,
        )
    )

    dispatch = Dispatch()

    service = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionService(

    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .RETRIED
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert repository.record.attempt == 1
    assert dispatch.calls == 1

    repository = Repository(
        record(
            PlatformIntelligenceEscalationDeliveryStatus.FAILED,
            attempt=4,
        )
    )

        email_request=object(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert (
        result.status
        is PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus
        .NOT_ELIGIBLE
    )

    assert repository.retries == 0
    assert dispatch.calls == 0


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_retry_requires_existing_durable_record():
    repository = Repository(None)
    dispatch = Dispatch()

        delivery_repository=repository,

        ValueError,

class FakePolicy:
    def __init__(self, eligible: bool) -> None:
        self.eligible = eligible
        self.calls = 0

    async def evaluate(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.eligible


class FakeClaimService:
    def __init__(self, claim) -> None:
        self.claim_result = claim
        self.calls = 0

    async def claim(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.claim_result


class FakeStateReader:
    def __init__(self, state) -> None:
        self.state = state
        self.calls = 0

    async def get_retry_state(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        return self.state


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class FakeClaim:
    token: str = "claim-token"


class FakeRetryExecution:
    def __init__(
        self,
        result: PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult,
    ) -> None:
        self.result = result
        self.calls = 0
        self.kwargs = None

    async def retry(self, **kwargs):
        self.calls += 1
        self.kwargs = kwargs
        return self.result


def valid_state() -> PlatformIntelligenceRetryState:
    return PlatformIntelligenceRetryState(
        notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
        recommendation_id="recommendation-1",
        recommendation_status="RECOMMENDED",
        review_status="IN_REVIEW",
        decision_status="PENDING_HUMAN_REVIEW",
        quality_gate_status="PASSED",
        dispatch_request=dispatch_request(),
        email_request=object(),
    )


@pytest.mark.asyncio
async def test_not_policy_eligible_does_not_claim_or_execute() -> None:
    policy = FakePolicy(False)
    claims = FakeClaimService(FakeClaim())
    state = FakeStateReader(valid_state())

    execution = FakeRetryExecution(
        PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionResult(
            status=PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryExecutionStatus.RETRIED,
            notification_key=NOTIFICATION_KEY,
            attempt=1,
            reason="SUCCEEDED",
        )
    )

    orchestrator = PlatformIntelligenceEscalationRetryOrchestrator(
        state_reader=state,
        policy_evaluator=policy,
        claim_service=claims,
        retry_execution=execution,
    )

    result = await orchestrator.run_candidate(
        candidate=candidate(),
        reference_at=REFERENCE,
    )

    assert result.status is PlatformIntelligenceRetryRunStatus.SKIPPED
    assert result.reason == "RETRY_POLICY_NOT_ELIGIBLE"
    assert claims.calls == 0
    assert state.calls == 0
    assert execution.calls == 0
